1

Temporal Changes in Nonlinear Heart Rate Variability Following Pulmonary Vein Isolation for Paroxysmal Atrial Fibrillation.

Acta Cardiol Sin·2026-09-24·PMID 42780719
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Ting-Wei Ernie Liao、Li-Wei Lo、Yenn-Jiang Lin 等 11 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Heart rate variability (HRV) provides insights beyond standard electrocardiography (ECG). Although prior studies have shown altered short-term linear HRV in patients with paroxysmal atrial fibrillation (PAF) after pulmonary vein isolation (PVI), long-term nonlinear changes are unclear. This study aimed to investigate longitudinal nonlinear HRV changes before and after PVI in PAF patients. METHODS: Forty-two drug-refractory PAF patients (55.50 ± 9.53 years old, 11 females) undergoing PVI were enrolled. Holter ECG was recorded before, at 1-3 months, and at 6-12 months post-PVI. Nonlinear HRV variables including multi-scale entropy (MSE), detrended fluctuation analysis (DFA), and Poincaré plot analysis, were analyzed. RESULTS: Significant reductions in standard deviation (SD)1, SD1/SD2, MSE 1-5, and MSE 6-20 were found 6-12 months post-PVI compared to baseline. SD2, MSE slope 5, and DFA slope 1 decreased at 1-3 months post-PVI but showed significant increases at 6-12 months post-PVI. DFA slope 2 increased significantly after PVI. CONCLUSIONS: Long-term fractal correlation properties (DFA slope 2) increased significantly following PVI. Heart rate complexity (MSE 1-5 and MSE 6-20) and vagal activity (SD1) decreased significantly after PVI, whereas short-term fractal correlation properties (DFA slope 1) and short-term heart rate complexity dynamics (MSE slope 5) decreased at 1-3 months post-PVI and then increased by 6-12 months post-PVI. These results highlight the significant and varied effects of PVI on cardiac neuromodulation and complexity, as demonstrated by HRV parameters.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

42 例药物难治性阵发性房颤患者 PVI 后，非线性心率变异性呈时相性改变：6-12 个月时心率复杂度（MSE）与迷走活性（SD1）显著下降，长程分形相关（DFA slope 2）显著升高。

研究动机

心率变异性（HRV）可提供常规心电图之外的自律神经信息。既往研究多关注 PAF 患者 PVI 后短期线性 HRV 的变化，而长期非线性 HRV（复杂度、分形特征）的演变规律尚不明确；本研究旨在刻画 PVI 前后非线性 HRV 的纵向变化轨迹，以反映消融对心脏神经调控的影响。

方法

单中心前瞻性纵向观察研究。纳入 42 例药物难治性阵发性房颤接受 PVI 的患者（年龄 55.50 ± 9.53 岁，女性 11 例）。于 PVI 术前、术后 1-3 个月、术后 6-12 个月分别行动态心电图（Holter）记录，分析非线性 HRV 指标，包括多尺度熵（MSE）、去趋势波动分析（DFA）及 Poincaré 散点图分析（SD1、SD2、SD1/SD2、MSE slope 5、DFA slope 1、DFA slope 2 等）。主要终点为各非线性 HRV 指标相对基线的纵向变化；具体统计方法与效应量、95%CI、P 值摘要未报告。

结果

与基线相比，术后 6-12 个月 SD1、SD1/SD2、MSE 1-5 及 MSE 6-20 均显著降低。SD2、MSE slope 5 与 DFA slope 1 在术后 1-3 个月下降，但在 6-12 个月显著回升。DFA slope 2 在 PVI 后显著升高。摘要未报告各指标的具体数值、效应量、95% 置信区间及 P 值，亦未报告消融术式细节与房颤复发情况。

结论与意义

PVI 对心脏自主神经调控与心率复杂性的影响具有时相性且方向不一：长程分形相关（DFA slope 2）持续升高，提示心率波动的长程相关性增强；心率复杂度（MSE 1-5、MSE 6-20）与迷走活性（SD1）在术后 6-12 个月显著下降；而短程分形相关（DFA slope 1）与短程复杂度动态（MSE slope 5）呈先降后升的恢复模式。上述结果提示非线性 HRV 或可作为评估 PVI 后神经重构的辅助指标。局限性包括样本量小（42 例）、缺乏对照或未消融组、随访节点区间较宽、摘要未报告复发率与统计细节，尚需更大样本验证其对临床结局的预测价值。

Epicardial Fragmentation Mapping in Brugada Syndrome: A Three-Patient Case Series with Literature Review.

Acta Cardiol Sin·2026-09-24·PMID 42780649
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Cheng-Han Chan、Chin-Yu Lin

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Brugada syndrome (BrS) carries a high risk of ventricular fibrillation (VF) despite protective therapy with implantable cardioverter-defibrillators (ICDs). Catheter ablation targeting epicardial substrate in the right ventricular outflow tract (RVOT) has emerged as a promising adjunctive strategy; however, optimal procedural endpoints remain unclear. OBJECTIVE: To present a case series of three BrS patients who underwent high-density (HD) epicardial substrate ablation assisted by electrogram fragmentation mapping, and to review contemporary evidence on Brugada substrate ablation. METHODS: Three symptomatic BrS patients underwent epicardial HD mapping using the EnSite X three-dimensional mapping system and Advisor HD Grid catheter. A dedicated fractionation mapping algorithm was used to identify regions of abnormal electrograms. All spontaneous fractionated or late potentials were ablated until the fragmentation map showed no residual abnormal points. RESULTS: In our three cases, HD mapping localized clusters of low-voltage, fractionated electrograms in the RVOT epicardium, which were successfully eliminated with epicardial radiofrequency ablation. Complete substrate removal resulted in normalization of the Brugada electrocardiographic pattern in all patients and rendered VF non-inducible. Over 6 months of follow-up, no VF recurrence was noted and no ICD therapies were delivered. No major procedural complications were observed. Post-ablation voltage maps remained patchy (reflecting residual scar tissue); however, the fragmentation maps became uniformly clean, supporting their utility as an objective endpoint. CONCLUSIONS: This case series demonstrates the feasibility of using fragmentation mapping as an intra-procedural guide and endpoint for Brugada ablation in the absence of drug challenge. Fragmentation mapping appeared to reproducibly confirm complete substrate elimination.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

3例症状性Brugada综合征患者接受高密度心外膜碎裂电位标测指导的右室流出道基质消融，术后Brugada心电图形态均恢复正常、室颤不可诱发，随访6个月无室颤复发及ICD治疗。

研究动机

Brugada综合征患者即使接受ICD保护性治疗，仍有较高的室颤风险，因此需要额外的治疗手段。针对右室流出道（RVOT）心外膜基质的导管消融已成为有前景的辅助策略，但最佳的手术终点尚不明确；既往证据多来自小样本研究，缺少客观、可重复的消融终点指标。本研究拟以碎裂电位标测作为术中指导与终点，并回顾相关证据。

方法

这是一项病例系列研究（含文献回顾），纳入3例有症状的Brugada综合征患者。研究采用EnSite X三维电解剖标测系统与Advisor HD Grid导管进行心外膜高密度标测，并用专门的碎裂电位标测算法识别异常电位区域，包括自发性碎裂电位与晚电位；对这些电位全部进行消融，直至碎裂标测图上无残余异常点。观察终点包括Brugada心电图形态是否正常化、室颤是否仍可诱发、6个月随访期内室颤复发与ICD治疗情况以及手术并发症；统计方法在摘要中未报告。

结果

3例患者均在RVOT心外膜标测到低电压、碎裂电位聚集区，经心外膜射频消融成功消除该区域；基质完全消除后，3例患者Brugada心电图形态均恢复正常，室颤均不可诱发。6个月随访期内无室颤复发，未给予ICD治疗，也未观察到重大手术并发症。消融后电压标测仍呈斑片状（提示残余瘢痕组织），但碎裂标测图均变得无异常，支持其作为客观终点。摘要未报告具体效应量、95%置信区间与P值。

结论与意义

本研究提示，在未行药物激发试验的情况下，碎裂电位标测可作为Brugada综合征心外膜消融的术中指导与终点，并可重复地确认基质完全消除。但该研究仅为3例病例系列，无对照组，随访仅6个月，结论的推广仍需更大样本和更长随访验证。

Leveraging AI-ECG Technology for Early Notification and Tracking of AF Development (LATENT): Study Rationale and Design of a Randomized Controlled Trial.

Acta Cardiol Sin·2026-09-24·PMID 42780633
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Chiao-Chin Lee、Wen-Yu Lin、Wei-Ting Liu 等 15 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is a progressive condition that is often undiagnosed until adverse events occur. Traditional risk scores provide limited value for predicting short-term latent AF. Artificial intelligence (AI)-enabled electrocardiogram (ECG) algorithms enable early risk stratification from sinus rhythm ECGs, however their clinical utility remains unvalidated in randomized controlled trials (RCTs). OBJECTIVE: To evaluate an AI-ECG-guided strategy for targeted ambulatory Holter monitoring to enhance the early detection of AF or pre-AF atrial findings (PAAFs, defined as premature atrial complexes [PACs] > 500 beats per 24 hours, consecutive short runs of PACs > 20 beats, or non-sustained AF or atrial flutter) compared with usual care. DESIGN: The LATENT trial is a prospective, open-label, two-arm RCT (NCT06847932) that will recruit 14,726 participants undergoing routine ECGs at a tertiary medical center in Taiwan. Participants will be randomized 1:1 to the intervention group (AI-ECG risk stratification) or control group (usual care). In the intervention group, high-risk individuals identified by the AI-ECG model will be invited to undergo up to 7 days of ambulatory Holter monitoring. In the control group, AI-ECG analysis will be retrospectively applied after study completion to identify those at high risk. The primary endpoint is newly detected AF or PAAFs within 90 days. SUMMARY: This trial will evaluate the clinical utility of an AI-ECG-guided risk stratification strategy combined with targeted ambulatory Holter monitoring for the early detection of AF or PAAFs in a real-world hospital setting. The findings will inform future strategies for integrating AI-ECG technology to support early AF detection and prevention.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

LATENT 随机对照试验将在台湾一家三级医疗中心纳入 14,726 例常规心电图受试者，评估 AI-ECG 风险分层联合针对性动态 Holter 监测能否在 90 天内提高新发 AF 或前房性心房表现（PAAF）的检出率。

研究动机

AF 为进展性疾病，常在发生不良事件后才被诊断；传统风险评分对短期潜伏性 AF 的预测价值有限。AI-ECG 算法可基于窦性心律心电图进行早期风险分层，但其临床效用尚缺乏随机对照试验验证，本研究旨在填补这一证据缺口。

方法

LATENT（NCT06847932）为前瞻性、开放标签、两臂随机对照试验，在台湾一家三级医疗中心招募 14,726 例接受常规心电图检查的受试者，按 1:1 随机分为干预组（AI-ECG 风险分层，高危者接受最多 7 天动态 Holter 监测）与对照组（常规诊疗，研究结束后回顾性应用 AI-ECG 识别高危者）。主要终点为 90 天内新检出的 AF 或 PAAF（PAC>500 次/24 小时、连续短串 PAC>20 次、非持续性 AF 或心房扑动）。摘要未报告具体统计方法与样本量计算假设。

结果

摘要未报告研究结果；本文仅介绍研究依据与设计，试验尚在进行中。

结论与意义

该试验将在真实世界医院环境中评估 AI-ECG 指导的风险分层联合针对性动态 Holter 监测用于 AF 与前房性心房表现早期检出的临床价值，结果可为 AI-ECG 技术整合进 AF 早期筛查与预防策略提供随机对照证据。局限性包括开放标签设计、单中心、终点依赖 Holter 监测时长（最多 7 天）且对照组 AI-ECG 分析为回顾性，可能影响检出敏感性及组间可比性。

Dual direct current external cardioversion in refractory atrial fibrillation: a systematic review and single-arm meta-analysis.

Am J Emerg Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42784943
房颤与消融 Journal Article Review

Mohammed Ayyad、Ahmed Ayyad、Dana SayyedAhmad 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

INTRODUCTION: Electrical direct-current cardioversion (DCCV) is a cornerstone rhythm-control strategy for atrial fibrillation (AF). Conventional DCCV is less effective in patients with obesity and elevated transthoracic impedance, but the role of dual simultaneous DCCV in refractory AF remains uncertain. METHODS AND RESULTS: We performed a systematic review and single-arm meta-analysis of dual simultaneous external DCCV, with quantitative synthesis restricted to adults with refractory AF after failed single-defibrillator cardioversion. PubMed, EMBASE, and the Cochrane Library were searched from inception through November 2025. The review followed PRISMA guidelines. Risk of bias was assessed using the Cochrane Risk of Bias 2.0 tool and Newcastle-Ottawa Scale. Pooled estimates were calculated using a random-effects generalized linear mixed model, with Freeman-Tukey double-arcsine transformation for sensitivity analyses. Eight studies were included in the qualitative synthesis and seven in the meta-analysis. Pooled cardioversion success was 79% (95% CI, 74%-83%; I2 = 0%). Ventricular arrhythmias occurred in 1% of patients (95% CI, 0%-4%) and transient bradyarrhythmia in 13% (95% CI, 4%-31%); no deaths were reported. Exploratory subgroup analyses showed no significant differences in success by pad configuration or number of prior failed single-defibrillator attempts. CONCLUSION: Dual simultaneous external DCCV was associated with high acute conversion success and few reported serious adverse events in refractory AF. However, predominantly observational evidence, single-arm synthesis, heterogeneous procedural approaches, and limited contemporary biphasic data constrain conclusions regarding comparative efficacy, safety, and optimal patient selection. Prospective comparative studies using contemporary biphasic defibrillators are needed to define its clinical role, including evaluation in emergency department and other acute-care settings, before routine implementation.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在单次除颤失败的难治性房颤患者中，双台同步体外直流电复律的汇总即刻成功率为79%，室性心律失常发生率仅1%，但证据以观察性单臂研究为主。

研究动机

直流电复律（DCCV）是房颤节律控制的基石，但肥胖及经胸阻抗升高的患者常规单次 DCCV 成功率偏低。双台同步体外 DCCV 能否改善难治性房颤的复律效果与安全性，既往证据不足、结论不确定，缺乏量化合成数据。

方法

系统评价与单臂 meta 分析，遵循 PRISMA 规范，检索 PubMed、EMBASE 与 Cochrane Library 自建库至2025年11月。纳入标准为成人难治性房颤（单除颤器复律失败后）接受双台同步体外 DCCV；共 8 项研究进入定性合成、7 项进入定量合成。偏倚风险采用 Cochrane RoB 2.0 工具与 Newcastle-Ottawa 量表评估；采用随机效应广义线性混合模型合并效应量，敏感性分析使用 Freeman-Tukey 双反正弦变换。主要终点为复律成功率，安全性终点包括室性心律失常、一过性缓慢性心律失常与死亡；并按电极片贴放方式及既往单除颤器复律失败次数进行探索性亚组分析。

结果

合并复律成功率为 79%（95%CI 74%-83%），研究间异质性 I2=0%。室性心律失常发生率为 1%（95%CI 0%-4%），一过性缓慢性心律失常为 13%（95%CI 4%-31%），未报告死亡事件。探索性亚组分析显示，不同电极片配置及既往单除颤器复律失败次数之间，成功率均无显著差异（摘要未报告具体组间效应量与 P 值）。

结论与意义

双台同步体外 DCCV 在难治性房颤中与较高的急性转复成功率和较少的严重不良事件相关。但证据以观察性研究为主、采用单臂合成、操作方案异质、现代双相波除颤器数据有限，限制了对比较疗效、安全性及最佳患者选择的推断。在常规开展前，需使用当代双相除颤器开展前瞻性对照研究，并纳入急诊科及其他急性救治场景评估其临床定位。

Preserved Ejection Fraction Despite Progressive Functional Decline in a Patient With Single-Ventricle Physiology Without Documented Successful Surgical Palliation.

Cureus·2026-09-24·PMID 42781643
房颤与消融 Case Reports Journal Article

Mohamad Karaali、Elias Hanna、Georges Ghanem 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

Seventh-decade survival with single-ventricle congenital heart disease (CHD) without documented successful surgical palliation is exceedingly rare. The prognostic value of ejection fraction (EF) in this population remains poorly characterized, and validated staging tools are absent from current guidelines. We report a 62-year-old man with complex single-ventricle CHD who survived without documented successful surgical palliation and had a common atrium, systemic right ventricle, and severe common atrioventricular valve regurgitation. Despite a preserved EF of 55-60%, he was NYHA Class IV with chronic hypoxemia. Four hospitalizations over 26 months were driven by atrial fibrillation with rapid ventricular response and community-acquired pneumonia; medication nonadherence was a documented precipitant in at least two admissions. He died during the fourth admission of refractory hypoxemic respiratory failure. In this patient, preserved EF did not reflect functional reserve in the context of single-ventricle physiology. AV valve regurgitation, obligate right-to-left shunting, and preload dependence may collectively decouple systolic function from effective cardiac output. Arrhythmia and infection were the dominant modifiable tipping points in this case. This case illustrates the potential inadequacy of EF-centered staging and the critical role of medication adherence, arrhythmia management, and infection prevention in deferring irreversible functional decline.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

62岁复杂单心室先心病患者EF保留至55-60%，却已达NYHA IV级并在26个月内4次住院后死亡，提示EF不能反映单心室循环的功能储备。

研究动机

研究背景与动机：单心室先天性心脏病（CHD）患者未获得成功外科姑息手术却存活至第七个十年极为罕见；EF在该人群中的预后价值尚不明确，现行指南缺乏经验证的分期工具。本报告旨在探讨以EF为中心的分期能否反映单心室循环患者的真实功能储备。

方法

个案报告，单例（n=1）。对象为62岁男性复杂单心室CHD患者，未记录成功的外科姑息手术，解剖特点为共同心房、右心室型系统心室及共同房室瓣重度反流。以描述性方式随访26个月，观察NYHA心功能分级、EF、低氧血症、住院次数与诱因及最终转归；无对照组、无预设终点定义与统计检验（摘要未报告）。

结果

EF保留于55-60%，但患者为NYHA IV级并伴慢性低氧血症。26个月内共住院4次，主要驱动因素为心房颤动伴快速心室率与社区获得性肺炎，至少2次住院存在明确的药物不依从。患者于第4次住院期间死于难治性低氧性呼吸衰竭。效应量、95%CI与P值摘要未报告。

结论与意义

在单心室循环中，保留的EF并不代表功能储备：房室瓣重度反流、强制右向左分流与前负荷依赖可共同使收缩功能与有效心输出量相互脱耦联。心律失常与感染是本例中占主导地位的可干预转折点。提示以EF为中心的分期体系在此类人群可能不足，临床应重视用药依从性、心律失常管理与感染预防，以延缓不可逆的功能衰退。局限性为单例经验、无对照、结论不可外推，且缺乏经验证的分期工具。

Successful pulsed field ablation for refractory premature ventricular contractions using a novel dual-energy focal catheter: a case report.

Eur Heart J Case Rep·2026-09-24·PMID 42781665
房颤与消融 Case Reports Journal Article

Kohei Ukita、Roman Mamaev、Sorin Stefan Popescu 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Radiofrequency ablation (RFA) is an established therapy for symptomatic premature ventricular contractions (PVCs), but arrhythmias from deep intramural substrates may remain refractory. Pulsed field ablation (PFA) is a nonthermal modality with myocardial selectivity, and dual-energy focal catheters allow delivery of both RFA and PFA using a single device. We report successful PFA for RFA-refractory PVCs using this technology. CASE SUMMARY: An 81-year-old man with ischaemic cardiomyopathy and symptomatic monomorphic PVCs was referred after failed prior RFA of both right ventricular outflow tract (RVOT) and left ventricular outflow tract (LVOT). During repeat ablation, activation mapping identified earliest activation at the septal RVOT and anterior LVOT. Using a dual-energy focal catheter, sequential RFA and PFA were delivered at both sites. In the RVOT, PFA caused only transient suppression with recurrence of PVCs. In the LVOT, the first PFA application failed to eliminate PVCs, whereas additional applications resulted in immediate and sustained suppression. No complications occurred, and the patient remained free of PVCs during follow-up. DISCUSSION: This case demonstrates the feasibility of dual-energy focal catheter-based PFA for ventricular arrhythmias refractory to RFA. The elimination of PVCs after repeated PFA suggests that multiple PFA applications may enhance lesion formation in deep intramural substrates. However, this observation should be interpreted cautiously because histological confirmation was unavailable and alternative explanations, including cumulative energy exposure and autonomic variability during conscious sedation, cannot be excluded. Further studies are required to define optimal energy strategy, lesion characteristics, and long-term outcomes of dual-energy ablation for ventricular arrhythmias.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例81岁缺血性心肌病患者在RVOT与LVOT射频消融失败后，改用双能量聚焦导管行脉冲场消融，LVOT多次PFA后PVC即刻且持续消失，随访无复发。

研究动机

射频消融是症状性室性早搏（PVC）的成熟治疗手段，但深部心肌内基质起源的室性心律失常常对RFA抵抗，复发率高。脉冲场消融（PFA）为非热消融方式，具有心肌选择性，而双能量聚焦导管可在同一器械上切换RFA与PFA，其在RFA难治性PVC中的可行性尚缺乏报道，本文以病例形式补充这一证据缺口。

方法

设计为个案报道（case report），非随机、无对照组。研究对象为1例81岁男性，缺血性心肌病，症状性单形性PVC，既往已行右心室流出道（RVOT）与左心室流出道（LVOT）射频消融均失败。再次消融术中行激动标测，最早激动点位于RVOT间隔侧与LVOT前部；使用双能量聚焦导管在两处序贯行RFA与PFA。观察终点为PVC是否被即刻及持续抑制、围术期并发症及随访期复发情况；摘要未报告具体标测参数、消融能量与放电次数、随访时长等数值。

结果

RVOT部位PFA仅产生短暂抑制，PVC随后复发；LVOT部位首次PFA未能消除PVC，追加放电后PVC即刻且持续消失。术中及术后无并发症发生，随访期间患者无PVC复发。摘要未报告随访时间、Holter负荷变化及能量参数等定量数据。

结论与意义

本病例提示双能量聚焦导管行PFA对RFA难治性室性心律失常具有可行性，且提示对深部心肌内基质可能需要多次PFA放电以增强损伤形成。但证据强度有限：为单例观察，缺乏组织学验证，且不能排除累积能量暴露与清醒镇静下自主神经变异等混杂因素。双能量消融的最佳能量策略、损伤特征与长期结局尚需进一步研究，目前不宜据此改变临床实践。

Beyond the classical triangle: morphological variations of the Triangle of Koch in human hearts and their clinical implications.

Folia Morphol (Warsz)·2026-09-24·PMID 42781957
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Apurba Patra、Rajiv Kumar、Vandana Tiwari 等 10 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: The Triangle of Koch (TOK) is an important anatomical region located in the right atrium of the heart. It contains the atrioventricular (AV) node and forms a crucial landmark during electrophysiological and cardiac surgical procedures. Variations in its boundaries and morphology may increase the risk of inadvertent injury to the cardiac conduction system. The present study aimed to evaluate the morphology (shape, boundaries, and anatomical variations) of the TOK in thirty donated human hearts and its clinical implications. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Thirty formalin-fixed adult human hearts were studied in the Department of Anatomy. The right atrium was opened carefully to expose the TOK. The boundaries formed by the tendon of Todaro (TT), the septal leaflet of the tricuspid valve, and the ostium of the coronary sinus (CS) were identified. Morphological features, location of the apex, and anatomical variations were documented and photographed. RESULTS: The TOK was identified in all specimens. Considerable variations were observed in shape, prominence of the TT, and position of the CS ostium. In most cases, it was broad and triangular, while in some hearts, the triangle appeared narrow and elongated. The apex corresponding to the AV nodal region showed positional variation relative to the membranous septum. Variability in the septal leaflet attachment and CS morphology was also noted. CONCLUSIONS: The TOK demonstrates significant anatomical variation in its shape and boundaries. Knowledge of these variations is essential for cardiologists, electrophysiologists, and cardiac surgeons during catheter ablation, pacemaker implantation, and valve surgeries to prevent injury to the AV node and associated conduction pathways.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在30例成人尸体心脏中，Koch三角形态和边界存在显著变异，可呈宽三角形或窄长形，尖端相对于膜部室间隔位置多变，提示消融和起搏操作需个体化解剖评估。

研究动机

Koch三角是右心房内包含房室结的重要解剖标志，其边界和形态变异可能增加房室传导系统误伤风险；本研究旨在系统评估其形态变异及临床意义。

方法

解剖学研究；30例经福尔马林固定的成人尸体心脏，切开右心房暴露Koch三角，识别由Todaro腱、三尖瓣隔瓣和冠状静脉窦口构成的边界，记录形态、尖端位置及解剖变异并拍照；摘要未报告统计检验方法。

结果

30例均识别出Koch三角；形态、Todaro腱突出程度及冠状静脉窦口位置变异明显。多数呈宽三角形，部分呈窄长形；对应房室结区的尖端相对于膜部室间隔位置存在变异，三尖瓣隔瓣附着和冠状静脉窦形态亦有差异。摘要未报告效应量、95%CI或P值。

结论与意义

Koch三角形态和边界存在显著解剖变异；电生理医师在导管消融、起搏器植入及瓣膜手术中应重视个体化解剖，以避免损伤房室结及传导通路。本研究为尸体解剖描述性研究，样本量30例，未提供临床结局数据。

9

Atrial fibrillation and coronary artery disease: interactions, outcomes and evolving management strategies.

Heart·2026-09-24·PMID 42785754
房颤与消融 Journal Article Review

Ashley Verburg、Dominik Linz、Tasnim Mohaissen 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

Atrial fibrillation (AF) and coronary artery disease (CAD) are prevalent cardiovascular disorders that increasingly coexist as populations age and cardiometabolic comorbidities rise. Their intersection creates a high-risk phenotype, with substantially higher rates of stroke, acute coronary syndromes, heart failure and mortality than either condition alone. This excess risk reflects shared comorbidities and complex bidirectional interactions whereby AF exacerbates myocardial ischaemia and endothelial dysfunction and CAD promotes atrial remodelling and arrhythmogenesis.Despite advances in antithrombotic and rhythm management strategies, care for patients with coexisting AF and CAD remains challenging. Diagnostic evaluation is often obscured by overlapping symptoms and confounding biomarker and imaging findings. Therapeutic decisions require careful integration of ischaemic and arrhythmic risk, particularly when determining antithrombotic intensity, selecting rate or rhythm control and managing ischaemia in AF-related haemodynamics. The evidence base has evolved rapidly, with short- and long-term antithrombotic studies and rhythm-control trials reshaping practice, prompting re-evaluation of management strategies.This Review synthesises current knowledge on epidemiology, pathophysiology and diagnostic challenges of AF-CAD coexistence and provides an updated integrated framework for therapeutic decision-making. We discuss contemporary antithrombotic strategies, rate and rhythm control, ischaemia management and clinical outcomes as well as highlight emerging evidence and practical considerations.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

综述指出，AF 与 CAD 共存构成高危表型，卒中、急性冠脉综合征、心衰和死亡风险显著高于任一单病；需整合缺血与出血、心律失常风险个体化制定抗栓及节律控制策略。

研究动机

随着人口老龄化和心血管代谢共病增多，AF 与 CAD 共存日益常见，但既往证据多来自单一疾病人群。二者存在双向交互：AF 加重心肌缺血与内皮功能障碍，CAD 则促进心房重构与致心律失常基质，使临床结局恶化。诊断上症状重叠、生物标志物与影像学结果互相混杂；治疗上抗栓强度、心率与节律控制的选择均缺乏统一路径，因此需要更新整合的决策框架。

方法

本文为叙述性综述（Review），非原始研究；摘要未报告样本量、纳入排除标准、终点定义及统计方法。综述内容涵盖 AF-CAD 共存的流行病学、病理生理机制与诊断难点，并整合近期短期与长期抗栓研究及节律控制试验的证据，围绕抗栓策略、心率与节律控制、缺血管理及临床结局，提出整合的治疗决策框架，并讨论新兴证据与实践考量。

结果

摘要未报告具体效应量、95%CI 或 P 值。综述归纳的要点为：共存患者卒中、急性冠脉综合征、心衰与死亡风险显著高于 AF 或 CAD 单病人群；诊断因症状重叠及生物标志物、影像学混杂而常被遮蔽；治疗决策须同时评估缺血风险与心律失常风险，重点包括抗栓治疗强度、心率控制与节律控制的选择，以及 AF 相关血流动力学状态下心肌缺血的处理；近期抗栓与节律控制试验结果正在重塑临床实践，需重新评估既有管理策略。

结论与意义

AF 与 CAD 共存应被视为独立的高危临床表型，管理上需以缺血风险与心律失常风险整合评估为基础，个体化确定抗栓强度并合理选择心率或节律控制。受叙述性综述设计限制，本文未提供定量合并的效应估计，所提出的整合框架仍需以不断更新的随机对照试验证据验证，并关注出血与缺血平衡在真实人群中的可操作性。

Clinical implications of adjunctive posterior wall isolation according to ablation modality: A subanalysis of the CORNERSTONE AF trial.

Heart Rhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42785611
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Shinsuke Miyazaki、Junichi Nitta、Osamu Inaba 等 21 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Left atrial posterior wall isolation (LAPWI) is a common adjunctive ablation strategy for persistent or long-standing persistent atrial fibrillation. OBJECTIVE: To investigate the impact of adjunctive LAPWI according to the pulmonary vein isolation (PVI) ablation modality. METHODS: This CORNERSTONE-AF randomized trial subanalysis compared 18-month outcomes between PVI alone (n = 259) and PVI + LAPWI (n = 254). The primary endpoint was freedom from atrial arrhythmia. Secondary endpoints included freedom from repeat ablation. Randomization was stratified by PVI ablation modality. RESULTS: PVI was performed using radiofrequency and cryoballoon ablation in 384 and 129 patients, respectively. In the LAPWI group, the LAPWI area was significantly larger in the cryoballoon subgroup than in the radiofrequency subgroup. Additional LAPWI significantly prolonged procedure time without increasing fluoroscopy time in both subgroups. In the cryoballoon subgroup, 18-month freedom from atrial arrhythmia was numerically higher with LAPWI than with PVI alone (82.0% vs 69.1%, P = .163), whereas no difference was observed in the radiofrequency subgroup (80.5% vs 79.4%, P = .309). There was no treatment-by-modality interaction (P for interaction = .64). Freedom from repeat ablation was higher with LAPWI in the cryoballoon subgroup (96.5% vs 82.0%, P = .029) but not in the radiofrequency subgroup (90.1% vs 88.5%, P = .210), without a significant treatment-by-modality interaction (P for interaction = .17). Overall complication rates were similar between groups; symptomatic gastroparesis occurred in 3 patients in the radiofrequency LAPWI subgroup. CONCLUSION: Adjunctive LAPWI did not significantly improve freedom from atrial arrhythmia with either ablation modality, with no significant treatment-by-modality interaction. The potentially favorable findings with LAPWI after cryoballoon PVI should be considered exploratory and hypothesis generating.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

CORNERSTONE-AF 亚组分析显示，无论采用射频还是冷冻球囊行 PVI，附加左房后壁隔离均未显著提高 18 个月无房性心律失常生存率，且无消融方式交互作用。

研究动机

持续性及长程持续性 AF 消融中，附加左房后壁隔离（LAPWI）应用广泛，但既往证据对其疗效存在争议；不同 PVI 消融方式（射频与冷冻球囊）下 LAPWI 的获益是否不同尚缺乏随机对照数据，而两种方式所能达到的后壁隔离面积与损伤特征本身可能存在差异。

方法

本研究为 CORNERSTONE-AF 随机试验的事后亚组分析，比较单纯 PVI（n=259）与 PVI+LAPWI（n=254）的 18 个月结局，随机化按 PVI 消融方式（射频 384 例、冷冻球囊 129 例）分层。主要终点为无房性心律失常，次要终点包括无重复消融；摘要未报告具体统计方法及效应量/95%CI。

结果

冷冻球囊亚组中实际隔离的后壁面积显著大于射频亚组；两种方式下附加 LAPWI 均显著延长手术时间，但不增加透视时间。18 个月无房性心律失常率：冷冻球囊组 LAPWI 与单纯 PVI 为 82.0% vs 69.1%（P=.163），射频组为 80.5% vs 79.4%（P=.309），治疗与消融方式的交互作用无统计学意义（P=.64）。无重复消融率：冷冻球囊组 96.5% vs 82.0%（P=.029），射频组 90.1% vs 88.5%（P=.210），交互作用 P=.17。总体并发症两组相近；射频+LAPWI 亚组有 3 例出现症状性胃轻瘫。

结论与意义

附加 LAPWI 在射频与冷冻球囊两种方式下均未显著改善无房性心律失常生存，且无显著的治疗-方式交互作用；冷冻球囊 PVI 后 LAPWI 在无重复消融及无房性心律失常方面的数值优势仅为探索性、假说生成性发现，合并 3 例症状性胃轻瘫提示后壁消融需权衡风险，尚不足以改变现有实践，需更大样本以主要终点为效能的随机研究验证。

11

Atrial fibrillation cycle length dynamics during pulmonary vein isolation and posterior wall pulsed field ablation.

Heart Rhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42785610
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Lorenzo Bianchini、Marco Schiavone、Alessio Gasperetti 等 17 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: The benefit of additional left atrial posterior wall ablation (LAPWA), beyond pulmonary vein isolation (PVI), in the treatment of persistent atrial fibrillation (PersAF) remains controversial. OBJECTIVE: We investigated whether signals recorded with a pentaspline pulsed field ablation (PFA) catheter can identify patients with PersAF with rapid left atrial posterior wall (LAPW) activity who may benefit from LAPWA. We also evaluated whether PFA-induced atrial fibrillation cycle length prolongation predicts arrhythmia-free survival. METHODS: In this multicenter observational study, consecutive patients with PersAF undergoing first-time PVI plus LAPWA with a pentaspline PFA catheter were included. Atrial fibrillation cycle length was measured using the FARS-10 (10 consecutive Fastest Atrial Repetitive Similar morphology signal) method at 3 time points-baseline, after PVI, and after LAPWA-using distal coronary sinus signals. LAPW cycle length (LAPW-CL) was measured after PVI from multiple LAPW positions. Any atrial tachyarrhythmia (ATA) lasting >30 seconds beyond the 2-month blanking period were recorded. RESULTS: 270 patients were included. The overall 12-month ATA-free survival rate was 72.6%. "Fast LAPW activity" was defined as an LAPW-CL value shorter than the mean LAPW-CL of the entire cohort (191.4 ± 2.4 ms; ≤190 ms). Fast LAPW activity was not associated with arrhythmia recurrence (hazard ratio = 1.263, 95% confidence interval = 0.739-2.160; P = .393). In contrast, a greater coronary sinus cycle length (CS-CL) prolongation was independently associated with a lower arrhythmia recurrence risk (hazard ratio = 0.298, 95% confidence interval = 0.017-0.520; P < .001). A CS-CL increase >23.5% from baseline had a sensitivity of 0.65 and a specificity of 0.72 for predicting ATA-free survival. CONCLUSION: Post-PVI fast LAPW activity does not predict recurrence after LAPWA in patients with PersAF. CS-CL prolongation >23.5% after LAPWA may serve as a useful real-time marker of effective substrate modification and arrhythmia control.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在持续性房颤患者中，PVI 后左房后壁快速活动并不能预测加做后壁脉冲场消融后的复发；而 LAPWA 后冠状窦房颤周径延长超过 23.5% 与无心律失常生存独立相关。

研究动机

持续性房颤（PersAF）在肺静脉隔离（PVI）之外是否应常规加做左房后壁消融（LAPWA），目前证据仍存争议，缺乏术中实时指标来筛选真正获益的患者。本研究探讨以五分支脉冲场消融（PFA）导管记录的信号能否识别后壁活动快速、可能从 LAPWA 获益的患者，并评估 PFA 导致的房颤周径（AF cycle length）延长能否预测无心律失常生存。

方法

多中心观察性研究，连续纳入首次接受 PVI+LAPWA、使用五分支 PFA 导管的持续性房颤患者，共 270 例。采用 FARS-10 方法（连续 10 个最快、形态相似的心房重复信号）在三个时间点（基线、PVI 后、LAPWA 后）以远端冠状窦信号测量房颤周径（CS-CL）；PVI 后自左房后壁多个位点测量 LAPW-CL，并以全队列 LAPW-CL 均值（191.4±2.4 ms）为界定义“后壁快速活动”（≤190 ms）。终点为 2 个月空白期后持续 >30 秒的任何房性快速性心律失常（ATA），采用生存分析（风险比及 95%CI）评估预测因素。

结果

12 个月总体无 ATA 生存率为 72.6%。后壁快速活动与复发无关联（HR=1.263，95%CI 0.739–2.160，P=0.393）。相反，CS-CL 延长幅度越大，复发风险越低且为独立相关因素（HR=0.298，95%CI 0.017–0.520，P<0.001）。CS-CL 较基线延长 >23.5% 预测无 ATA 生存的敏感度为 0.65、特异度为 0.72。

结论与意义

在持续性房颤患者中，PVI 后左房后壁快速活动不能预测 PVI+LAPWA 后的复发，不宜单独作为加做后壁消融的筛选依据；而 LAPWA 后 CS-CL 延长 >23.5% 可作为提示基质改良有效、心律失常控制良好的实时指标。本研究为观察性设计、样本来自单一人群且后壁快速活动界值源于队列均值，尚需前瞻性验证其对消融策略的指导价值。

Catheter ablation for atrial fibrillation in very young adults: Multicentric data from the AF-YOUNG registry.

Heart Rhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42785609
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Florian Doldi、Friederike Pavel、Fabienne Kreimer 等 11 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Atrial fibrillation below age 30 years is rare; ablation outcome data are scarce. OBJECTIVE: The multicenter AF-YOUNG registry analyzed clinical, procedural, and outcome data from consecutive patients aged ≤ 30 years undergoing first-time catheter ablation. METHODS: Two high-volume German centers retrospectively included patients aged ≤ 30 years undergoing first-time pulmonary vein isolation for atrial fibrillation (2005-2025). Recurrence was defined as documented atrial fibrillation after a 90-day blanking period. RESULTS: Of 140 patients (mean age of 29.1 ± 3.1 years; 28.6% female), 104 were included in the outcome analysis (paroxysmal atrial fibrillation, 83.6%; persistent atrial fibrillation, 16.4%; 18.6% with a family history of atrial fibrillation). For pulmonary vein isolation, radiofrequency was used in 49.3%, cryoballoon was used in 45.7%, and pulsed-field ablation was sued in 5%. Mean procedure duration was 117 ± 66 min. Over a median follow-up of 1 (interquartile range = 0.25-1.74) year, 10 patients had recurrences. Freedom from atrial fibrillation was 93.6, 88.2, and 82.1% at 1, 2, and 3 years; it was 94.9% 1 year after up to 2 procedures. Paroxysmal vs persistent atrial fibrillation showed a better outcome: 98.5% vs 67.7% at 1 year and 87.8% vs 50.8% at 3 years (log-rank P < .001). 3 in-hospital complications (2.1%) occurred: 1 vascular complication requiring surgical repair, a pericarditis, and a mild pulmonary-vein stenosis without need for intervention. CONCLUSION: In adults aged < 30 years, catheter ablation for atrial fibrillation delivers high rhythm control with low complication rates. Benefit is lower in persistent than in paroxysmal atrial fibrillation. Atrial fibrillation subtype is the strongest determinant, which may argue for early referral to ablation as first-line rhythm control in young adults.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

≤30岁房颤患者首次导管消融以PVI为主，3年无房颤生存率82.1%、院内并发症2.1%；阵发性显著优于持续性（3年87.8% vs 50.8%，P<0.001）。

研究动机

30岁以下成人房颤在临床上罕见，既往关于该人群导管消融的疗效与安全性证据极为匮乏，样本量小、随访短，导致极年轻患者是否应将消融作为一线节律控制手段、以及房颤类型对该人群结局的影响均不明确。AF-YOUNG注册研究旨在填补这一证据缺口。

方法

多中心回顾性注册研究（AF-YOUNG），纳入德国两家高容量中心2005—2025年连续收治的年龄≤30岁、首次因房颤行肺静脉隔离（PVI）的患者140例（平均年龄29.1±3.1岁，女性28.6%），其中104例进入结局分析（阵发性房颤83.6%、持续性房颤16.4%，18.6%有房颤家族史）。消融方式为射频49.3%、冷冻球囊45.7%、脉冲场消融5.0%。复发定义为90天空白期后记录到房颤。摘要仅报告采用log-rank检验比较房颤亚型间无房颤生存率，未说明多因素校正或其他统计方法。

结果

平均手术时长117±66分钟。中位随访1年（四分位距0.25–1.74年）期间共10例复发。1、2、3年无房颤生存率分别为93.6%、88.2%、82.1%；允许最多2次手术后1年无房颤生存率为94.9%。阵发性与持续性房颤相比结局更佳：1年98.5% vs 67.7%，3年87.8% vs 50.8%（log-rank P<0.001）。院内并发症3例（2.1%）：1例需外科修补的血管并发症、1例心包炎、1例轻度肺静脉狭窄且无需干预。

结论与意义

在30岁以下成人中，房颤导管消融可获得较高的节律控制率且并发症发生率低，但持续性房颤的获益明显低于阵发性房颤，房颤亚型是该人群结局的最强决定因素，提示极年轻患者一旦确诊应尽早转诊行消融，作为一线节律控制策略。局限性包括回顾性设计、仅两家中心、总样本140例且仅104例进入结局分析、中位随访仅1年、未报告多因素分析结果，上述结论仍需前瞻性大样本验证。

Anatomical approach from aortic sinus of Valsalva for para-Hisian premature ventricular contractions: Safety and outcomes.

Heart Rhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42785608
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Masafumi Sugawara、Jakub Sroubek、Justin Lee 等 20 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: The optimal ablation strategy for para-Hisian premature ventricular contractions (PVCs) remains uncertain because of the risk of injury to the conduction system. OBJECTIVE: To evaluate the safety and outcomes of catheter ablation for para-Hisian PVCs, with a particular focus on the efficacy and safety of an anatomical approach from the coronary cusps. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed 114 patients undergoing para-Hisian PVC ablation. Acute success, long-term clinical success (≥80% PVC reduction), conduction system injury, and ablation sites were assessed. RESULTS: Acute procedural success was achieved in 89 patients (78%). However, long-term clinical success was achieved in only 52% of the patients with acute success. Coronary cusp ablation was attempted in a total of 35 patients (31%) and was performed significantly more frequently in clinical success cases than in clinical failures (38% vs 7%, P = .005). Among the 35 patients undergoing cusp ablation, PVCs were terminated in 49% (17/35), while transient suppression or morphological change of PVCs was observed in an additional 31% (11/35). Predictors of successful cusp ablation included earlier activation at the cusp and close anatomical proximity between the cusp and the earliest activation site in the His bundle region. Conduction system injury occurred in 31 patients overall, predominantly during His bundle region ablation, with only 1 event occurring during cusp ablation. CONCLUSION: Coronary cusp ablation incorporated into a stepwise, multi-site ablation strategy may be associated with better procedural outcomes for para-Hisian PVC ablation without compromising safety. Earlier activation and close anatomical proximity between the para-Hisian site and the cusp region predict a successful ablation using this approach.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在114例旁希氏束室性早搏患者中，回顾性分析显示，经主动脉窦冠状动脉瓣解剖途径消融是阶梯式多部位策略的一部分；长期临床成功率仅52%，但冠状动脉瓣途径与临床成功相关且传导系统损伤风险低。

研究动机

旁希氏束起源的室性早搏（PVC）导管消融时，邻近希氏束及传导系统，存在导致房室传导阻滞、需植入起搏器的风险，因此最佳消融策略（尤其是是否以及何时采用主动脉窦冠状动脉瓣途径）尚不明确，既往证据多为小样本、缺乏对冠状动脉瓣解剖途径疗效与安全性的系统评估。

方法

单中心回顾性研究，纳入114例接受旁希氏束PVC消融的患者。评估指标包括急性手术成功率、长期临床成功率（PVC负荷减少≥80%）、传导系统损伤以及消融靶点部位。按临床成功与失败分组，比较冠状动脉瓣消融的使用比例，并分析冠状动脉瓣消融成功的预测因素。摘要未报告具体随访时长、统计模型及置信区间。

结果

急性成功率78%（89/114），但急性成功者中仅52%达到长期临床成功。共31%（35/114）的患者尝试了冠状动脉瓣消融，且临床成功组的使用比例显著高于失败组（38% vs 7%，P=.005）。35例冠状动脉瓣消融中，49%（17/35）PVC终止，另有31%（11/35）出现PVC一过性抑制或形态改变。冠状动脉瓣消融成功的预测因素为：冠状动脉瓣处记录到更早的激动，以及冠状动脉瓣与希氏束区最早激动点解剖位置邻近。全组共31例发生传导系统损伤，主要发生于希氏束区消融时，冠状动脉瓣消融过程中仅1例。

结论与意义

将主动脉窦冠状动脉瓣消融纳入阶梯式、多部位消融策略，可能在不增加安全风险的前提下改善旁希氏束PVC的消融结局；冠状动脉瓣处更早激动及冠状动脉瓣与旁希氏束靶点解剖邻近可预测该途径消融成功。但本研究为回顾性设计、长期成功率仅约半数、缺乏随机对照与统一随访标准，冠状动脉瓣途径的适应证与优势仍需前瞻性研究验证。

[Atrial fibrillation : An overview of the various treatment options].

Herz·2026-09-24·PMID 42782286
房颤与消融 Journal Article Review English Abstract

Laura Fischbach、Stephan Willems、Arian Sultan

英文摘要

Atrial fibrillation is associated with a reduced quality of life, an increased risk of thromboembolic events and higher mortality. A fast and individualized treatment is essential, particularly to positively influence the progression. In addition to risk-adapted oral anticoagulation, early rhythm control is crucial for reducing the burden of symptoms as well as for decreasing hospitalization, ischemic stroke and cardiovascular death. Options for rhythm control include pharmacological strategies, electrical cardioversion, and catheter-based ablation procedures. Electrical cardioversion is particularly suitable for the acute treatment of hemodynamically unstable patients, while pharmacological treatment and catheter ablation represent long-term treatment approaches. Class IC antiarrhythmic drugs such as flecainide and propafenone are contraindicated in patients with structural heart disease.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

该综述指出，房颤治疗应在风险适配的口服抗凝基础上尽早进行节律控制；节律控制手段包括药物、电复律与导管消融，其中电复律适用于血流动力学不稳定的急性期患者。

研究动机

房颤与生活质量下降、血栓栓塞事件风险升高及死亡率增加相关，快速且个体化的治疗对延缓疾病进展至关重要。既往证据的缺口在于：需要明确不同节律控制策略（药物、电复律、导管消融）各自的适用场景与长期定位，并厘清抗心律失常药物的禁忌人群；本文即针对各类治疗选择进行综述。

方法

本文为综述性文章，并非原始临床研究，因此摘要未报告研究类型细节、样本量、干预对照、终点定义及统计方法，上述信息均摘要未报告。文章按治疗类别进行梳理：风险适配的口服抗凝、早期节律控制（药物策略、电复律、导管消融），并讨论不同药物的适用与禁忌人群。

结果

摘要未报告任何定量结果，包括效应量、95% 置信区间及 P 值。文章给出以下定性结论：早期节律控制对减轻症状负担、减少住院、缺血性卒中及心血管死亡具有关键作用；电复律尤其适用于血流动力学不稳定患者的急性处理；药物治疗与导管消融属于长期治疗策略；IC 类抗心律失常药物（如氟卡尼、普罗帕酮）禁用于结构性心脏病患者。

结论与意义

房颤管理应采取抗凝与早期节律控制并重的个体化策略，并按临床情境分层选择治疗手段：血流动力学不稳定者首选电复律，长期节律控制可在抗心律失常药物与导管消融之间选择，结构性心脏病患者须避免 IC 类药物。本文为叙述性综述，未提供原始数据，亦未系统评价各策略间的疗效比较，结论的证据等级与剂量、消融方式等细节摘要未报告，临床应用时需结合最新指南与随机对照试验结果。

Postoperative Atrial Fibrillation and Long-Term Outcomes After Isolated Coronary Artery Bypass Grafting

Interdiscip Cardiovasc Thorac Surg·2026-09-24·PMID 42786670
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Soares Moreira H、Brandão B、Abreu A 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Objectives New-onset postoperative atrial fibrillation is the most common complication after cardiac surgery. This study aimed to evaluate the association between postoperative atrial fibrillation and short-term and long-term clinical outcomes after isolated coronary artery bypass grafting.Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study of adults who underwent isolated coronary artery bypass grafting between 2000 and 2015 at a tertiary care centre. Baseline differences between patients who developed postoperative atrial fibrillation and those who did not were adjusted using inverse probability weighting based on propensity scores derived from demographic, clinical and procedural variables. In-hospital outcomes were analysed using weighted regression models, and long-term all-cause mortality was assessed using weighted Cox proportional hazards regression.Results Among 5,170 included patients, postoperative atrial fibrillation occurred in 652 (13%). After inverse probability weighting, postoperative atrial fibrillation was associated with a higher risk of perioperative stroke (odds ratio 2.59, 95% confidence interval 1.19-5.64), prolonged mechanical ventilation (odds ratio 1.73, 95% confidence interval 1.22-2.45) and longer hospital stay (mean difference 2.4 days, 95% confidence interval 1.6-3.3). During a median follow-up of 12.8 years, postoperative atrial fibrillation was also associated with higher long-term all-cause mortality (hazard ratio 1.31, 95% confidence interval 1.23-1.51).Conclusions In patients undergoing isolated coronary artery bypass grafting, postoperative atrial fibrillation was associated, after inverse probability weighting, with major perioperative complications and reduced long-term survival. These findings suggest that postoperative atrial fibrillation may represent a marker of increased postoperative and long-term risk.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在接受单纯冠状动脉旁路移植术的5170例成人中，13%出现术后新发房颤；经逆概率加权后，该并发症与围术期卒中、机械通气延长、住院时间延长及远期全因死亡风险升高独立相关。

研究动机

术后新发房颤是心脏外科术后最常见的并发症，但既往证据多混杂外科类型、样本量有限，且对单纯冠状动脉旁路移植术（CABG）人群的短期并发症与远期生存的关联尚不明确，临床对术后房颤究竟是可干预的危险因素还是整体风险增高的标志物存在争议。

方法

单中心回顾性队列研究，纳入2000—2015年在该三级医疗中心接受单纯CABG的成人患者共5170例，其中652例（13%）发生术后新发房颤。以人口学、临床及手术相关变量构建倾向性评分，采用逆概率加权（IPW）平衡组间基线差异；院内结局采用加权回归模型分析，远期全因死亡采用加权Cox比例风险回归分析，中位随访12.8年。摘要未报告具体终点定义细节及随访完整度。

结果

加权后，术后房颤组围术期卒中风险升高（OR 2.59，95%CI 1.19–5.64），机械通气时间延长（OR 1.73，95%CI 1.22–2.45），住院时间平均延长2.4天（95%CI 1.6–3.3）。中位随访12.8年期间，术后房颤与远期全因死亡风险升高相关（HR 1.31，95%CI 1.23–1.51）。摘要未报告心血管死亡、卒中复发等次要结局及绝对风险差值。

结论与意义

在单纯CABG人群中，术后新发房颤经逆概率加权校正后仍与主要围术期并发症及远期生存率下降相关，提示其可能是术后及远期风险增高的标志物，而非单纯的良性电生理现象。临床意义在于应将术后房颤患者纳入更密切的围术期监护与长期随访，并值得进一步开展前瞻性研究评估节律干预或抗凝策略能否改善预后。局限性为单中心回顾性设计、残余混杂无法完全排除、缺乏死亡原因与房颤复发数据。

Clinical Outcomes of Left Atrial Appendage Occlusion During Cardiac Surgery in Patients Without Atrial Fibrillation: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis

J Am Heart Assoc·2026-09-24·PMID 42786657
房颤与消融 Meta-Analysis Systematic Review Journal Article

Song Q、Tu X、Zheng Z 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

Background The left atrial appendage is the primary source of cardiac thrombi, and prophylactic exclusion during cardiac surgery may reduce thromboembolic risk. However, its benefit in patients without preoperative atrial fibrillation (AF) remains uncertain.Methods We searched PubMed, Web of Science, EMBASE, and the Cochrane Library for randomized controlled trials and observational studies comparing surgical left atrial appendage occlusion (LAAO) versus no LAAO in patients with sinus rhythm undergoing cardiac surgery. Primary outcomes were new-onset transient ischemic attack (TIA)/stroke, postoperative AF, and all-cause death. Dichotomous outcomes were pooled as odds ratios and continuous outcomes as mean differences, with 95% CIs.Results Eleven studies involving 12 145 participants were included. Compared with no LAAO, surgical LAAO reduced new-onset TIA/stroke (odds ratio, 0.75 [95% CI, 0.62-0.90]) and bleeding (odds ratio, 0.78 [95% CI, 0.60-1.00]) but increased postoperative AF (odds ratio, 1.27 [95% CI, 1.06-1.53]). No significant differences were detected between the 2 groups in all-cause death, 30-day death, and length of stay. Subgroup analyses showed some variation in effect estimates across study design, type of surgery, and LAAO technique.Conclusions In patients with sinus rhythm undergoing cardiac surgery, surgical LAAO was associated with a lower risk of new-onset TIA/stroke and bleeding but a higher incidence of postoperative AF, with no significant differences in all-cause death, 30-day death, or length of hospital stay. Study design, type of cardiac surgery, and LAAO technique may influence these associations. These findings should be interpreted cautiously given low to moderate certainty of evidence.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在窦性心律接受心脏手术的患者中，同期外科左心耳封堵可降低新发TIA/卒中与出血风险，但术后房颤发生率升高，全因死亡无差异。

研究动机

左心耳是心源性血栓的主要来源，心脏手术中预防性切除或封堵左心耳可能降低血栓栓塞风险；但既往证据主要来自合并房颤人群，对术前无房颤、窦性心律患者是否有获益尚不明确，临床决策存在缺口。

方法

系统评价与荟萃分析。检索 PubMed、Web of Science、EMBASE、Cochrane Library，纳入比较心脏手术同期外科左心耳封堵（LAAO）与不封堵的随机对照试验及观察性研究，对象为窦性心律、接受心脏手术的患者。主要终点为新发短暂性脑缺血发作（TIA）/卒中、术后房颤、全因死亡；二分类变量以比值比（OR）合并、连续变量以均数差合并，均给出95%CI。

结果

共纳入11项研究、12 145例患者。与不封堵相比，外科LAAO降低新发TIA/卒中（OR 0.75，95%CI 0.62-0.90）与出血（OR 0.78，95%CI 0.60-1.00），但术后房颤增加（OR 1.27，95%CI 1.06-1.53）。全因死亡、30天死亡与住院时长两组间无显著差异（具体效应值摘要未报告）。亚组分析显示，研究设计、手术类型与LAAO技术可影响效应估计；证据确定性为低至中等。

结论与意义

对窦性心律接受心脏手术的患者，预防性外科左心耳封堵可能减少新发TIA/卒中及出血，但以术后房颤增多为代价，死亡与住院时长未见获益。该结果提示应个体化权衡血栓栓塞风险与术后房颤风险，并考虑术式与封堵技术差异；鉴于证据确定性低至中等且以观察性研究为主，尚不足以支持对所有无房颤心脏手术患者常规封堵，需更高质量随机试验验证。

Social Determinants of Guideline-Directed Oral Anticoagulation Prescription in Patients With Atrial Fibrillation Without Contraindications: Findings From the American Heart Association Get With the Guidelines-Atrial Fibrillation Registry

J Am Heart Assoc·2026-09-24·PMID 42786624
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Osawa I、Shimada YJ

英文摘要

Background Oral anticoagulant (OAC) prescriptions recommended by atrial fibrillation guidelines to prevent stroke may be influenced by social determinants of health (SDOH). We aimed to uncover patient and institutional SDOH associated with failure to prescribe guideline-directed OAC and to develop an SDOH-based scoring system to predict failure to prescribe OAC.Methods We analyzed the nationwide, 249-hospital American Heart Association GWTG-AFib (Get With The Guidelines-Atrial Fibrillation) registry. We included adult patients hospitalized for atrial fibrillation with class I indication for OAC and no contraindications. Using a logistic regression model, we identified patient and institutional SDOH of failure to prescribe OAC, adjusting for the other SDOH. Next, we developed a machine learning-based prediction model in a training data set and examined the relative contribution of each SDOH. We then compared the risk of failure to prescribe OAC in an independent test data set for validation.Results Among 68 628 eligible patients, 1345 (2.0%) patients did not receive guideline-directed OAC prescription. The guideline-directed OAC prescription was influenced by both institutional SDOH (eg, hospital region, hospital size, the absence of a cardiology team) and patient SDOH (eg, sex, insurance). The absence of a cardiology team was the most important predictor (adjusted odds ratio [OR], 3.72 [95% CI, 3.20-4.32]). Patients with the higher-than-Youden-index-cutoff prediction score had higher risk of failure to prescribe OAC than those with the lower-than-cutoff prediction score (OR, 3.11 [95% CI, 2.67-3.63]).Conclusions We identified both institutional and patient SDOH of guideline-directed OAC prescription failure. We further developed and validated a SDOH-based scoring system to predict failure to prescribe OAC.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

基于美国 249 家医院 GWTG-AFib 登记中 68 628 例有 I 类适应证且无禁忌的住院 AF 患者，2.0% 未获指南推荐的 OAC；无心脏科团队是最强预测因子（aOR 3.72），并据此建立并验证了 SDOH 预测评分。

研究动机

指南推荐 OAC 可显著降低 AF 相关卒中风险，但临床实践中仍有部分有明确 I 类适应证且无禁忌的患者未获处方。既往研究多聚焦单一人口学因素，对医院层面社会健康决定因素（SDOH）关注不足，且缺乏可量化、可验证的预测工具。本研究旨在系统识别与未处方指南导向 OAC 相关的患者及机构层面 SDOH，并构建基于 SDOH 的预测评分。

方法

基于美国心脏协会 GWTG-AFib 全国登记（249 家医院）的回顾性分析。纳入因 AF 住院、具有 OAC I 类适应证且无禁忌证的成年患者，共 68 628 例。主要终点为出院时未处方指南导向 OAC。采用 logistic 回归模型在校正其他 SDOH 后识别患者与机构层面的独立相关因素；随后在训练集中构建机器学习预测模型并评估各 SDOH 的相对贡献，在独立测试集中比较不同预测评分水平者的未处方风险以完成验证。

结果

68 628 例中 1 345 例（2.0%）未获指南导向 OAC 处方。未处方同时受机构层面 SDOH（医院所在地区、医院规模、缺乏心脏科团队）与患者层面 SDOH（性别、保险类型）影响。缺乏心脏科团队是最重要的预测因子，校正 OR 3.72（95%CI 3.20–4.32）。预测评分高于 Youden 指数切点者未处方 OAC 的风险显著高于低于切点者（OR 3.11，95%CI 2.67–3.63）。各因素的具体 P 值摘要未报告。

结论与意义

指南导向 OAC 处方的缺失不仅取决于临床因素，还与机构和患者层面的社会健康决定因素密切相关，其中缺乏心脏科团队的影响最为突出。所建立的 SDOH 评分在独立数据集中完成验证，可用于识别未处方风险较高的医院与人群，为质量改进与靶向干预提供依据。局限在于数据来自自愿参与的住院登记、事件率仅 2.0%、且为观察性设计，无法推断因果，评分的外部适用性仍需前瞻性验证。

PubMed DOI 原文

Efficacy and Safety of 60 W Versus 90 W, 4-s Radiofrequency Applications to the Esophagus-Adjacent Left Atrial Posterior Wall During Pulmonary Vein Isolation: A Pilot Study.

J Arrhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42781049
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Hideharu Okamatsu、Ken Okumura、Kakeru Tomaru 等 10 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: In very high-power (51-90 W), short-duration (4 s) radiofrequency applications (QMODE+) using the QDOT MICRO catheter, the optimal power setting at the left atrial (LA) posterior wall adjacent to the esophagus for treating atrial fibrillation (AF) remains unclear. OBJECTIVE: We evaluated the efficacy and safety of 60 W versus 90 W QMODE+ radiofrequency applications in the periesophageal region. METHODS: Fifty AF patients were randomized into two groups based on the QMODE+ power applied to the periesophageal region: 60 W (n = 24) and 90 W (n = 26). We compared the incidence of residual conduction gaps in the periesophageal region and endoscopic esophageal lesions, as well as the 1-year atrial tachyarrhythmia (AT) recurrence rate between the two groups. RESULTS: Median contact force was significantly higher in the 60 W group than in the 90 W group (12.9 g [IQR 10.2, 15.8] in the 60 W group and 10.9 g [IQR 8.4, 14.2] in the 90 W group, p < 0.001). The rates of residual conduction gaps (8.3% and 11.5%, respectively, p = 1.00) and esophageal lesions (4.2% in each group) were similar between the two groups. The 1-year AT recurrence rate did not significantly differ between the two groups (20.8% vs. 7.7%, respectively, p = 0.24). CONCLUSION: 60 W and 90 W QMODE+ RF applications for the periesophageal region achieved comparable rates of acute conduction block, endoscopy-detected esophageal injury, and 12-month AT recurrence-free survival, although contact force was significantly higher in the 60 W group. TRIAL REGISTRATION: Japan Registry of Clinical Trials (jRCT); jRCT1072230115 (Registered on February 8, 2024).

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

50例房颤患者随机比较食管邻近左房后壁60 W与90 W QMODE+（4 s）消融，残余传导间隙、内镜下食管损伤及1年房性快速性心律失常复发率均无显著差异。

研究动机

使用QDOT MICRO导管的QMODE+模式可行极高功率（51–90 W）、短时间（4 s）射频消融，但在紧邻食管的左房后壁区域，最佳功率设置尚不明确：功率越高理论上单点损伤越充分、效率越高，却可能增加食管热损伤风险，而现有证据不足以指导该部位的功率选择。

方法

日本开展的前瞻性随机对照预试验（jRCT1072230115，2024年2月8日注册）。共纳入50例接受肺静脉隔离的房颤患者，按食管旁区域所采用的QMODE+功率随机分为60 W组（n=24）与90 W组（n=26）。比较两组食管旁区域残余传导间隙发生率、内镜下食管损伤发生率以及1年房性快速性心律失常（AT）复发率；摘要未报告具体统计学方法及效应量的95%置信区间。

结果

60 W组中位接触力显著高于90 W组（12.9 g［IQR 10.2–15.8］vs 10.9 g［IQR 8.4–14.2］，p<0.001）。残余传导间隙发生率两组相似（8.3% vs 11.5%，p=1.00）；内镜检出食管损伤发生率两组均为4.2%。1年AT复发率两组无显著差异（20.8% vs 7.7%，p=0.24）。摘要未报告手术时间、单点消融次数及并发症的其他细节。

结论与意义

在食管旁左房后壁行QMODE+消融时，60 W与90 W在急性传导阻滞、内镜检出食管损伤及12个月AT无复发生存方面表现相当，提示90 W并未带来额外疗效，亦未在本样本中显示更高的食管损伤风险，但60 W组需更高的接触力。该结果为预试验，样本量小、单中心、未报告置信区间且随访1年，不足以确证两种功率的等效性；临床实践中仍应根据接触力、导管稳定性与食管位置个体化选择功率，并需更大样本研究验证。

A Special Muscle Fiber Surviving After Septal Line Ablation of Mitral‐Isthmus Dependent Atrial Flutter

J Arrhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42787446
房颤与消融 brief-report Brief Report Journal Article 开放获取

Yamada T、Akrawinthawong K、Miyazaki Y 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

Following unsuccessful supero-septal line ablation for mitral isthmus-dependent atrial flutter, a conduction gap may occur along the septoatrial bundle (SAB). In such cases, linear ablation extending perpendicularly to the supero-septal ablation line (SSL) toward Bachmann's bundle (BB) may achieve complete conduction block across the supero-septal ablation line.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

对二尖瓣峡部依赖性心房扑动行上间隔线消融失败后，残余传导可沿间隔心房束存在；自上间隔线垂直向Bachmann束延伸消融可能实现上间隔线完全传导阻滞。

研究动机

二尖瓣峡部依赖性心房扑动常需线性消融，但上间隔线消融后仍可能因间隔心房束（SAB）存活而形成传导间隙，导致跨线阻滞不全；摘要指出这一临床问题，但未系统交代既往证据缺口。

方法

摘要未报告研究类型、样本量、纳入人群及统计方法；所描述策略为：上间隔线（SSL）消融未成功后，如存在沿间隔心房束（SAB）的传导间隙，则加做垂直于SSL、指向Bachmann束（BB）的线性消融。终点为SSL跨线完全传导阻滞，摘要未报告终点定义细节与评估方法。

结果

摘要未报告定量结果、效应量、95%CI或P值；仅提出上述垂直延伸消融策略可能实现上间隔线完全传导阻滞，未报告成功率和随访数据。

结论与意义

对上间隔线消融后经SAB残余传导的病例，向BB方向垂直加线可能是有待验证的补救策略，可能提高跨线阻滞成功率；但缺乏样本量、对照和随访数据，不能据此改变临床实践，需进一步研究确认。

Effectiveness of Bachmann's Bundle Pacing in a Patient With Severe Heart Failure and Refractory Atrial Fibrillation

J Arrhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42787434
房颤与消融 brief-report Brief Report Journal Article 开放获取

Murase Y、Ogawa Y、Imai H 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

This case report highlights CRT-D with Bachmann's bundle pacing (BBP) in a patient with severe heart failure and refractory atrial fibrillation. BBP preserved atrioventricular conduction without PR prolongation, maintained left ventricular-only pacing, and was associated with suppression of atrial fibrillation and improvement in left atrial contractile function.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例重度心衰合并难治性房颤患者，在 CRT-D 基础上行 Bachmann 束起搏（BBP），起搏后房室传导保留、PR 间期未延长，维持单纯左室起搏，并观察到房颤被抑制、左房收缩功能改善。

研究动机

重度心衰合并难治性房颤患者节律控制困难，而 CRT 疗效依赖房室同步和足够的双室起搏比例。传统右房起搏可使左右房电激动不同步、PR 间期延长，甚至削弱左室起搏比例；Bachmann 束起搏（BBP）通过起搏 Bachmann 束使双房再同步，理论上可兼顾房室同步与房颤抑制，但在重度心衰合并难治性房颤人群中的可行性证据仅限于零散个案，缺乏系统数据。

方法

个案报道（case report），共 1 例重度心力衰竭合并难治性心房颤动的患者，接受 CRT-D 并 Bachmann 束起搏（BBP）。观察内容包括：房室传导是否保留及 PR 间期变化、起搏模式（能否维持单纯左室起搏）、房颤是否被抑制，以及左房收缩功能变化。样本仅 1 例；随访时长、起搏参数设置、房颤负荷量化方法与左房功能评估的具体手段，摘要均未报告；统计方法摘要未报告（描述性个案资料）。

结果

摘要未报告任何定量数据（PR 间期数值、左房功能参数、房颤负荷或发作次数等均未给出），仅作定性描述：BBP 下房室传导保留且未见 PR 间期延长，得以维持单纯左室起搏，并伴有房颤抑制与左房收缩功能改善。摘要亦未报告并发症，也未报告具体随访期内房颤复发情况。

结论与意义

该个案提示，对重度心衰合并难治性房颤且已置入 CRT-D 的患者，加用 Bachmann 束起搏可能在不延长 PR 间期的前提下维持房室同步与单纯左室起搏，并可能有助于抑制房颤、改善左房机械功能，为难治性房颤的器械治疗提供了一种思路。但该研究为单例、无对照、缺乏量化终点与随访数据，证据等级低，仅具假设生成价值；对电生理实践而言，尚不足以支持将该策略作为常规推荐，需在前瞻性队列或随机研究中明确适应证、起搏参数优化方案及对房颤负荷和心衰结局的长期影响。

Association Between Temperature Management Using Passive Thermal Insulation and a Modified Aldrete Score During Cryoablation of Paroxysmal Atrial Fibrillation Under General Anesthesia

J Arrhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42787973
房颤与消融 research-article Journal Article 开放获取

Yamaguchi A、Takemoto M、Nagata T 等 13 位作者

英文摘要

Background Unintentional intraoperative hypothermia (core temperature < 36°C), a common and frequently preventable complication in patients undergoing surgery under general anesthesia (GA), involves adverse effects on coagulation and prolonged recovery from GA. Thus, this study aimed to clarify the effect of passive thermal insulation (PTI) with a heat insulation blanket (HIB) on successful early recovery from GA in patients undergoing cryoablation for atrial fibrillation (AF) under GA.Methods Fifty-five patients undergoing cryoablation under GA for nonvalvular paroxysmal AF were prospectively assigned to the Blanket (n = 28) or Control (conventional towelettes; n = 27) group. The esophageal temperature was measured using an esophageal temperature probe before and after cryoablation. The esophageal hypothermia was defined as a temperature < 36°C. The Δ in esophageal temperature was defined as the difference between the pre- and post-anesthesia temperature. The primary endpoint of this study was successful early recovery from GA, defined as a Modified Aldrete score (MAS) ≥ 9 at 2 h after the discontinuation of sedation/anesthesia in patients undergoing cryoablation under GA.Results This study demonstrated that the PTI using HIBs significantly reduced the incidence of esophageal hypothermia from 93% to 68% and increased the rate of successful early recovery from GA from 37% to 64%.Conclusions PTI using HIBs was associated with a smaller decline in esophageal temperature and a higher proportion of patients achieving MAS ≥ 9 at 2 h after discontinuation of anesthesia during cryoablation under GA. Larger studies using validated core temperature measurements and clinically meaningful recovery endpoints are warranted.Trial registration This study was registered in the University Hospital Medical Information Network Clinical Trial Registry of Japan (UMIN000057904).

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在55例全麻下阵发性房颤冷冻消融患者中，保温毯被动隔热使食管低温发生率由93%降至68%，术后2小时MAS≥9比例由37%升至64%。

研究动机

全麻手术中非计划性术中低体温（核心温度<36°C）常见且可预防，可影响凝血并延长麻醉恢复；在房颤冷冻消融全麻患者中，被动隔热是否改善早期复苏尚不明确。

方法

前瞻性研究，纳入55例非瓣膜性阵发性AF全麻下冷冻消融患者，分为保温毯组（n=28）与常规毛巾对照组（n=27）。用食管温度探头于冷冻消融前后测食管温度，食管低温定义为<36°C，Δ温度为麻醉前后差值。主要终点为停镇静/麻醉后2小时改良Aldrete评分（MAS）≥9的早期成功复苏。摘要未报告具体统计方法。

结果

被动隔热使食管低温发生率由93%降至68%，停麻醉后2小时MAS≥9比例由37%升至64%；摘要未报告P值、95%CI及体温下降幅度具体数值。

结论与意义

全麻下AF冷冻消融中，保温毯被动隔热与较小的食管温度下降及更高的2小时MAS≥9比例相关，可能有利于早期复苏；但样本量小、摘要未提供统计学显著性和验证性核心体温/临床结局，需更大样本验证。

Racial Differences in the Incidence and Outcomes of Postoperative Atrial Fibrillation After Isolated Coronary Artery Bypass Grafting: A Systematic Review.

J Cardiothorac Vasc Anesth·2026-09-24·PMID 42786026
房颤与消融 Journal Article Review

Asia Fields、Jasmine Vo、Adham Elsherbini 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Postoperative atrial fibrillation (POAF) is a common complication after coronary artery bypass grafting (CABG), with an estimated incidence of up to 20% to 40%. Existing evidence suggests that inequities in access to health care may impact clinical outcomes following POAF. This systematic review was conducted in compliance with Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses 2020 guidelines across the Cochrane, MEDLINE, and Embase databases to retrieve potential studies. Studies reporting racial and/or socioeconomic differences in the incidence or outcomes of new-onset POAF after isolated CABG were included. Title and abstract screening and full-text review were performed using Covidence software for systematic reviews. The risk of bias was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Tool. The search strategy retrieved 1,155 studies. After duplicate removal, 1,149 studies underwent the screening process. Eventually, 6 studies were included for data extraction. The results showed a higher reported incidence of POAF among White patients than among African American and other racial and ethnic groups. However, African American patients appear to have worse survival after developing POAF. Income, education, insurance status, and other socioeconomic or access-to-care variables were not consistently reported or adjusted for in the included studies. Racial differences can impact the incidence and clinical outcomes in patients who develop POAF after CABG. Future work could investigate the role of other socioeconomic factors.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本系统评价纳入 6 项研究，发现冠脉搭桥术后房颤（POAF）的报告发生率在白人患者中高于非裔美国人及其他族裔，但非裔美国人发生 POAF 后生存更差，提示族裔差异同时影响发生率与结局。

研究动机

POAF 是单纯冠脉搭桥（CABG）术后常见并发症，估计发生率约 20%~40%。既往证据提示医疗服务可及性方面的不公平可能影响 POAF 后的临床结局，但族裔与社会经济差异在 POAF 发生率及结局中的作用尚缺乏系统梳理，本研究即针对这一证据缺口展开。

方法

系统评价，遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南，检索 Cochrane、MEDLINE 与 Embase 数据库。纳入报告单纯 CABG 术后新发 POAF 发生率或结局的族裔和/或社会经济差异的研究。共检索到 1,155 条记录，去重后 1,149 条进入筛选，使用 Covidence 完成题录/摘要筛选与全文审阅，最终纳入 6 项研究进行数据提取，并采用 JBI 关键评价工具评估偏倚风险；摘要未报告研究设计构成、样本量汇总及定量合并方法。

结果

纳入的 6 项研究一致显示，白人患者报告的 POAF 发生率高于非裔美国人及其他种族/族裔群体；但非裔美国患者在发生 POAF 后生存结局更差。收入、教育、保险状态及其他社会经济或医疗可及性变量在纳入研究中报告不一致，且多数未作为混杂因素进行校正。摘要未报告各研究的具体发生率、效应量、95% CI 或 P 值，也未进行荟萃分析合并。

结论与意义

族裔差异可能同时影响 CABG 术后 POAF 的发生率与临床结局，提示需关注非裔患者 POAF 后的生存劣势。但本系统评价证据基础薄弱（仅 6 项研究）、缺乏定量合并，社会经济因素信息不完整，尚不能推断因果关系。未来研究应系统采集并校正收入、教育、保险与就医可及性等变量，以厘清差异背后的驱动机制。

Effects of Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease on the Inpatient Outcomes of Patients Admitted for Atrial Fibrillation: An Analysis from the National Inpatient Sample Database (NIS 2016-2019).

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783043
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Xiuhong Lyu、Bolun Liu、Yiting Li

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD), which is recognized under the updated nomenclature as Metabolic Dysfunction-Associated Steatotic Liver Disease (MASLD), is increasingly prevalent in the United States and worldwide. Emerging evidence links NAFLD to an elevated risk of atrial fibrillation (AF), yet data regarding the impact of NAFLD on inpatient outcomes among patients admitted for AF remain limited. METHODS: The National Inpatient Sample (NIS) from 2016 to 2019 was used to identify adult patients with a primary discharge diagnosis of AF (ICD-10: I48.x). Patients with concurrent NAFLD were compared to those without. Multivariable linear and logistic regression analyses were performed, adjusting for age, sex, race, insurance status, and Charlson Comorbidity Index. The primary outcome was inpatient all-cause mortality. Secondary outcomes included length of stay, inflation-adjusted hospital costs, discharge disposition, and in-hospital complications. RESULTS: Of 1,891,479 weighted AF admissions, 13,840 carried a concurrent NAFLD diagnosis. Concurrent NAFLD was associated with longer length of stay (3.99 vs. 3.35 days, p < 0.001) (which is statistically significant though might be clinically insignificant) and higher hospital costs ($13,841.65 vs. $12,154.55, p = 0.046). No significant difference in inpatient mortality was observed. CONCLUSIONS: Concurrent NAFLD among AF hospitalizations is associated with greater resource utilization without a significant mortality difference, highlighting the importance of addressing this comorbidity to reduce the economic burden of AF-related hospitalizations.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在因房颤住院的成人患者中，合并 NAFLD 与住院时间延长和住院费用增加相关，但未观察到院内死亡率差异。

研究动机

NAFLD（新命名 MASLD）在美國及全球的患病率持续上升，越来越多证据提示其与 AF 风险升高相关；然而，NAFLD 对因 AF 住院患者院内结局（死亡、资源占用、并发症）的影响，既往证据仍有限，缺乏基于全国性大样本住院数据库的数据。

方法

基于美国全国住院患者样本（NIS）2016—2019 年的回顾性队列研究。纳入主要出院诊断为 AF（ICD-10：I48.x）的成人患者，按是否合并 NAFLD 分为两组。采用多因素线性与 logistic 回归，校正年龄、性别、种族、保险状态及 Charlson 合并症指数。主要终点为院内全因死亡；次要终点包括住院时间、经通胀调整的住院费用、出院去向及院内并发症。加权后共 1,891,479 例 AF 住院，其中 13,840 例合并 NAFLD。

结果

合并 NAFLD 组住院时间更长（3.99 天 vs 3.35 天，p<0.001，作者提示统计学显著但临床意义可能有限），住院费用更高（13,841.65 美元 vs 12,154.55 美元，p=0.046）。院内死亡率未见显著差异。出院去向及院内并发症的具体结果，摘要未报告。

结论与意义

在因 AF 住院的患者中，合并 NAFLD 主要体现为医疗资源占用增加（住院时间与费用上升），并未增加院内死亡风险。这提示临床应对该共病进行识别与管理，以减轻 AF 住院的经济负担；但本研究为行政数据库的回顾性分析，NAFLD 依赖 ICD 编码识别、可能存在漏诊与残余混杂，且缺乏消融治疗、节律控制策略及随访结局等信息，结论有待前瞻性研究验证。

The Price of Redo Mitral Valve Replacement: Determinants of Major Adverse Postoperative Events.

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783038
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Barış Timur、Zinar Apaydın

英文摘要

Background: Redo mitral valve replacement is a high-risk operation in which early mortality alone may underestimate postoperative burden. We evaluated whether concomitant tricuspid valve repair independently increases major adverse postoperative events after redo mitral valve replacement. Methods: Consecutive patients undergoing redo mitral valve replacement between 2019 and 2025 were retrospectively reviewed. Isolated redo mitral valve replacement was compared with redo mitral valve replacement plus concomitant tricuspid valve repair. The primary endpoint was major adverse postoperative events, defined as early mortality, acute kidney injury, cerebrovascular event, prolonged ventilation, reoperation, deep sternal infection, mechanical circulatory support, or permanent pacemaker implantation. Results: The final cohort included 249 patients: 134 underwent isolated redo mitral valve replacement and 115 underwent concomitant tricuspid valve repair. Patients receiving tricuspid valve repair were older, had higher pulmonary artery pressure, and had a longer interval from the previous operation. They also had longer ventilation, longer intensive care unit stay and higher rates of atrial fibrillation, atrioventricular block, and permanent pacemaker implantation. Early mortality was similar between groups. Major adverse postoperative events occurred in 122 patients. Concomitant tricuspid valve repair was not associated with major adverse postoperative events in univariable analysis. In multivariable analysis, concomitant tricuspid valve repair was not independently associated with MAPE. Increasing age, smoking, lower left ventricular ejection fraction, and longer cardiopulmonary bypass time remained independently associated with MAPE. Conclusions: Early major postoperative morbidity after redo mitral valve replacement was associated primarily with patient-related risk factors and operative complexity. Concomitant tricuspid valve repair was not independently associated with MAPE after adjustment for the available covariates.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 249 例再次二尖瓣置换患者中，合并三尖瓣修补并未独立增加主要术后不良事件（MAPE），MAPE 主要由年龄、吸烟、低 LVEF 和体外循环时间决定。

研究动机

再次二尖瓣置换属于高危手术，仅用早期死亡率评估会低估术后负担；既往证据不清之处在于，同期行三尖瓣修补是否会独立增加术后并发症，从而影响手术决策与风险分层。

方法

单中心回顾性队列研究，连续纳入 2019–2025 年接受再次二尖瓣置换的患者 249 例，其中单纯再次二尖瓣置换 134 例、同期合并三尖瓣修补 115 例。主要终点 MAPE 为早期死亡、急性肾损伤、脑血管事件、延长机械通气、再手术、深部胸骨感染、机械循环支持或永久起搏器植入的复合终点；采用单因素及多因素分析校正可得协变量。

结果

合并三尖瓣修补组年龄更大、肺动脉压更高、距前次手术间隔更长，机械通气与 ICU 停留时间更长，房颤、房室传导阻滞及永久起搏器植入率更高；两组早期死亡率相近。全队列 122 例发生 MAPE。单因素分析中三尖瓣修补与 MAPE 无关联，多因素校正后仍未显示独立关联；年龄增加、吸烟、LVEF 较低及体外循环时间较长与 MAPE 独立相关。摘要未报告具体效应量、95%CI 与 P 值。

结论与意义

再次二尖瓣置换术后早期并发症负担主要取决于患者自身危险因素与手术复杂程度，同期三尖瓣修补在现有协变量校正后并非 MAPE 的独立预测因素，因此不应仅因担心并发症而回避必要的三尖瓣干预。该结论受回顾性设计与单中心样本限制，且未报告效应量，需前瞻性或更大样本验证；术后房颤、房室传导阻滞与起搏器植入风险升高值得电生理随访关注。

Effect of Left Atrial Appendage Closure on Cardiac Function.

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783032
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Qi Wang、Xin Li、Qi Zou 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most common clinically significant cardiac arrhythmia with a marked age-related increase in prevalence. Current guidelines recommend left atrial appendage closure (LAAC) as an alternative to long-term oral anticoagulation in selected patients at high-risk of both thromboembolic and bleeding events. Although the use of LAAC has increased substantially in recent years, its effects on cardiac function and post-procedural functional recovery remain incompletely understood. Therefore, this study aimed to evaluate changes in periprocedural cardiac function and functional parameters in patients with AF who underwent LAAC while receiving guideline-directed medical therapy. A retrospective cohort study was performed in 250 patients who underwent percutaneous LAAC at Lanzhou University Second Hospital between May 2019 and July 2025. A total of 162 patients were included in the final analysis. Echocardiographic parameters, NYHA functional class, and Barthel Index scores were compared before and after the procedure. Significant improvements were observed in several echocardiographic parameters. Post-procedural changes in NYHA functional class distribution and Barthel Index scores indicated improved functional status, with no evidence of deterioration in cardiac structure or function. These findings support the safety of LAAC with respect to cardiac functional recovery and demonstrate that the procedure does not adversely affect cardiac performance in patients with AF receiving guideline-directed medical therapy. Nevertheless, further large-scale, multi-center prospective studies are warranted to clarify the mechanisms underlying the observed improvements in cardiac function following LAAC.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在162例接受经皮左心耳封堵的AF患者中，围术期超声心动图参数、NYHA心功能分级分布及Barthel指数均改善，未见心脏结构或功能恶化。

研究动机

AF是最常见的具有临床意义的心律失常，患病率随年龄明显上升。指南推荐LAAC作为高血栓栓塞风险且高出血风险的部分患者长期口服抗凝的替代方案；尽管LAAC应用近年显著增加，但其对心脏功能及术后功能恢复的影响仍不明确。本研究旨在评估接受指南导向药物治疗的AF患者行LAAC前后围术期心脏功能与功能学参数的变化。

方法

单中心回顾性队列研究，纳入2019年5月至2025年7月在兰州大学第二医院接受经皮LAAC的250例患者，最终162例进入分析。干预为经皮LAAC，患者同时接受指南导向药物治疗。比较术前与术后的超声心动图参数、NYHA心功能分级及Barthel指数评分。主要终点与次要终点的具体定义及统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

术后多个超声心动图参数较术前改善；NYHA心功能分级分布及Barthel指数评分提示功能状态改善，未发现心脏结构或功能恶化的证据。摘要未报告各参数的效应量、95%置信区间及P值，亦未报告随访时间与超声参数的具体数值。

结论与意义

结果支持LAAC在心脏功能恢复方面具有安全性，并提示在接受指南导向药物治疗的AF患者中该操作不产生不良心脏功能影响。但本研究为单中心回顾性设计，样本从250例降至162例，随访时点及统计细节缺失，存在选择偏倚与残余混杂可能；仍需大规模多中心前瞻性研究明确LAAC后心脏功能改善的机制。

Long-Term Clinical Outcomes After Procedural Hemopericardium Requiring Pericardiocentesis During Atrial Fibrillation Ablation.

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783014
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Soohyun Kim、Soyoon Park、Hwajung Kim 等 6 位作者

英文摘要

Hemopericardium is a rare but serious complication of radiofrequency catheter ablation (RFCA) for atrial fibrillation (AF). While prompt pericardial drainage is effective, the long-term clinical course and the role of adjunctive anti-inflammatory therapy remain unclear. In this prospective single-center cohort study, we included patients who developed hemopericardium requiring pericardiocentesis during RFCA for AF. All patients underwent immediate percutaneous drainage. Colchicine (0.6 mg twice daily for 14 days) was prescribed at the operator's discretion. The primary outcome was freedom from AF or atrial tachycardia (AT) at 12 months after a 3-month blanking period. Among 2133 patients undergoing RFCA, 75 (3.5%) developed hemopericardium. Of these, 21 received colchicine and 54 received usual care. During a median follow-up of 367 days, freedom from AF/AT did not differ between groups (76.2% vs. 74.1%, log-rank p = 0.93). Residual pericardial effusion was infrequent and resolved in all patients by 3 months. Although colchicine was not associated with a reduction in atrial arrhythmia recurrence, patients receiving colchicine had lower CRP levels at 3 months than those receiving usual care (p = 0.021). However, treatment-limiting adverse events occurred in 6 of 21 patients (28.6%) receiving colchicine. No cases of constrictive pericarditis were observed. In patients with procedural hemopericardium during AF ablation, prompt pericardial drainage was associated with favorable clinical outcomes and absence of long-term pericardial sequelae. Adjunctive colchicine therapy was not associated with a significant reduction in arrhythmia recurrence; however, given the small sample size and limited number of events, a clinically meaningful treatment effect cannot be excluded.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

房颤射频消融术中发生需心包穿刺的心脏压塞性血心包者（75/2133，3.5%），及时引流后12个月无AF/AT复发率约75%，长期无心包缩窄等后遗症；辅助秋水仙碱未降低复发，但可降低3个月CRP，且不良反应达28.6%。

研究动机

血心包是房颤射频导管消融（RFCA）罕见但严重的并发症，及时心包穿刺引流通常有效，但此类患者远期心律失常复发情况、残留心包积液与缩窄性心包炎的转归，以及辅助抗炎治疗（秋水仙碱）是否获益，既往证据均不明确。

方法

前瞻性单中心队列研究。纳入RFCA治疗房颤过程中发生血心包并需心包穿刺的患者，全部即刻行经皮心包引流；秋水仙碱（0.6 mg，每日两次，共14天）由术者酌情决定，据此分为秋水仙碱组与常规治疗组。主要终点为3个月空白期后12个月无房颤/房性心动过速（AF/AT）复发；同时观察残留心包积液、CRP水平、药物不良反应及缩窄性心包炎发生情况。统计方法包括组间比较与log-rank检验，摘要未报告具体回归模型。

结果

2133例接受RFCA的患者中75例（3.5%）发生血心包，其中21例接受秋水仙碱、54例常规治疗。中位随访367天，12个月无AF/AT复发率两组无差异（76.2% vs 74.1%，log-rank p=0.93）。残留心包积液少见，全部患者在3个月内吸收消退，未见缩窄性心包炎病例。秋水仙碱组3个月CRP水平低于常规治疗组（p=0.021），但21例中6例（28.6%）出现导致治疗中止的不良事件。

结论与意义

房颤消融术中血心包经及时心包引流后远期临床转归良好，无长期心包后遗症。辅助秋水仙碱未显著减少房性心律失常复发，但可降低炎症指标且不良反应发生率较高；鉴于样本量小、事件数有限，不能排除具有临床意义的治疗效应，需更大样本研究验证。

Demographic Profile of Cardiac Resynchronization Therapy Recipients in a Saudi Tertiary Center: A Descriptive Single-Center Study.

J Cardiovasc Echogr·2026-09-24·PMID 42781648
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Alhanouf Muneer Alotaibi、Jamilah S AlRahimi、Amjad Amjed SaemalDahar

英文摘要

OBJECTIVE: Women remain underrepresented in landmark cardiac resynchronization therapy (CRT) trials, despite evidence suggesting they may derive substantial clinical benefit. Age at implantation also influences outcomes and long-term management. However, demographic and clinical data on CRT recipients in Saudi Arabia remain limited. This study aimed to describe the sex distribution and age profile of CRT recipients in a tertiary Saudi center and to contextualize these findings within international CRT trial and registry data. MATERIALS AND METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed consecutive CRT implantations performed between January 2017 and December 2021 at a tertiary cardiac center in Jeddah, Saudi Arabia. Demographic and clinical variables, including age and sex, underlying cardiomyopathy, New York Heart Association (NYHA) class, conduction morphology, atrial fibrillation, and baseline echocardiographic parameters, were collected. Descriptive statistics were summarized and compared narratively with published data from major CRT randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and large international registries. RESULTS: Among 53 CRT recipients, 18 (34.0%) were women (95% confidence interval [CI]: 22.7-47.4). Mean age was 65.5 ± 10.5 years (95% CI: 62.6-68.4). Ischemic cardiomyopathy accounted for 54.7% of cases, left bundle branch block morphology was present in 69.8%, and the mean left ventricular ejection fraction was 27% ±6%. Most patients (67.9%) were in NYHA Class III. Female proportion was broadly similar to that reported in large international registries (≈30%-36%) and higher than landmark RCTs (≈20%-25%). Age distribution was comparable to global CRT cohorts. Most patients received CRT-D devices (94.3%). CONCLUSION: CRT recipients in this Saudi cohort exhibited demographic and clinical characteristics comparable to international real-world CRT populations. These findings provide much-needed foundational data on CRT utilization in Saudi Arabia and highlight the importance of establishing multicenter national CRT registries to better characterize access patterns and identify potential sex- or age-related disparities in advanced heart failure therapy.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

沙特吉达单中心 2017–2021 年 53 例 CRT 植入者中女性占 34.0%，平均年龄 65.5 岁，人口学与临床特征与国际真实世界 CRT 人群一致，女性比例高于既往里程碑 RCT。

研究动机

女性在 CRT 里程碑随机对照试验中长期被低估入组，尽管已有证据提示女性可能从 CRT 获得更大临床获益；植入年龄同样影响疗效与长期管理。沙特阿拉伯乃至海湾地区缺乏 CRT 受者的性别与年龄分布数据，无法评估当地晚期心力衰竭器械治疗的可及性与是否存在性别或年龄相关差异，本研究旨在填补这一基础数据缺口。

方法

单中心回顾性描述性研究。连续纳入 2017 年 1 月至 2021 年 12 月在沙特吉达某三级心脏中心接受 CRT 植入的全部患者，共 53 例。收集人口学与临床变量，包括年龄、性别、心肌病病因、NYHA 心功能分级、传导阻滞形态、合并心房颤动及基线超声心动图参数（如 LVEF）。采用描述性统计（比例及 95%CI、均数±标准差及 95%CI）汇总，并与已发表的 CRT 大型随机对照试验及国际注册研究数据进行叙述性比较；摘要未报告正式假设检验或回归分析。

结果

53 例受者中女性 18 例（34.0%，95%CI 22.7–47.4）；平均年龄 65.5±10.5 岁（95%CI 62.6–68.4）。缺血性心肌病占 54.7%，左束支传导阻滞形态占 69.8%，平均 LVEF 27%±6%，NYHA III 级占 67.9%，94.3% 植入 CRT-D。女性比例与大型国际注册研究（约 30%–36%）大致相当，高于里程碑 RCT（约 20%–25%）；年龄分布与全球 CRT 队列相近。摘要未报告与其他中心比较的统计学检验结果。

结论与意义

该沙特队列 CRT 受者的人口学与临床特征与国际真实世界 CRT 人群基本一致，提示当地 CRT 适用人群构成与全球相似。研究提供了沙特 CRT 应用的首批基础数据，但样本量小（53 例）、单中心、回顾性且缺乏对照与随访结局，无法评估 CRT 反应率、生存获益或真实的性别/年龄差异。作者据此强调应建立全国多中心 CRT 注册登记，以刻画器械可及性路径并识别晚期心力衰竭治疗中潜在的性别或年龄相关差异。

High Incidence of Short-term Subsequent Atrial Fibrillation Following Restrictive Filling Mitral Flow Profile in Patients without Structural Heart Disease.

J Cardiovasc Echogr·2026-09-24·PMID 42781162
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Tomoo Nagai、Hitomi Horinouchi、Yuji Ikari

英文摘要

PURPOSE: This study investigated the implications of a restrictive filling (RF) mitral flow profile on the frequency of short-term subsequent atrial fibrillation (AF) in cases without thrombotic conditions during routine transthoracic echocardiography (TTE). METHODS: A TTE database was retrospectively analyzed from 2015 to 2023 (n = 123,210). We enrolled middle-aged and older adults (≥50 years) with RF mitral flow (the ratio of the early to atrial mitral inflow peak velocities [E/A ratio] ≥2), normal sinus rhythm during the TTE study, and a large left atrial (LA) dimension (≥40 mm) excluding those with structural heart disease and depressed left ventricular ejection fraction. Ad hoc analyses of LA emptying fraction (LAEF) and LA longitudinal strain during the reservoir phase (LASr) were performed. We calculated the ratio of LASr to LAEF (LASr/LAEF) for the discrepancy between LAEF and LASr. RESULTS: The cohort comprised 163 cases (0.13%, 163/123,210) recruited from 3788 cases with RF mitral flow profile (3.1%, 3788/123,210). Subsequent AF was frequently confirmed in this cohort (14.7%, 24/163). LAEF ≤25.9% was selected as a predictor (P = 0.023, odds ratio: 5.547) in a logistic model, while LASr/LAEF ≥0.65 was a predictor in another model (P = 0.020, OR: 3.68). CONCLUSIONS: The RF mitral flow profile is rare but may indicate short-term subsequent AF. Among TTE parameters, low LAEF and increased LASr/LAEF were the predictors. Atrial stunning might play a significant role.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在无结构性心脏病、E/A≥2 且左房增大的中老年人群中，限制性充盈型二尖瓣血流提示短期内新发房颤风险升高（14.7%），低 LAEF 与 LASr/LAEF 升高是独立预测因子。

研究动机

限制性充盈（RF）型二尖瓣血流通常被视为左室充盈压升高的标志，但既往证据多集中于结构性心脏病或心衰人群；在无结构性心脏病、左室射血分数正常者中，该血流频谱是否提示心房肌受累及短期新发房颤风险，尚缺乏大样本数据，临床对此类 TTE 偶然发现的处置亦无明确依据。

方法

单中心回顾性研究，分析 2015—2023 年经胸超声心动图（TTE）数据库共 123,210 例。其中 3,788 例（3.1%）存在限制性充盈型二尖瓣血流（E/A≥2），最终纳入 ≥50 岁、TTE 时为正常窦性心律、左房内径 ≥40 mm，并排除结构性心脏病与左室射血分数降低者，共 163 例（0.13%）。附加分析左房排空分数（LAEF）与左房储器期纵向应变（LASr），并计算 LASr/LAEF 比值。终点为短期随访新发房颤，采用 logistic 回归筛选预测因子；摘要未报告随访时长、房颤监测方式及模型校正变量。

结果

队列共 163 例，中位随访期内 24 例（14.7%）确认新发房颤。logistic 模型中，LAEF ≤25.9% 为预测因子（P=0.023，OR 5.547，摘要未报告 95%CI）；另一模型中 LASr/LAEF ≥0.65 亦为预测因子（P=0.020，OR 3.68，摘要未报告 95%CI）。RF 型血流在整体 TTE 数据库中占比 3.1%，符合全部纳入条件者仅 0.13%。

结论与意义

限制性充盈型二尖瓣血流在无结构性心脏病人群中虽少见，但提示短期继发房颤风险不容忽视；低 LAEF 与 LASr/LAEF 升高（反映左房排空与储器应变之间的不匹配）为预测指标，提示心房顿抑可能参与其中。对 TTE 发现 E/A≥2 且左房增大者，应加强房颤筛查与随访。局限性：单中心回顾性设计、样本量小（163 例）、事件数少（24 例）、缺乏外部验证，随访时间与房颤检出方式未在摘要中报告，OR 的置信区间亦未提供。

Atrioventricular block and optimal synchronous pacing on ECG in a single lead VDD pacing population

J Electrocardiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42800391
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Mascia G、Barca L、Minghini A 等 11 位作者

英文摘要

Introduction In patients with atrioventricular (AV) block and preserved sinus node function on 12‑leads electrocardiogram (ECG) a single‑lead VDD pacing is today an established alternative to dual-chamber pacing, potentially offering reduced complications. Optimal pacing in AV block should maintain the AV synchrony on ECG in order to maximize cardiac output as well as stroke volume. Therefore in single‑lead VDD pacing population a proper planning of the AV interval is critical, as it allows the atrial contraction to complete before ventricular activation begins.Methods and results We enrolled 67 consecutive patients with complete AV block and preserved sinus node function, with a single‑lead VDD pacemaker indication. According to atrial dipole length patients were divided into a long-dipole group (>20 mm, n = 25) and a short-dipole group (≤20 mm, n = 26), while 16 patients were excluded (8 due to loss at follow-up, 8 for clinical non-eligibility considering atrial fibrillation and/or documented sinus node dysfunction). The mean atrial sensing amplitude was 0.60 ± 0.56 mV in the long-dipole group versus 1.16 ± 0.92 mV in the short-dipole group (p = 0.02) at 6-months follow-up. AV synchrony was significantly higher with long dipoles (95.4 ± 4.6%) compared with short dipoles (84.6 ± 25.5%; p = 0.04) at same follow-up.Conclusions In a single‑lead VDD pacing population, a longer atrial dipole is associated with a superior AV synchrony on ECG despite lower atrial sensing, supporting the concept that detection stability and electro-geometric consistency may prevail over absolute signal amplitude in preserving AV synchrony.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在单导线 VDD 起搏的完全性房室阻滞患者中，心房双极长度>20 mm 者 6 个月随访时心电图 AV 同步性显著优于≤20 mm 者（95.4% vs 84.6%），尽管其心房感知振幅反而更低。

研究动机

对于完全性 AV 阻滞而窦房结功能保留的患者，单导线 VDD 起搏因其并发症少于双腔起搏而成为既定替代方案。AV 阻滞患者起搏的关键在于维持心电图上 AV 同步，从而最大化心输出量与每搏输出量；因此 AV 间期规划至关重要，需保证心房收缩在心室激动前完成。既往证据未明确单导线 VDD 系统中决定 AV 同步性的器械因素（尤其心房电极长度/感知特性），这一缺口是本研究的出发点。

方法

连续性入组 67 例具有单导线 VDD 起搏适应证的完全性 AV 阻滞、窦房结功能保留患者；按心房电极（dipole）长度分为长电极组（>20 mm，n=25）与短电极组（≤20 mm，n=26），16 例被排除（8 例失访，8 例因心房颤动和/或证实窦房结功能不全而不符合条件），最终 51 例可供分析。观察指标包括 6 个月随访时的心房感知振幅与心电图 AV 同步性，两组间进行比较；摘要未报告随机化、盲法、具体终点定义细则及所采用的统计检验方法。

结果

6 个月随访时，长电极组平均心房感知振幅为 0.60±0.56 mV，显著低于短电极组的 1.16±0.92 mV（p=0.02）；而同一随访点长电极组的心电图 AV 同步性显著更高，为 95.4±4.6%，短电极组为 84.6±25.5%（p=0.04）。摘要未报告 AV 间期程控参数、心室起搏比例、心功能或生活质量等临床终点的组间比较及效应量的 95% 置信区间。

结论与意义

在单导线 VDD 起搏人群中，较长的感知电极与更优的心电图 AV 同步性相关，即使其心房感知振幅更低，提示在维持 AV 同步方面，感知信号的稳定性与电极—心房电几何一致性可能比绝对信号振幅更为重要；这为 AV 阻滞患者选择 VDD 导线提供了器械层面的参考。局限性包括样本量小、按电极长度非随机分组、随访仅 6 个月、缺乏硬临床终点，且结论尚需更大规模前瞻性研究验证。

4D Flow MRI Characterization of Left Atrial Vortical Flow in Atrial Fibrillation Following Catheter Ablation.

J Imaging·2026-09-24·PMID 42783865
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Omar Hassan、Hadi Hassan、Shuvam Prasai 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

Atrial fibrillation (AF) is associated with abnormal left atrial (LA) blood flow patterns that contribute to thromboembolic risk. While blood flow stasis in AF has been extensively studied, the effects of AF and pulmonary vein isolation (PVI) on LA vortical flow remain incompletely understood. This study used four-dimensional flow magnetic resonance imaging to quantify LA vorticity and vortex volume in 107 patients with AF before PVI, 56 patients after PVI, and 19 non-AF controls. Vortex structures were identified using the Lambda2 method, and group differences were assessed after adjustment for age and sex. Pre-ablation patients demonstrated significantly lower LA vorticity than both post-ablation patients and controls, whereas post-ablation patients did not differ from controls. In contrast, mean and maximum LA vortex volumes remained elevated in both AF groups relative to controls, with no significant differences between pre- and post-ablation patients. These findings indicate that PVI is associated with improvements in LA vorticity but not vortex volume. The persistence of enlarged LA vortices despite normalization of vorticity suggests that these metrics capture distinct aspects of atrial remodeling and may provide complementary insight into AF-related pathophysiology.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

4D Flow MRI 显示，房颤患者 PVI 前左房涡旋强度（涡度）显著低于 PVI 后患者与对照，PVI 后涡度恢复正常；但平均与最大左房涡旋容积在 PVI 前后均持续高于对照，未被 PVI 逆转。

研究动机

房颤与左房血流模式异常相关，是血栓栓塞风险的重要机制。既往研究多聚焦血流淤滞（stasis），而房颤本身及肺静脉隔离（PVI）对左房涡旋（vortical）血流的影响尚不明确，缺乏对涡度与涡旋容积的定量刻画，也缺乏 PVI 前后与无房颤对照的对比证据。

方法

单中心影像学研究，采用四维血流磁共振（4D Flow MRI）定量左房涡度（vorticity）与涡旋容积（vortex volume）。研究对象共 182 例：PVI 前房颤患者 107 例、PVI 后房颤患者 56 例、非房颤对照 19 例。涡旋结构以 Lambda2 方法识别，组间比较在校正年龄与性别后进行。主要观察指标为左房涡度与涡旋容积（平均及最大）的组间差异。具体随访时点、扫描参数及统计模型细节摘要未报告。

结果

PVI 前患者的左房涡度显著低于 PVI 后患者及对照组，而 PVI 后患者与对照之间无显著差异。相反，平均与最大左房涡旋容积在两个房颤组中均较对照升高，且 PVI 前与 PVI 后两组之间无显著差异。摘要未报告具体效应量、95% 置信区间及 P 值。

结论与意义

PVI 与左房涡度的改善相关，但未伴随涡旋容积的下降；涡度正常化而涡旋容积持续增大，提示两者反映心房重构的不同层面，可作为互补指标用于理解房颤相关病理生理。临床意义在于提示节律控制/消融后血流力学改善并不完全，残余涡旋扩大可能仍与血栓栓塞风险相关。局限性：样本量不均（对照仅 19 例）、横断面设计无法确立因果与时间顺序、缺乏临床终点（卒中/血栓）关联、摘要未提供具体数值与随访信息。
