Association of Early-Onset Atrial Fibrillation and Estimated Atrial Fibrillation Burden with Mortality in Critically Ill Adults: A Landmark Cohort Study.

J Intensive Care Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42782161
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Anar Mammadli、Ayse Irem Demirtola、Onur Yildirim 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

BackgroundNew-onset atrial fibrillation (AF) occurs in 5%-15% of non-cardiac-surgery intensive care unit (ICU) patients and is associated with increased mortality. Most studies classify AF over the entire ICU stay, introducing immortal time bias, and treat AF as binary, ignoring dose-response relationships.AimsTo estimate associations of early-onset AF and estimated AF burden with mortality using a 48-h landmark design.MethodsWe retrospectively analyzed Medical Information Mart for Intensive Care IV adults with at least 48 h of ICU observation whose first informative rhythm record indicated sinus rhythm, excluding cardiac-surgery patients. Early-onset AF and cumulative AF duration were estimated from intermittently charted rhythm records during the first 48 h; follow-up began at the landmark. Cox and logistic regression estimated adjusted associations with 28-day, 1-year, and in-hospital mortality.ResultsAmong 20,925 landmark survivors, 1650 (7.9%) had early-onset AF. After adjustment, early-onset AF was associated with 28-day (HR 1.22, 95% CI 1.10-1.36), 1-year (HR 1.12, 95% CI 1.03-1.22), and in-hospital mortality (OR 1.50, 95% CI 1.31-1.72). Proportional hazards were not met for 28-day mortality: the HR was 1.32 (95% CI 1.17-1.48) during days 0-14 after the landmark and 0.90 (95% CI 0.70-1.16) during days 15-28. Among AF-positive patients, each approximate doubling of estimated AF duration was associated with an 8% higher hazard of 28-day mortality (HR 1.08, 95% CI 1.01-1.16).ConclusionsAmong ICU patients who remained under observation and survived to a 48-h landmark, early-onset AF was associated with higher subsequent mortality, and longer estimated AF duration was modestly associated with mortality among AF-positive patients. These observational findings do not establish benefit from AF-directed treatment or reducing AF duration.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 MIMIC-IV 中经历 48 小时 landmark（标志点）且仍存活的 20,925 例 ICU 患者中，7.9% 出现早发房颤；早发房颤与 28 天、1 年及住院死亡率升高相关，且房颤持续时间越长，28 天死亡风险越高。

研究动机

非心脏手术 ICU 患者中新发房颤发生率约 5%-15%，且与死亡率升高相关。既往研究多以整个 ICU 住院期间的房颤作为分类依据，容易引入不死时间偏倚（immortal time bias）；同时，这些研究把房颤简单处理为二分类暴露，忽略了房颤负荷与死亡风险之间可能存在的剂量-反应关系。本研究采用 48 小时 landmark 设计，同时评估早发房颤和估测房颤负荷与死亡率的关联。

方法

本研究为回顾性队列研究，数据来自 MIMIC-IV 数据库。纳入标准：成人、ICU 观察时间≥48 小时、首条可判读的心律记录为窦性心律；排除心脏外科手术患者。以 48 小时为 landmark，根据前 48 小时内间歇记录的节律资料估测早发房颤及累计房颤时长；随访自 landmark 起算。采用 Cox 回归和 logistic 回归，估计校正后与 28 天、1 年及住院死亡率的关联。

结果

共有 20,925 例患者通过 48 小时 landmark 且仍存活，其中 1,650 例（7.9%）存在早发房颤。校正后，早发房颤与 28 天死亡（HR 1.22，95%CI 1.10-1.36）、1 年死亡（HR 1.12，95%CI 1.03-1.22）及住院死亡（OR 1.50，95%CI 1.31-1.72）相关。28 天死亡率不满足比例风险假定：landmark 后 0-14 天 HR 为 1.32（95%CI 1.17-1.48），15-28 天 HR 为 0.90（95%CI 0.70-1.16）。在房颤阳性患者中，估测房颤时长每约翻倍，28 天死亡风险升高 8%（HR 1.08，95%CI 1.01-1.16）。

结论与意义

在 ICU 中持续观察并存活至 48 小时 landmark 的患者里，早发房颤与后续死亡率升高相关；在房颤阳性患者中，估测房颤持续时间与死亡率呈轻度正相关，提示可能存在剂量-反应关系。上述结果均为观察性关联，不能证明针对房颤的治疗或缩短房颤时长可带来获益；房颤负荷基于间歇记录的节律资料估测，存在测量误差和残余混杂，需前瞻性研究验证。

Abnormal Cardiac PET Imaging in ATTR Amyloidosis.

JACC Case Rep·2026-09-24·PMID 42782031
房颤与消融 Case Reports Journal Article

Mihir Shah、Jacob Agronin、Jared Hassler 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Transthyretin amyloidosis (ATTR) is an infiltrative cardiomyopathy caused by deposition of misfolded transthyretin protein in the myocardium. Diagnosis typically relies on technetium-99m-based imaging or magnetic resonance imaging. CASE SUMMARY: A 72-year-old man presented for evaluation of multifocal premature ventricular contractions and nonsustained ventricular tachycardia after atrial fibrillation ablation. Cardiac positron emission tomography (PET) was highly suggestive of cardiac sarcoidosis. However, voltage-guided endomyocardial biopsy revealed a tissue diagnosis of ATTR. DISCUSSION: Although PET typically identifies inflammatory disease, this case suggests that fluorodeoxyglucose uptake may also occur in ATTR. TAKE-HOME MESSAGES: Diagnosis of ATTR often requires multimodality imaging to identify and differentiate from other infiltrative cardiomyopathies. Cardiac PET, although not classically used for this application, may have utility in the diagnostic pathway.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

72岁男性房颤消融后出现多灶性室早与非持续性室速，心脏PET高度提示心脏结节病，但电压指导心内膜心肌活检确诊为ATTR淀粉样变，提示FDG摄取亦可见于ATTR。

研究动机

转甲状腺素蛋白淀粉样变（ATTR）为错误折叠的转甲状腺素蛋白沉积于心肌所致的浸润性心肌病，诊断通常依赖锝-99m标记显像或心脏磁共振。心脏PET的氟脱氧葡萄糖（FDG）摄取一般被用于识别炎症性疾病（如心脏结节病），其在ATTR中的表现认识不足，可能导致误诊并延误针对性的治疗决策。

方法

单例病例报告。对象为72岁男性，因心房颤动消融术后评估多灶性室性早搏与非持续性室性心动过速就诊。所行检查包括心脏正电子发射断层显像（PET），随后行电压指导的心内膜心肌活检以获取组织学诊断。摘要未报告样本量、终点定义及统计方法（单个病例，无组间比较与统计检验）。

结果

心脏PET结果高度提示心脏结节病；但电压指导的心内膜心肌活检获得组织学诊断，证实为ATTR淀粉样变。摘要未报告PET的具体定量参数（如最大标准化摄取值）、活检取材部位与数量、随访时间及临床转归。

结论与意义

ATTR的诊断常需多模态影像联合应用以识别并与其他浸润性心肌病相鉴别；心脏PET虽非该病的经典检查手段，但本病例提示FDG摄取也可出现于ATTR，因而可能进入其诊断流程。对拟诊心脏结节病但临床背景不典型者，应考虑组织学确诊。局限性为单例报告，缺乏可推广的敏感性与特异性数据，尚需队列研究验证。

Stellate Ganglion Block Reduces the Incidence of Postoperative Atrial Fibrillation After Coronary Artery Bypass Grafting.

JACC Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42782230
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Shunping Tian、Chao Chen、Wei Liang 等 11 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Postoperative atrial fibrillation (POAF) is associated with sympathetic overactivity. Stellate ganglion is a potential target for intervention. OBJECTIVES: The aim of this study was to examine the efficacy of preemptive left stellate ganglion block (SGB) in reducing POAF in patients undergoing coronary artery bypass grafting with cardiopulmonary bypass. METHODS: Patients were randomized to the SGB group or the control group. The primary endpoint was the incidence of POAF within 5 days postoperatively. Secondary endpoints included POAF-associated characteristics and recovery indicators. RESULTS: A total of 124 patients underwent initial randomization, with 108 in modified intention-to-treat and 98 in per-protocol analysis. In modified intention-to-treat analysis, POAF occurred in 18.5% of the SGB group and 44.4% of the control group (OR: 0.288; 95% CI: 0.106-0.731; P = 0.007). Per-protocol analysis yielded consistent results. SGB group showed significantly lower POAF burden, shorter duration of first POAF episode, fewer POAF episodes and antiarrhythmic treatments, decreased C-reactive protein, interleukin-6, high-sensitivity cardiac troponin T, and creatine kinase-MB on certain postoperative time points, and decreased postoperative hospitalization than the control group (all P < 0.05). Exploratory subgroup analyses suggested that the association between SGB pretreatment and lower POAF incidence was more pronounced among patients without diabetes (OR: 0.10; 95% CI: 0.019-0.480; P = 0.004) and those at lower baseline risk of POAF (OR: 0.10; 95% CI: 0.005-0.642; P = 0.040). CONCLUSIONS: In patients undergoing coronary artery bypass grafting with cardiopulmonary bypass, preemptive left SGB was associated with a reduced incidence of POAF within 5 postoperative days. Exploratory subgroup analyses suggested a potentially greater association among patients without diabetes and those at lower risk of POAF.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在接受体外循环冠状动脉旁路移植术的患者中，术前预防性行左侧星状神经节阻滞可将术后5天内新发房颤的发生率从44.4%降至18.5%（OR 0.288，95%CI 0.106-0.731，P=0.007）。

研究动机

术后房颤（POAF）是冠脉搭桥术后常见并发症，既往证据提示交感神经过度激活在其发生中起重要作用，而星状神经节是可供干预的交感神经靶点；但术前预防性左侧星状神经节阻滞（SGB）能否降低体外循环冠脉搭桥患者的POAF发生率尚缺乏随机对照证据。

方法

单中心随机对照研究。研究对象为接受体外循环冠状动脉旁路移植术的患者，随机分为SGB组与对照组。主要终点为术后5天内POAF发生率；次要终点包括POAF负荷、首次发作持续时间、发作次数、抗心律失常治疗次数、炎症与心肌损伤标志物（CRP、IL-6、hs-cTnT、CK-MB）及术后住院时间。共124例进入初始随机，改良意向性治疗（mITT）分析纳入108例，符合方案分析纳入98例；并进行了探索性亚组分析。

结果

mITT分析中，SGB组POAF发生率为18.5%，对照组为44.4%（OR 0.288；95%CI 0.106-0.731；P=0.007），符合方案分析结果一致。SGB组POAF负荷更低、首次发作持续时间更短、发作次数及抗心律失常治疗更少，部分术后时间点CRP、IL-6、hs-cTnT与CK-MB降低，术后住院时间缩短（均P<0.05）。探索性亚组提示，在无糖尿病患者（OR 0.10；95%CI 0.019-0.480；P=0.004）及基线POAF风险较低者（OR 0.10；95%CI 0.005-0.642；P=0.040）中，SGB与较低POAF发生率的关联更明显。

结论与意义

在体外循环冠脉搭桥患者中，术前预防性左侧星状神经节阻滞与术后5天内POAF发生率降低相关，并伴随POAF负荷、心肌损伤与炎症标志物及住院时间的改善；无糖尿病和基线风险较低的患者可能获益更明显。该结果为POAF的神经调控预防提供了随机对照证据，但亚组分析属探索性、样本量有限，且主要来自单一中心，仍需多中心大样本研究验证其对临床硬终点的作用。

Increased Heart Failure Risk After Low-Voltage-Area Ablation in Patients With Paroxysmal Atrial Fibrillation

JACC Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42848334
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Ochi M、Sunaga A、Uematsu H 等 13 位作者

英文摘要

Background Extensive atrial ablation may impair atrial function; however, its impact on long-term heart failure (HF) risk remains unclear.Objectives This study sought to evaluate the association between low-voltage area (LVA) ablation and HF outcomes after catheter ablation for paroxysmal atrial fibrillation (AF).Methods This was a long-term follow-up analysis of the Volatile Coronary Lesion and Obstructive Nodal Atrial Fibrillation trial. A total of 398 patients were followed for up to 7 years and classified into 3 groups: no LVAs (group A), LVAs with LVA ablation (group B), and LVAs without LVA ablation (group C). The primary outcome was HF hospitalization.Results During a median follow-up of 1,660 (735-2,555) days, HF hospitalization occurred in 21 patients. Sinus rhythm was maintained in 96% of patients, with no differences among groups (P = 0.26). Patients with LVAs had a higher incidence of HF hospitalization than those without (18% vs 3%; P < 0.001). Among patients with LVAs, HF hospitalization occurred more frequently in those undergoing LVA ablation (30% vs 6%; P = 0.01). In a competing-risk analysis accounting for all-cause death, the cumulative incidence of HF hospitalization remained significantly higher in patients undergoing LVA ablation (Gray's test, P = 0.042). Among patients who experienced HF hospitalization, most events occurred during documented sinus rhythm.Conclusions Among patients with paroxysmal AF and LVAs, LVA ablation was associated with a higher incidence of HF hospitalization despite a high rate of sinus rhythm maintenance. Given the limited sample size and number of events, these findings should be considered exploratory and hypothesis-generating, and warrant confirmation in larger prospective studies.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在阵发性房颤合并低电压区的患者中，接受低电压区消融者的心衰住院率明显更高（30% vs 6%），尽管96%的患者维持窦性心律。

研究动机

广泛的心房消融可能损害心房机械功能，但低电压区（LVA）基质改良对远期心衰（HF）风险的影响尚不明确。既往研究多聚焦于房颤复发这一节律终点，缺乏对LVA消融后心衰住院等临床结局的长期随访证据，本研究旨在填补这一缺口。

方法

本研究为 Volatile Coronary Lesion and Obstructive Nodal Atrial Fibrillation 试验的长期随访分析，纳入398例阵发性房颤患者，随访最长7年，按是否存在LVA及是否行LVA消融分为三组：无LVA组（A组）、有LVA并接受LVA消融组（B组）、有LVA但未行LVA消融组（C组）。主要终点为心衰住院；采用将全因死亡作为竞争风险的竞争风险分析（Gray检验）。各组的样本量和消融策略细节，摘要未报告。

结果

中位随访1,660天（735–2,555天），共21例发生心衰住院。96%的患者维持窦性心律，三组间无差异（P=0.26）。合并LVA者的心衰住院率高于无LVA者（18% vs 3%，P<0.001）；在合并LVA的患者中，接受LVA消融者的心衰住院率更高（30% vs 6%，P=0.01）。在校正全因死亡的竞争风险分析中，LVA消融组心衰住院的累积发生率仍显著升高（Gray检验，P=0.042）。发生心衰住院的患者中，多数事件发生于记录到的窦性心律期间。

结论与意义

在合并LVA的阵发性房颤患者中，LVA消融与心衰住院风险升高相关，且该风险并非由房颤复发解释，因为窦性心律维持率高，且事件多发生于窦性心律时，提示心房机械功能受损可能是潜在机制。这提示对心房基质进行广泛消融时需权衡获益与心功能代价，并加强术后心房功能与心衰症状的监测。但本研究样本量小、事件数少（仅21例），作者将其定位为探索性、产生假说的结果，尚需更大规模前瞻性研究证实。

Impact of Interelectrode Spacing on Ventricular Tachycardia Substrate Mapping: Insights Into 3-Dimensional Characteristics of the Arrhythmogenic Substrate

JACC Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42820875
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Iioka Y、Komatsu Y、Shinoda Y 等 14 位作者

英文摘要

Background Re-entrant ventricular tachycardia (VT) circuits often involve 3-dimensional activation across the endocardium, epicardium, and mid-myocardium, highlighting the importance of comprehensive substrate assessment. Wider interelectrode spacing may incorporate broader-field electrical components that are less apparent on conventional closely spaced recordings.Objectives This study sought to investigate the electrophysiological impact of interelectrode spacing on substrate mapping and its potential implications for 3-dimensional features of VT substrate.Methods Sixty-eight high-density substrate maps from 35 patients undergoing catheter ablation for scar-related VT were retrospectively analyzed. Closely spaced (2-mm) and widely spaced (8-mm) bipolar electrograms were simultaneously acquired using a decapolar catheter and used to generate closely and widely spaced bipolar electrode maps (CEM and WEM).Results Compared with CEM, WEM, which incorporates a greater contribution of far-field components, demonstrated higher bipolar amplitudes, a greater prevalence of multicomponent electrograms, and a lower prevalence of split potentials (all P < 0.01). Lines of block delineated by split potentials on CEM were frequently attenuated or no longer apparent on WEM because the corresponding electrograms appeared fragmented and lacked a distinct split configuration. In an exploratory analysis of 6 VTs with evaluable activation maps, activation gaps were larger with closely than widely spaced bipolar mapping (32.5% vs 20.0%; P = 0.03). In 4 VTs, widely spaced recordings identified diastolic or presystolic far-field potentials not evident with closely spaced recordings.Conclusions Widely spaced bipolar electrograms may provide complementary functional information beyond that obtained with conventional near-field recordings by incorporating broader-field electrical components, thereby offering additional insight into the potential 3-dimensional characteristics of the arrhythmogenic substrate.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

35例瘢痕相关VT消融患者共68份高密度标测图显示，8 mm宽间距双极电图较2 mm窄间距振幅更高、多成分电位更多，可补充揭示致心律失常基质的三维特征。

研究动机

折返性VT环路常涉及心内膜、心外膜及中层心肌的三维激动，提示需全面评估基质。常规基质标测依赖2 mm窄间距双极电图，主要反映近场局部电活动；宽间距电极可能纳入更广域的电成分，但电极间距如何影响基质标测判读、及其与VT基质三维特征的关系尚缺乏系统证据。

方法

单中心回顾性研究，纳入35例因瘢痕相关VT接受导管消融的患者，共分析68份高密度基质标测图。使用十极导管同步采集2 mm窄间距与8 mm宽间距双极电图，分别构建窄间距（CEM）与宽间距（WEM）双极电图标测图。比较两组的双极电压振幅、多成分电图比例、分裂电位（split potential）比例，并以分裂电位勾画的阻滞线作为基质指标；探索性分析6例激动图可评估的VT中激动缺口（activation gap）比例，并观察宽间距记录能否识别舒张期或收缩前期远场电位。具体统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

与CEM相比，包含更多远场成分的WEM双极振幅更高、多成分电图更常见，而分裂电位更少见（均P<0.01）。CEM上由分裂电位界定的阻滞线在WEM上常被削弱或不再显现，相应电图表现为碎裂、缺乏清晰的分裂形态。在6例激动图可评估的VT中，窄间距标测的激动缺口大于宽间距标测（32.5% vs 20.0%，P=0.03）。4例VT中，宽间距记录识别出窄间距记录未能显示的舒张期或收缩前期远场电位。具体振幅数值、效应量95%CI及两组电图例数摘要未报告。

结论与意义

宽间距双极电图通过纳入更广域的电成分，可提供常规近场记录之外的功能信息，有助于解读致心律失常基质潜在的三维特征，可能在VT基质标测中起互补作用。局限性：本研究为回顾性、单中心、小样本研究，探索性分析仅基于6例与4例VT，未报告电压振幅绝对值与置信区间，也未评估消融成功率、VT复发等临床终点，宽间距标测的参数设置与临床价值尚需前瞻性研究验证。

Comparative Efficacy of Rebamipide, Pantoprazole, and Their Combination for Gastroprotection in Patients with Atrial Fibrillation Receiving Direct Oral Anticoagulants: The Randomized REGATA Study.

Med Sci (Basel)·2026-09-24·PMID 42783438
房颤与消融 Journal Article Randomized Controlled Trial Comparative Study

Natalya M Vorobyeva、Irina P Malaya、Vadim D Zakiev 等 9 位作者

英文摘要

Background: Patients with atrial fibrillation (AF) receiving direct oral anticoagulants (DOAC) are at an elevated risk of gastrointestinal (GI) mucosal damage and bleeding. Proton pump inhibitors (PPI) are conventional gastroprotectors, but their efficacy in the lower GI tract is limited. This study evaluated the efficacy and safety of rebamipide monotherapy and combination pantoprazole-rebamipide (P + R) therapy compared to pantoprazole monotherapy in this vulnerable population. Methods: In the randomized, parallel-group, open-label, single-center REGATA trial, 210 AF patients receiving DOAC were randomized (1:1:1) to three gastroprotective regimens (n = 70 each) for 24 weeks: pantoprazole monotherapy, rebamipide monotherapy, or their combination. Efficacy analyses were performed in the per-protocol population (n = 118: pantoprazole, n = 36; rebamipide, n = 39; P + R, n = 43). The primary and secondary outcomes included composite clinical GI events (bleeding classified by ISTH/BARC, ulcers/erosions, and severe dyspepsia) and biomarkers of mucosal barrier function (fecal zonulin and calprotectin). Results: The incidence of primary efficacy endpoint events was 13.9% (n = 5; 95% CI: 3.1-25.7) in the pantoprazole group, 10.3% (n = 4; 95% CI: 2.3-21.1) in the rebamipide group, and 9.3% (n = 4; 95% CI: 2.1-18.6) in the P + R group, successfully confirming the non-inferiority hypothesis for both experimental arms within the pre-specified margin of 10% (p < 0.05). Multivariable regression analysis showed that compared with pantoprazole monotherapy, rebamipide monotherapy demonstrated an odds ratio OR of 0.71 (95% CI: 0.18-2.88, p = 0.630), while the P + R therapy was associated with an OR of 0.64 (95% CI: 0.16-2.57, p = 0.525). No major GI bleeding events or fatalities occurred; a single episode of GI bleeding (ISTH minor/BARC Type 2) was recorded in the pantoprazole arm (2.8%). Fecal calprotectin levels significantly decreased in the rebamipide group (by 37.6%, from 139 to 78 µg/g; p < 0.001) and the P + R group (by 50.0%, from 135 to 61 µg/g; p < 0.001), while no significant changes were observed in the pantoprazole group (p = 0.530). Concurrently, fecal zonulin levels, reflecting intestinal epithelial permeability, fell by 45.1% in the rebamipide arm (from 434 to 225 ng/mL; p < 0.001) and by 47.1% in the P + R arm (from 347 to 182 ng/mL; p < 0.001) compared to a minimal 3.0% fluctuation in the pantoprazole group. Conclusions: While demonstrating non-inferiority regarding primary clinical endpoints, both rebamipide monotherapy and combination (P + R) therapy achieved some advantages over pantoprazole monotherapy in decreasing subclinical GI inflammation and restoring intestinal epithelial barrier integrity.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在服用DOAC的房颤患者中，替普瑞酮单药及泮托拉唑联合替普瑞酮的24周胃肠道保护方案，在复合临床胃肠道事件上不劣于泮托拉唑单药，且能显著降低粪便钙卫蛋白与连蛋白水平、改善肠黏膜屏障。

研究动机

房颤患者长期服用DOAC，胃肠道黏膜损伤与出血风险升高；PPI虽是常规胃黏膜保护剂，但对下消化道保护作用有限，临床上缺乏针对该人群替代或联合保护策略的随机对照证据。

方法

随机、平行分组、开放标签、单中心REGATA试验。210例接受DOAC的AF患者按1:1:1随机分为泮托拉唑单药、替普瑞酮单药及两药联合组（各70例），疗程24周；疗效分析基于符合方案人群（n=118，分别为36、39、43例）。主要终点为复合临床胃肠道事件（按ISTH/BARC分级的出血、溃疡或糜烂、重度消化不良），次要终点为黏膜屏障功能生物标志物（粪便连蛋白、钙卫蛋白），并采用多因素回归分析比较组间风险。

结果

主要疗效终点事件发生率：泮托拉唑组13.9%（5例，95%CI 3.1–25.7），替普瑞酮组10.3%（4例，95%CI 2.3–21.1），联合组9.3%（4例，95%CI 2.1–18.6），两个试验组在预设10%非劣效界值内均达到非劣效（p<0.05）。多因素回归显示，与泮托拉唑单药相比，替普瑞酮单药OR 0.71（95%CI 0.18–2.88，p=0.630），联合治疗OR 0.64（95%CI 0.16–2.57，p=0.525）。无重大消化道出血及死亡事件，泮托拉唑组记录到1例消化道出血（ISTH次要/BARC 2型，2.8%）。粪便钙卫蛋白在替普瑞酮组下降37.6%（139→78 µg/g，p<0.001）、联合组下降50.0%（135→61 µg/g，p<0.001），泮托拉唑组无显著变化（p=0.530）；粪便连蛋白在替普瑞酮组下降45.1%（434→225 ng/mL，p<0.001）、联合组下降47.1%（347→182 ng/mL，p<0.001），泮托拉唑组仅波动3.0%。

结论与意义

在接受DOAC的房颤患者中，替普瑞酮单药及与泮托拉唑联用于临床终点不劣于泮托拉唑单药，并在降低亚临床胃肠道炎症、恢复肠上皮屏障完整性方面显示出优势，提示其可作为下消化道保护的可选策略；但本研究为开放标签、单中心、符合方案人群样本量较小（n=118），临床事件数少、置信区间宽，上述获益需更大规模盲法试验验证。

Atrial Fibrillation in Retinal Artery Occlusion: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Med Sci (Basel)·2026-09-24·PMID 42783426
房颤与消融 Journal Article Systematic Review Meta-Analysis

Aurelia Mihaela Nica、Madalina Andreea Beldie、Samer Andrei Nica 等 10 位作者

英文摘要

Background/Objectives: Retinal artery occlusion (RAO) represents an ischemic event of the anterior circulation, driven by impaired blood flow through the ophthalmic branches of the internal carotid artery. Atrial fibrillation (AF) is an increasingly recognized cardioembolic source in patients with RAO, yet its prevalence remains poorly characterized. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis to estimate the pooled prevalence of AF in RAO patients and to summarize evidence on its incidence and association with this condition. Methods: Four databases were searched through April 2025, updated on 18 August 2026. Studies reporting AF prevalence, incidence, or association in adults with RAO were included. Pooled prevalence was estimated using the Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation with a random-effects model. Subgroup analyses, meta-regression, and sensitivity analyses were performed to explore heterogeneity. Results: Sixty-two studies were included in the qualitative synthesis, of which 58 reported AF prevalence data. Among the 36 studies comprising 139,222 patients, the pooled AF prevalence was 11.53% (95% CI: 9.41-13.82; I2 = 99.2%; prediction interval: 2.00-26.9%). AF prevalence was significantly higher in patients aged ≥70 years (15.86%) and in central RAO cohorts (16.54%). Incident AF following RAO ranged from 10.5% to 41%, increasing progressively with monitoring intensity. The pooled odds ratio for the AF-RAO association was 1.56 (95% CI: 1.06-2.29). Conclusions: AF affects approximately one in nine patients with RAO, though with substantial uncertainty across populations (prediction interval 2.00-26.9%); the extent to which this exceeds the general-population estimates independent of age, cardiovascular risk profile, and geographic factors remains uncertain. These findings support cardiac rhythm surveillance following RAO diagnosis in selected patients, although its effect on clinical outcomes remains to be established prospectively.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

系统评价与荟萃分析显示，视网膜动脉闭塞（RAO）患者中房颤总体患病率约11.53%，即约每9例RAO患者中有1例合并AF，且年龄≥70岁与视网膜中央动脉闭塞者患病率更高。

研究动机

RAO属于前循环缺血事件，由颈内动脉眼动脉分支血流受损所致；房颤作为心源性栓塞来源在RAO患者中日益受到重视，但其患病率一直缺乏可靠量化，既往证据多为单中心小样本，缺乏汇总估计，因此需要系统评价明确AF在RAO人群中的负担及其与RAO的关联强度，为RAO后心律监测策略提供依据。

方法

系统评价与荟萃分析，检索4个数据库至2025年4月（2026年8月18日更新），纳入报告成人RAO患者AF患病率、发生率或关联性的研究。合并患病率采用Freeman-Tukey双反正弦变换与随机效应模型估计，并通过亚组分析、meta回归和敏感性分析探讨异质性。共62项研究进入定性合成，其中58项报告AF患病率数据；36项研究、139,222例患者纳入患病率合并分析。

结果

合并AF患病率为11.53%（95%CI 9.41-13.82；I²=99.2%；预测区间2.00-26.9%）。亚组中年龄≥70岁者患病率15.86%，视网膜中央动脉闭塞队列为16.54%，显著高于相应亚组。RAO后新发AF为10.5%-41%，随监测强度增加而升高。AF与RAO关联的合并OR为1.56（95%CI 1.06-2.29）。

结论与意义

AF约累及1/9的RAO患者，但跨人群不确定性很大（预测区间2.00-26.9%），其超出一般人群估计的程度在排除年龄、心血管风险谱和地理因素后仍不明确。结果支持对选择性RAO患者诊断后开展心律监测，但其对临床结局的改善尚待前瞻性研究证实；异质性极高（I²=99.2%）为主要局限。

Switching from Vitamin K Antagonists to Direct Oral Anticoagulants in Frail Elderly Patients with Atrial Fibrillation: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Med Sci (Basel)·2026-09-24·PMID 42783389
房颤与消融 Journal Article Meta-Analysis Systematic Review

Paschalis Karakasis、Ricardo Gómez-Huelgas、Stylianos Tzeis 等 12 位作者

英文摘要

Background: Frailty complicates anticoagulation management in older patients with atrial fibrillation (AF), particularly when long-term vitamin K antagonist (VKA) therapy is already established. Whether switching to a direct oral anticoagulant (DOAC) improves outcomes in this setting remains uncertain, and guideline recommendations are limited. We performed a systematic review and meta-analysis comparing outcomes of VKA-to-DOAC switching versus VKA continuation in frail elderly patients with AF. Methods: MEDLINE, Scopus, and the Cochrane Library were systematically searched from inception through 4 April 2026. Studies enrolling frail elderly patients with AF receiving VKA therapy and directly comparing switching to a DOAC versus continued VKA treatment were eligible. The primary outcome was a composite of stroke/systemic embolism, major bleeding, or all-cause mortality. Adjusted hazard ratios (HRs) were pooled using three-level random-effects models. Results: Four studies (five treatment comparisons) comprising 57,099 patients were included. Overall, switching from VKA to DOAC therapy was not associated with a significant difference in the composite outcome compared with continued VKA treatment (HR 1.18, 95% CI 0.84-1.67). No significant differences were observed for major bleeding (HR 1.13, 95% CI 0.80-1.60), thromboembolism (HR 1.03, 95% CI 0.63-1.68), or all-cause mortality (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.77-1.16). In analyses restricted to randomized trials, results remained neutral across all outcomes. Conclusion: In frail elderly patients with AF receiving VKA therapy, switching to a DOAC was not associated with significant reductions in thromboembolism, major bleeding, mortality, or the composite outcome compared with continued VKA treatment.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在已长期服用维生素K拮抗剂（VKA）的体弱老年房颤患者中，换用DOAC与继续VKA相比，卒中、系统性栓塞、大出血及全因死亡的复合终点，无显著差异（HR 1.18，95%CI 0.84-1.67）。

研究动机

衰弱使老年房颤患者的抗凝管理更加复杂，尤其对于已经长期使用VKA的患者。既往DOAC的关键性随机试验中，体弱高龄人群所占比例低，且这些试验比较的是“起始使用DOAC”与“起始使用VKA”，未回答“已稳定使用VKA者是否应换用DOAC”这一临床常见问题；指南对此推荐有限、证据存在缺口。

方法

系统综述与荟萃分析。检索MEDLINE、Scopus与Cochrane Library，时间自建库至2026年4月4日。纳入标准：体弱老年房颤患者，已接受VKA治疗，并直接比较换用DOAC与继续VKA的研究。主要终点为卒中或系统性栓塞、大出血、全因死亡的复合终点；效应量为校正后风险比（HR），采用三水平随机效应模型合并。共纳入4项研究、5组治疗比较、57,099例患者，并对随机对照试验进行限制性分析。

结果

换用DOAC组与继续VKA组的主要复合终点无显著差异（HR 1.18，95%CI 0.84-1.67）。大出血（HR 1.13，95%CI 0.80-1.60）、血栓栓塞事件（HR 1.03，95%CI 0.63-1.68）与全因死亡（HR 0.95，95%CI 0.77-1.16）也均未达到统计学差异。仅纳入随机对照试验的敏感性分析中，所有终点同样呈中性结果。摘要未报告各终点的事件绝对发生率与随访时长。

结论与意义

对于已接受VKA治疗的体弱老年房颤患者，换用DOAC未能在血栓栓塞、大出血、死亡或复合终点方面带来显著获益，不支持仅以改善预后为目的常规换药，提示对这类患者，若INR控制尚可，继续VKA是合理选择，换药决策应基于个体化评估（如衰弱程度、出血风险、INR达标时间、依从性与费用）。局限在于纳入研究以观察性数据为主、样本量集中于少数研究、存在残余混杂与选择偏倚，且随机证据有限，结论需谨慎外推到普通房颤人群。

Exploratory Modeling of Postoperative Atrial Fibrillation After Cardiac Surgery with Cardiopulmonary Bypass Using Inflammatory Biomarkers and Clinical-Surgical Factors.

Med Sci (Basel)·2026-09-24·PMID 42783386
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Rosa Michel Martínez-Contreras、Marina María de Jesús Romero-Prado、Karla Mayela Bravo-Villagra 等 11 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND/OBJECTIVES: Postoperative atrial fibrillation (POAF) is a common complication after cardiac surgery with cardiopulmonary bypass (CPB), increasing morbidity and prolonging hospitalization. This study aimed to develop and validate an exploratory prediction model that integrates perioperative inflammatory biomarkers with clinical and surgical variables to identify patients at risk of early POAF. METHODS: A prospective exploratory cohort of 89 patients undergoing coronary artery bypass grafting (CABG; n = 36), valve surgery (n = 40), or CABG-valve surgery (n = 13) was evaluated. Clinical, surgical, and proinflammatory serum biomarkers (IL-6, IL-8, IL-10, and CRP) were recorded preoperatively (T1) and at 24 h (T2) and 48 h (T3) postoperatively. Multiple-comparison adjustments were made using the Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate. Predictor selection was based on bootstrap-derived stability using LASSO-penalized logistic regression, and the final model was estimated using Firth's bias-reduced logistic regression. RESULTS: POAF incidence was 8.3% in CABG, in contrast to 22.5% and 30.8% in valve and CABG-valve surgeries, respectively. After multiple-comparison corrections, only IL-6 at T2 postoperatively was significantly higher in patients who subsequently developed POAF. Bootstrap-based stability selection retained T2 postoperative IL-10 and magnesium concentrations in the final model, which achieved an apparent AUC of 0.776 and a bootstrap optimism-corrected AUC of 0.728, with acceptable calibration (Brier score = 0.103), negligible multicollinearity (VIF = 1.04), and a negative predictive value of 95.5% at the optimal Youden threshold. CONCLUSIONS: Our findings support an exploratory prediction model with moderate discrimination for POAF after cardiac surgery with CPB, providing a methodological foundation for future multicenter validation studies.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在接受体外循环心脏手术的89例患者中，术后早期房颤发生率在单纯CABG组为8.3%、瓣膜手术组为22.5%、CABG联合瓣膜组为30.8%；仅术后24小时IL-6与POAF独立相关，基于IL-10与镁的探索性模型校正AUC为0.728。

研究动机

体外循环心脏手术后房颤（POAF）是常见并发症，可增加病残率并延长住院时间。既往预测模型多依赖临床与手术因素，围术期炎症生物标志物与临床-手术变量整合的预测价值尚不明确，缺乏可供外部验证的建模方法学基础。

方法

前瞻性探索性队列研究，纳入89例接受体外循环心脏手术患者：单纯CABG 36例、瓣膜手术40例、CABG联合瓣膜手术13例。于术前（T1）、术后24小时（T2）和48小时（T3）检测血清IL-6、IL-8、IL-10与CRP，并记录临床与手术变量。多重比较采用Benjamini-Hochberg错误发现率校正；预测因子筛选基于LASSO惩罚logistic回归的bootstrap稳定性，最终模型用Firth偏差校正logistic回归估计。终点为术后早期POAF，评估指标包括表观AUC、bootstrap乐观校正AUC、Brier评分、方差膨胀因子（VIF）及最佳Youden阈值下的阴性预测值。

结果

POAF发生率：单纯CABG 8.3%，瓣膜手术22.5%，CABG联合瓣膜手术30.8%。多重比较校正后，仅术后24小时IL-6在随后发生POAF的患者中显著升高。bootstrap稳定性筛选最终保留术后24小时IL-10与镁浓度进入模型：表观AUC 0.776，bootstrap乐观校正AUC 0.728，校准可接受（Brier评分=0.103），多重共线性可忽略（VIF=1.04），最佳Youden阈值下阴性预测值为95.5%。摘要未报告各预测因子的具体OR值、95%CI及P值。

结论与意义

该探索性模型对体外循环心脏手术后POAF具有中等判别能力，高阴性预测值提示其潜在用于低危患者筛选，为后续多中心外部验证提供了方法学基础。但样本量小（89例）、事件数有限、仅为单中心探索性分析且缺乏外部验证，IL-6、IL-10与镁等指标的独立效应量未报告，尚不足以直接指导临床决策。

Longitudinal Plasma Lipidomic Changes in Chinese Patients with Nonvalvular Atrial Fibrillation During 12 Weeks of Edoxaban Treatment.

Metabolites·2026-09-24·PMID 42783797
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Cheng Cui、Junhao Chen、Haodi Chai 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

Introduction: Altered lipid metabolism is implicated in atrial fibrillation, but longitudinal lipidomic changes during anticoagulation remain poorly characterized. Objectives: To characterize plasma lipidomic changes during 12 weeks of edoxaban treatment and explore associated metabolic pathways in Chinese patients with nonvalvular atrial fibrillation (NVAF). Methods: This prespecified substudy of a prospective multicenter study analyzed fasting predose plasma collected at baseline and week 12 from 74 patients receiving protocol-assigned edoxaban doses (15 mg, n = 3; 30 mg, n = 23; 60 mg, n = 48). Widely targeted UPLC-MS/MS lipid profiling, with laboratory quality control (QC CV < 30%), yielded 1099 lipid features retained for statistical analysis. Differential lipids were defined by paired testing with Benjamini-Hochberg correction (|log2FC| > 0.58 and q < 0.05). Results: The overall, 30 mg, and 60 mg analyses identified 25, 15, and 35 FDR-significant features, respectively; none was significant in the exploratory 15 mg group. Twelve features were shared across the primary analyses and increased at week 12; they comprised mainly acylcarnitines, together with docosadienoic acid and DG (21:0/16:0/0:0), and four lacked HMDB identifiers. The corresponding FDR panels yielded participant-grouped out-of-fold AUCs of 0.918 (95% CI 0.890-0.963), 0.786 (0.701-0.890), and 0.891 (0.835-0.950) for discriminating baseline from week 12, respectively. Pathway mapping identified no multi-compound enriched pathways (q < 0.05). Observed annotations represented single-metabolite signals-most notably DG (21:0/16:0/0:0) (HMDB0094301) and octanoylcarnitine (HMDB0000791)-rather than coordinated, pathway-level alterations. Conclusions: Paired analyses identified consistent longitudinal plasma lipidomic changes in patients receiving edoxaban. The 12 shared features provide focused candidates for targeted validation. Controlled studies are needed to determine whether these changes are specific to edoxaban and to establish their biological and clinical relevance.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

74例中国非瓣膜性房颤患者接受依度沙班治疗12周后，血浆脂质组出现一致的纵向变化：以酰基肉碱升高为主的12个脂质特征在各主要分析中共享，但未发现通路水平的协同改变。

研究动机

脂代谢异常与房颤相关，但抗凝治疗期间血浆脂质组的纵向变化尚缺乏系统刻画。本研究旨在描述中国非瓣膜性房颤（NVAF）患者接受依度沙班治疗12周过程中的血浆脂质组学改变，并探索相关代谢通路，为后续寻找抗凝相关的脂质标志物提供线索。

方法

研究为一项前瞻性多中心研究的预设亚组分析；纳入74例按方案接受依度沙班治疗的中国NVAF患者（15 mg 组 n=3、30 mg 组 n=23、60 mg 组 n=48）。采集基线与第12周空腹、服药前血浆，采用广泛靶向 UPLC-MS/MS 脂质组学检测，经实验室质控（QC CV<30%）后保留1099个脂质特征进入统计分析。差异脂质采用配对检验并以 Benjamini-Hochberg 法校正，定义为 |log2FC|>0.58 且 q<0.05；采用按受试者分组的折外 AUC 评估基线与第12周的判别能力，并行通路富集分析。

结果

总体分析、30 mg 组与60 mg 组分别识别出25、15和35个 FDR 显著特征；探索性15 mg 组（n=3）无显著特征。12个特征在主要分析中共享，且在第12周均升高，主要为酰基肉碱，另有多二十二碳二烯酸（docosadienoic acid）与 DG(21:0/16:0/0:0)，其中4个缺乏 HMDB 编号。相应 FDR 特征集的受试者分组折外 AUC 分别为0.918（95%CI 0.890-0.963）、0.786（95%CI 0.701-0.890）和0.891（95%CI 0.835-0.950），用于区分基线与第12周。通路映射未发现多化合物富集的通路（q<0.05），所观察到的注释均为单代谢物信号，尤以 DG(21:0/16:0/0:0)（HMDB0094301）与辛酰肉碱（octanoylcarnitine，HMDB0000791）为著，而非通路水平的协同改变。

结论与意义

配对分析显示，接受依度沙班治疗的患者存在一致的纵向血浆脂质组变化，12个共享特征可作为靶向验证的重点候选物。但本研究为单臂设计、样本量小（尤其15 mg 组仅3例）、随访仅12周且缺乏未抗凝对照，无法判断这些变化是否为依度沙班所特有；其对房颤血栓栓塞与出血风险的生物学及临床意义，尚需设立对照的研究加以明确。

Targeted Quantitative Metabolomics and Lipidomics Reveal Dysregulated Metabolic Networks and a Serum Candidate Biomarker for Atrial Fibrillation.

Metabolites·2026-09-24·PMID 42783755
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Yuqing Zhang、Yunpeng Xie、Xinyu Liu 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most prevalent clinical arrhythmia with severe cardiovascular complications, yet its metabolic molecular mechanisms remain poorly defined. Omics-based metabolic profiling provides a powerful strategy to systematically decode AF-associated metabolic disorders. METHODS: In this work, high-coverage targeted liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC-MS/MS) metabolomics and lipidomics were applied to absolutely quantify 746 serum metabolites from AF patients and healthy controls. RESULTS: We systematically characterized global metabolic perturbations in AF serum, including impaired fatty acid metabolism, suppressed mitochondrial β-oxidation, myocardial lipotoxic lipid accumulation, and systemic depletion of glycerophospholipids. Global multiscale embedded correlation network analysis (MECNA) further identified 11 AF-specific dysregulated metabolic modules and core hub metabolites driving metabolic remodeling. Leveraging binary logistic regression, we constructed and independently validated a two-molecule diagnostic biomarker panel to distinguish AF patients from healthy subjects. The combined biomarkers Phe-Trp and FA 22:5 achieved outstanding diagnostic performance, with area under the curve (AUC) values of 0.964 in the discovery cohort and 0.993 in the validation cohort. CONCLUSIONS: Collectively, this study adopts high-depth targeted quantitative omics to comprehensively map AF metabolic signatures, dissect disease-relevant metabolic networks, and establish a robust serum biomarker panel with great translational potential for non-invasive AF clinical diagnosis.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

采用靶向LC-MS/MS定量代谢组与脂质组学比较AF患者与健康对照血清，发现脂肪酸代谢受损、线粒体β氧化受抑，并构建Phe-Trp联合FA 22:5诊断面板，发现队列AUC 0.964、验证队列0.993。

研究动机

AF是最常见的临床心律失常，可导致严重心血管并发症，但其代谢分子机制长期未明。既往研究多为非靶向、半定量的代谢轮廓分析，缺乏对AF系统性代谢紊乱的精确刻画，也缺少可用于无创诊断的血清标志物。本研究拟通过高覆盖靶向定量组学系统解码AF相关代谢异常，并筛选候选生物标志物。

方法

研究为病例对照设计，纳入AF患者与健康对照，具体样本量、来源中心及匹配方式摘要未报告。研究采用高覆盖靶向液相色谱-串联质谱（LC-MS/MS）代谢组学与脂质组学，对血清中746种代谢物进行绝对定量。分析内容包括总体代谢扰动谱刻画、多尺度嵌入相关网络分析（MECNA）识别AF特异性失调代谢模块及核心hub代谢物，并以二元logistic回归构建两分子诊断面板，在独立验证队列中评估其区分AF患者与健康受试者的诊断效能（主要指标为AUC）。敏感性分析、协变量校正及具体统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

AF血清存在全局性代谢扰动，表现为脂肪酸代谢受损、线粒体β氧化受抑、心肌脂毒性脂质蓄积及甘油磷脂系统性耗竭。MECNA识别出11个AF特异性失调代谢模块及驱动代谢重塑的核心hub代谢物。基于二元logistic回归构建的两分子面板（Phe-Trp联合FA 22:5）诊断效能突出，AUC在发现队列为0.964、在验证队列为0.993。各指标的敏感度、特异度及95%置信区间摘要未报告。

结论与意义

本研究以高深度靶向定量组学全面绘制AF代谢特征图谱，解析疾病相关代谢网络，并建立稳健的血清两分子标志物面板，为非侵入性AF临床诊断提供了具有转化潜力的工具。局限性包括：队列样本量与基线特征未报告，横断面病例对照设计无法推断代谢紊乱与AF的因果关系，且未报告标志物与AF负荷、亚型及消融或卒中结局的关联；结果需多中心、前瞻性大队列外部验证后方可进入临床。对电生理实践而言，该面板有望作为AF机会性筛查与风险分层的辅助手段，但目前尚不能替代心电图与动态监测。

Esophageal Temperature Changes During Pulsed Field Ablation With Three Catheter Systems.

Pacing Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42784423
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Kanto Saida、Takunori Aoyama、Hitoshi Hirano 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Pulsed field ablation (PFA) is considered less thermally injurious than conventional ablation technologies, but esophageal temperature elevation can still occur. Human data directly comparing esophageal temperature changes among different PFA catheter systems remain limited. We compared esophageal temperature changes during PFA using VARIPULSE, PulseSelect, and FARAPULSE. METHODS: This retrospective single-center study included consecutive patients with nonvalvular atrial fibrillation who underwent pulmonary vein isolation using PFA with luminal esophageal temperature monitoring between April 2025 and March 2026. Esophageal temperature was measured at five sites every 0.5 s. For each patient, esophageal temperature rise (ΔT) was defined as the maximum esophageal temperature recorded during PFA minus the mean esophageal temperature during the 5-min interval immediately preceding the PFA application associated with that maximum temperature. A sensitivity analysis using the same ΔT definition was performed in patients who underwent pulmonary vein isolation alone. RESULTS: A total of 70 patients were analyzed. ΔT differed among the three groups and was greatest with VARIPULSE (1.61 ± 1.00°C vs. 1.10 ± 0.61°C vs. 0.88 ± 0.43°C for VARIPULSE, PulseSelect, and FARAPULSE, respectively; p = 0.0302). In patients undergoing pulmonary vein isolation alone (n = 56), ΔT remained significantly different among the three groups (1.61 ± 1.03°C vs. 1.05 ± 0.61°C vs. 0.74 ± 0.38°C, respectively; p = 0.0386). Clinically significant temperature elevation (ΔT ≥ 2.0°C) occurred most frequently with VARIPULSE in both the overall cohort and the pulmonary vein isolation-only subgroup. Temperature elevation was most prominent during ablation near the left pulmonary veins and in anatomically adjacent regions to the esophagus. No patient developed symptoms suggestive of esophageal ulceration; however, systematic post-procedural endoscopic assessment was not performed. CONCLUSIONS: Measured esophageal temperature elevation during PFA differed according to catheter system and was greatest with VARIPULSE; this between-system difference persisted in patients undergoing pulmonary vein isolation alone. Because procedural characteristics differed among systems, these findings should not be interpreted as establishing a device-specific causal mechanism or esophageal safety.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

70 例非瓣膜性房颤患者回顾性比较三种脉冲场消融（PFA）系统行肺静脉隔离时的食管温升，VARIPULSE 升幅最大（1.61°C），显著高于 PulseSelect 与 FARAPULSE。

研究动机

PFA 通常被认为较传统射频/冷冻消融热损伤更小，但临床上仍可观察到食管温度升高，而食管热损伤是房颤消融最受关注的并发症之一。既往缺乏在同一中心、同一测量方法下直接比较不同 PFA 导管系统食管温升幅度的人体数据，临床在选择器械时缺少这方面的参考。

方法

回顾性单中心研究，连续纳入 2025 年 4 月至 2026 年 3 月接受 PFA 肺静脉隔离并全程行腔内食管温度监测的非瓣膜性房颤患者，共 70 例，按所用导管系统分为 VARIPULSE、PulseSelect、FARAPULSE 三组。食管温度于 5 个位点每 0.5 秒采集一次；ΔT 定义为 PFA 放电期间记录到的最高食管温度减去该次放电前 5 分钟 interval 的平均食管温度。另在仅行肺静脉隔离（n=56）的患者中按同一定义做敏感性分析。比较三组 ΔT，并记录 ΔT≥2.0°C 的发生情况与温升的解剖分布。未报告具体的组间样本量分配及统计检验方法。

结果

三组 ΔT 差异有统计学意义，VARIPULSE 最高（1.61±1.00°C vs. PulseSelect 1.10±0.61°C vs. FARAPULSE 0.88±0.43°C，p=0.0302）。在仅行肺静脉隔离的 56 例中该差异持续存在（1.61±1.03°C vs. 1.05±0.61°C vs. 0.74±0.38°C，p=0.0386）。临床意义的温升（ΔT≥2.0°C）在总体人群及单纯 PVI 亚组中均以 VARIPULSE 最常见。温升在左肺静脉附近及与食管解剖相邻区域的消融时最为明显。无患者出现提示食管溃疡的症状，但未行系统性术后内镜评估。摘要未报告组间 95% 置信区间及多因素校正结果。

结论与意义

在接受 PFA 肺静脉隔离的患者中，食管温升幅度因导管系统而异，VARIPULSE 的 ΔT 最高，且在排除同期其他消融操作的亚组中结论一致。但各系统的操作参数与消融序列本身不同，本研究为回顾性观察、样本量有限、缺乏内镜结局，不能据此推断器械特异性的因果机制或食管安全性差异；临床上应结合术中食管温度监测与器械操作特点个体化决策，仍需前瞻性、统一操作流程并含内镜评估的研究验证。

Contact Strategies and Isolation Endpoints in Pulsed Field Ablation of Persistent Left Superior Vena Cava: A Case Series.

Pacing Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42784417
房颤与消融 Case Reports Journal Article

Lu Bai、Ruikun Jia、Xinmeng Wang 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

Persistent left superior vena cava (PLSVC) can trigger atrial fibrillation. Thermal ablation of this vessel carries risks of phrenic nerve palsy and coronary artery spasm, whereas pulsed field ablation still lacks procedural guidance for the PLSVC diameter taper, which narrows from the distal segment to a dilated coronary sinus ostium. We report two contact strategies applied in three patients: a guidewire-anchored rail with steerable sheath tension for a fixed-loop catheter, and loop-diameter adjustment for a variable-loop catheter. Acute bidirectional block was confirmed by pacing in all three cases; in one case, dissociated spontaneous potentials provided an endpoint more stringent than atrial fibrillation termination alone. No complications occurred. These findings provide initial guidance for ablating the tapered PLSVC, although long-term durability requires prospective validation.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在3例持续性左上腔静脉相关房颤患者中，脉冲场消融采用导丝锚定轨道联合鞘管张力或环径调整的接触策略，均实现急性双向阻滞且无并发症。

研究动机

持续性左上腔静脉（PLSVC）可作为房颤触发灶，但热消融存在膈神经麻痹和冠状动脉痉挛风险；脉冲场消融（PFA）对该血管从远端狭窄至冠状窦口扩张的锥形解剖尚缺乏操作指导，接触与隔离终点策略不明确。

方法

病例系列，纳入3例PLSVC相关房颤患者。对固定环导管采用导丝锚定轨道并借助可调弯鞘张力维持接触，对可变环导管采用环径调整策略行PFA。以起搏证实的急性双向阻滞作为急性隔离终点，并观察自发电位及并发症；摘要未报告基线特征、消融参数、随访方案及统计方法。

结果

3例均经起搏证实急性双向阻滞；其中1例记录到分离的自发电位，作为较单纯房颤终止更严格的消融终点。摘要报告无并发症发生；未报告效应量、95%CI、P值及随访结果。

结论与意义

该病例系列为锥形PLSVC的PFA接触策略和隔离终点提供了初步操作参考，提示可实现急性双向阻滞且短期安全；但样本量仅3例、缺乏长期随访，长期隔离耐久性需前瞻性研究验证。

Central Composite Design-Optimized Dabigatran Etexilate Cubosomes for Sustained Oral Release and Enhanced In Vivo Pharmacokinetics

Pharm Res·2026-09-24·PMID 42786368
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Bhatt AH、Patel HP、Shaikh PS 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Objectives Dabigatran etexilate mesylate (DEM), a prodrug of the direct thrombin inhibitor dabigatran used for stroke prevention in nonvalvular atrial fibrillation, exhibits poor aqueous solubility, P-glycoprotein-mediated efflux, and limited oral bioavailability, which pose challenges for oral delivery. This study aimed to develop and optimize a cubosome-based nanocarrier system to enhance the oral bioavailability and sustain the release of DEM.Methods DEM-loaded cubosomes were prepared using glycerol monooleate (GMO) and Poloxamer 407 by melt dispersion-emulsification. A Central Composite Design (CCD) was employed to optimize formulation variables based on particle size, entrapment efficiency, and drug release responses. Optimized formulations were characterized by dynamic light scattering, TEM, FT-IR, DSC, and XRD analyses. In vitro drug release and in vivo pharmacokinetic studies in Wistar rats were performed to evaluate sustained release behavior and relative oral bioavailability enhancement.Results The optimized cubosomes exhibited nanoscale particle size (~ 19-30 nm), narrow size distribution, high entrapment efficiency (~ 87%), and sustained biphasic drug release up to 24 h. Structural characterization supported incorporation of DEM within the lipid matrix and reduced drug crystallinity. In vivo pharmacokinetic evaluation demonstrated prolonged plasma exposure and enhanced relative bioavailability compared with the marketed formulation, with approximately 2.28-fold higher relative bioavailability.Conclusions The optimized cubosomal system effectively improved the oral delivery and sustained release of DEM. The formulation demonstrated enhanced relative bioavailability and prolonged systemic exposure, supporting further development for oral DEM delivery. Anticoagulant efficacy, gastrointestinal safety, and appropriate dosing regimens remain to be established.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

研究将以熔融分散-乳化法制备的达比加群酯介孔立方液晶纳米载体（cubosomes）用于口服递送，在大鼠中相对生物利用度较市售制剂提高约2.28倍，体外缓释可达24小时。

研究动机

甲磺酸达比加群酯（DEM）是直接凝血酶抑制剂达比加群的前体药，用于非瓣膜性房颤的卒中预防，但存在水溶性差、P-糖蛋白介导的外排以及口服生物利用度有限等问题，构成口服递送的主要障碍。既往证据缺口在于缺乏一种既能提高生物利用度又能实现缓释的口服纳米载体系统；本研究的动机是开发并优化基于cubosome的纳米载体来解决上述问题。

方法

这是一项制剂开发与临床前药代动力学研究，研究对象为DEM负载的cubosome，采用甘油单油酸酯（GMO）与Poloxamer 407，以熔融分散-乳化法制备。研究采用中心复合设计（CCD），以粒径、包封率和药物释放为响应值，优化处方变量；优化处方经动态光散射、TEM、FT-IR、DSC和XRD表征；并开展体外释放试验与Wistar大鼠体内药代动力学研究，以评估缓释行为及相对口服生物利用度的提升。终点包括粒径、包封率、体外释放曲线、血浆暴露与相对生物利用度。动物样本量、统计方法、置信区间及检验水准在摘要中均未报告。

结果

优化后的cubosome粒径约19–30 nm，粒径分布较窄，包封率约87%，体外呈双相缓释，持续至24小时。结构表征结果支持DEM被包载于脂质基质中，且药物结晶度降低。体内药代动力学显示，与市售制剂相比，优化制剂的血浆暴露时间延长，相对生物利用度提高约2.28倍。关键比较的95%置信区间与P值摘要未报告。

结论与意义

优化后的cubosome系统可有效改善DEM的口服递送并实现缓释，相对生物利用度提高，全身暴露时间延长，支持进一步开发口服DEM递送制剂。局限性在于：本研究为动物水平的制剂学与药代动力学评价，抗凝疗效、胃肠道安全性以及合适给药方案均尚未确立；样本量、统计细节及人体数据摘要未报告。就电生理与房颤抗凝实践而言，该结果提示可能改善达比加群的口服吸收与依从性，但目前尚不能改变临床抗凝决策。

Left Atrial Diameter Index Predicts Cerebrovascular Events after Postoperative Atrial Fibrillation following Coronary Artery Bypass Grafting

Thorac Cardiovasc Surg·2026-09-24·PMID 41237814
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Doğan AC、Demir AR、Yazıcı AA 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

Objective Postoperative atrial fibrillation (POAF) is a frequent complication after coronary artery bypass grafting (CABG) and is linked to increased short- and long-term stroke risk. This study evaluated the prognostic value of the left atrial diameter index (LADI) for predicting cerebrovascular events (CVE) in patients undergoing isolated CABG who developed POAF, had no prior atrial fibrillation (AF), and were discharged in sinus rhythm.Materials and methods Among 2,094 patients undergoing isolated CABG between June 2015 and June 2022, 392 consecutive patients with POAF and discharge in sinus rhythm met inclusion criteria. LADI was calculated preoperatively, and an optimal cutoff (21.2 mm/m2) was determined via receiver operating characteristic curve and Youden index. Patients were classified as low (≤21.2 mm/m2) or high (>21.2 mm/m2) LADI. The association between LADI and CVE was assessed over a mean follow-up of 42.4 ± 20.0 months using Cox proportional hazards modeling.Results Thirty patients (7.7%) experienced a CVE, including eight in-hospital cases (2.0%). CVE incidence was higher in the high LADI group compared with the low LADI group (19.8 vs. 2.8%). Multivariable analysis identified older age, hypertension, congestive heart failure, and high LADI as independent CVE predictors. Kaplan-Meier analysis confirmed significantly higher CVE rates in the high LADI group (log-rank, p < 0.001).Conclusion In patients developing POAF after isolated CABG, LADI is a simple, easily obtainable parameter that independently predicts long-term CVE risk and may aid in postoperative risk stratification.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在接受单纯冠状动脉旁路移植术（CABG）后出现术后房颤（POAF）且出院时为窦性心律的患者中，左心房内径指数（LADI）＞21.2 mm/m² 可独立预测长期脑血管事件，其发生率约为此类指数较低患者的 7 倍。

研究动机

POAF 是 CABG 术后常见并发症，与短期及长期卒中风险升高相关。既往证据未能明确：对无房颤病史、出院时已恢复窦性心律的这部分患者，如何在术后进行简便的卒中风险分层；左心房内径指数（LADI）为术前常规超声即可获得的指标，但其在该人群中的预后价值尚缺乏证据。

方法

单中心回顾性队列研究，纳入 2015 年 6 月至 2022 年 6 月期间接受单纯 CABG 的 2,094 例患者，最终纳入 392 例连续患者；这些患者出现 POAF、出院时为窦性心律且既往无房颤。术前计算 LADI，通过 ROC 曲线与约登指数确定最佳截断值为 21.2 mm/m²，据此分为低值组（≤21.2 mm/m²）与高值组（＞21.2 mm/m²）。终点为脑血管事件（CVE），采用 Cox 比例风险模型评估 LADI 与 CVE 的关联，平均随访 42.4 ± 20.0 个月。

结果

共 30 例（7.7%）发生 CVE，其中院内事件 8 例（2.0%）。高 LADI 组的 CVE 发生率明显高于低 LADI 组（19.8% 比 2.8%）。多因素分析显示，高龄、高血压、充血性心力衰竭和高 LADI 是 CVE 的独立预测因素（具体 HR 与 95%CI 摘要未报告）。Kaplan-Meier 分析证实，高 LADI 组的 CVE 累积发生率显著更高（log-rank 检验，P＜0.001）。

结论与意义

在接受单纯 CABG 后发生 POAF、且出院时为窦性心律的患者中，LADI 是简便易得且能独立预测长期 CVE 风险的指标，有助于术后风险分层，可能提示对高 LADI 患者加强随访或延长抗凝、监测策略。局限在于：本研究为单中心回顾性设计，存在选择偏倚，多因素效应量未报告，且截断值 21.2 mm/m² 缺乏外部验证，尚不能直接据此改变临床决策。

Multimodal Characterization of Atrial Fibrillation: From Patient-Specific Anatomy and Electrophysiology to Standardized Atrial Mapping.

Tomography·2026-09-24·PMID 42784270
房颤与消融 Journal Article Review

Tiantian Wang、Quan Zou、Huan Yang

英文摘要

This narrative review synthesizes a comprehensive multimodal characterization framework for atrial fibrillation (AF), tracing its progression from patient-specific anatomical reconstruction to electrophysiological phenotyping and standardized spatial mapping. The literature was identified through searches of PubMed, Web of Science, and Google Scholar, covering publications from January 2010 to June 2026, with additional studies identified from relevant references. Anatomical characterization leverages clinical imaging modalities alongside advanced deep learning models to execute precise whole-chamber, subregional, and tissue-level modeling. Electrophysiological profiling spans multi-scale modalities, including 12-lead electrocardiography (ECG), body surface potential mapping (BSPM), electrocardiographic imaging (ECGI), and electroanatomical mapping (EAM), complemented by wearable sensors for longitudinal rhythm surveillance. To bridge heterogeneous datasets across subjects and modalities, standardized coordinate systems and multimodal registration enable reproducible data integration. These integrated data parameterize patient-specific computational models to advance mechanistic insight, risk stratification, and personalized AF management. However, broad clinical translation remains constrained by imaging variability, reconstruction noise, registration uncertainty, and limited prospective multicenter validation. Future progress hinges on moving beyond technical accuracy toward prospective trials evaluating algorithm-guided clinical utility.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本叙述性综述提出房颤多模态表征框架：从患者个体化解剖重建、电生理表型分型到标准化空间标测，指出其临床转化仍受影像变异与缺乏前瞻性多中心验证的限制。

研究动机

房颤的机制高度异质，导管消融策略制定与风险分层均依赖对心房结构和电生理的精准刻画。既往证据多局限于单一模态（临床影像或电解剖标测），缺乏跨模态、跨个体的可重复数据整合方案，使患者特异性计算模型难以真正进入临床决策，这是本综述试图弥补的缺口。

方法

研究类型为叙述性综述（narrative review），摘要未报告纳入文献数量及筛选流程细节。检索 PubMed、Web of Science 与 Google Scholar，覆盖 2010 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月的文献，并追溯相关参考文献补充。综述框架包括三部分：一是解剖表征，用临床影像模态联合深度学习模型，行全心腔、亚区及组织水平建模；二是电生理表征，涵盖 12 导联 ECG、体表电位标测（BSPM）、心电成像（ECGI）与三维电解剖标测（EAM），并辅以可穿戴传感器进行长程节律监测；三是通过标准化坐标系与多模态配准，实现跨受试者、跨模态的数据整合，用于参数化患者特异性计算模型。结局为框架构建与转化障碍的定性归纳，摘要未报告统计方法。

结果

摘要未报告定量结果，未给出效应量、95%CI 或 P 值。定性归纳显示，整合后的多模态数据可参数化患者特异性计算模型，服务于机制探索、风险分层与个体化房颤管理；主要转化障碍为影像模态间变异性、解剖重建噪声、多模态配准不确定性，以及缺乏前瞻性多中心验证。

结论与意义

多模态表征为房颤机制研究与个体化管理提供了统一的数据基础，但现有证据仍停留在技术可行性与回顾性层面，尚未证明可改善临床结局。对电生理实践的意义在于，它有望优化术前解剖与基质评估、辅助消融策略制定；未来研究需从追求算法精度转向以前瞻性试验验证算法引导的临床获益。局限性包括：叙述性综述固有的文献选择偏倚，纳入研究的数量与质量评价摘要未报告，且缺乏对各模态诊断效能的定量比较。

Antidepressant use and the risk of atrial fibrillation and ventricular arrhythmia: A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Eur J Clin Pharmacol·2026-09-23·PMID 42778795
房颤与消融 Journal Article Systematic Review Meta-Analysis

Aimen Amirat、Mousa Almasalma、Alaa Ashraf Mohamed 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Antidepressants (ADs) have been associated with adverse cardiovascular effects, including cardiac arrhythmias. Evidence on the association between AD use and the risk of atrial fibrillation (AF) and ventricular arrhythmia/sudden cardiac death (VA/SCD) remains inconsistent. This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to evaluate the relationship between AD use and the risk of AF and VA/SCD. METHODS: A detailed search was conducted in PubMed, Scopus, and Web of Science till June 2026. Cohort and case-control studies comparing AD users with non-users and reporting AF, VA, or SCD as outcomes were included. Pooled odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated using a random-effects model. RESULTS: Twelve studies with 9,619,278 participants were included. AD use was associated with a significantly increased risk of VA/SCD (9 studies, 6,373,522 participants, OR = 1.34, 95% CI: 1.15-1.55; I² = 90.5%). The association remained significant for both SSRIs and TCAs. For AF, no overall significant association emerged (3 Studies, 3,245,756 participants, OR = 1.75, 95% CI: 0.84-3.62; I² = 98.1%). However, sensitivity analysis excluding one influential study showed a significant association with AF (2 Studies, 18,109 participants, OR = 1.27, 95% CI: 1.07-1.50) and eliminated heterogeneity (I² = 0%). CONCLUSION: This meta-analysis suggests an association between AD use and an increased risk of VA/SCD, while evidence for AF remains uncertain because the results were influenced by a single study. Future large observational studies that better address confounding by indication and adequately adjust for depression are needed to provide more robust evidence.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

纳入 12 项研究、共 961 万余名受试者的荟萃分析显示，使用抗抑郁药与室性心律失常/心脏性猝死风险升高相关（OR 1.34，95%CI 1.15-1.55），而与房颤的关联受单项研究影响、尚不确定。

研究动机

抗抑郁药（AD）被认为存在包括心律失常在内的心血管不良反应，但既往关于 AD 使用与房颤（AF）及室性心律失常/心脏性猝死（VA/SCD）风险的证据并不一致，缺乏系统性的定量合并评估，临床在处方 AD 时难以权衡心律失常风险。

方法

系统综述与荟萃分析。检索 PubMed、Scopus 和 Web of Science，截止至 2026 年 6 月；纳入比较 AD 使用者与非使用者、并报告 AF、VA 或 SCD 结局的队列研究与病例对照研究。采用随机效应模型合并比值比（OR）及 95% 置信区间（CI），以 I² 评估异质性。摘要未报告偏倚风险评估工具、纳入研究的随访时长及按具体药物类别分层的预设方案。

结果

共纳入 12 项研究、9,619,278 名受试者。AD 使用与 VA/SCD 风险显著升高相关（9 项研究，6,373,522 人，OR=1.34，95%CI 1.15-1.55；I²=90.5%），SSRIs 与 TCAs 亚组中该关联均仍然显著。对于 AF，总体关联未达统计学显著（3 项研究，3,245,756 人，OR=1.75，95%CI 0.84-3.62；I²=98.1%）；但剔除 1 项影响较大的研究后，敏感性分析显示 AF 风险显著升高（2 项研究，18,109 人，OR=1.27，95%CI 1.07-1.50），且异质性消失（I²=0%）。

结论与意义

该荟萃分析提示，AD 使用与 VA/SCD 风险升高相关，而 AF 方面的证据因结果高度依赖单项研究而仍不确定。对电生理实践而言，提示在处方 AD（包括 SSRI 与 TCA）时应评估患者的室性心律失常与猝死风险，尤其对合并结构性心脏病或 ICD 植入者需加强随访与心电监测；但现有证据均为观察性研究，难以排除适应证混杂（confounding by indication），且抑郁本身可能未被充分校正，AF 相关研究数量少、异质性极高，上述结论需更大样本、校正更充分的前瞻性观察研究进一步验证。

Atrial fibrillation and atrial flutter in China: trends and projections to 2050 from GBD 2023.

Intern Emerg Med·2026-09-23·PMID 42778865
房颤与消融 Journal Article

Rui Wu、Zijun Zhou、Boxuan Sun 等 6 位作者

英文摘要

Atrial fibrillation and atrial flutter (AF/AFL) impose a mounting health burden worldwide, yet no comprehensive study has systematically evaluated their long‑term trajectories and future scenarios for China using the latest Global Burden of Disease (GBD) data. Using the GBD 2023 dataset, we extracted age- and sex-specific burden estimates of AF/AFL for China (1990-2023). Joinpoint regression, age-period-cohort modeling, decomposition analysis, frontier analysis, and autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA) forecasting were applied to characterize historical dynamics and future trajectories. In 2023, China had 12.39 million prevalent cases, 1.06 million incident cases, 65,008 deaths, and 1.75 million disability‑adjusted life years (DALYs). Males had higher age‑standardized prevalence rate (ASPR) (614.89 vs. 472.17) and age‑standardized incidence rate (ASIR) (49.22 vs. 43.00), while females had higher age‑standardized mortality rate (ASMR) (3.69 vs. 2.52) per 100,000. The age‑standardized DALY rate (ASDR) was slightly higher in males (79.24) than in females (77.34) per 100,000. The mortality rate, which fell substantially during 1998-2004 (annual percentage change [APC]: - 5.46; 95% confidence interval [CI]: - 6.28 to - 4.64), unexpectedly reversed to a sharp rise after 2020 (APC: 3.91; 95% CI: 2.23 to 5.62). Decomposition analysis identified population aging and demographic growth as the predominant drivers of the increasing burden. Frontier analysis revealed a persistent gap between observed burden and achievable benchmarks. ARIMA models project the burden will remain substantial through 2050, with sustained nonfatal morbidity. China's AF/AFL burden more than doubled from 1990 to 2023, driven by aging and population growth. The post-2020 mortality reversal and persistently high nonfatal burden highlight the urgent need for age- and sex-tailored prevention, early detection, and enhanced chronic care strategies.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

基于GBD 2023数据，中国AF/AFL患病数从1990年至2023年翻倍以上，2023年达1239万例，死亡负担在2020年后由长期下降转为上升（APC 3.91），预计至2050年仍维持高位。

研究动机

AF/AFL 全球疾病负担持续上升，但此前缺乏基于最新 GBD 2023 数据、系统评估中国长期轨迹与未来情景的研究，难以为分层防控与慢病管理资源配置提供依据。

方法

基于 GBD 2023 数据库提取中国 1990–2023 年 AF/AFL 的年龄别、性别别负担估计（患病、发病、死亡、DALY 及年龄标化率）。采用 Joinpoint 回归描述时间趋势（以年度变化百分比 APC 表示），并行年龄-时期-队列模型、分解分析（量化人口老龄化、人口增长与流行病学变化贡献）、前沿分析（比较实际负担与可达标化基准的差距）及 ARIMA 模型预测至 2050 年。摘要未报告具体建模协变量与模型拟合优度指标。

结果

2023 年中国 AF/AFL 患病 1239 万例、发病 106 万例、死亡 65,008 例、DALY 175 万。男性年龄标化患病率（614.89/10 万 vs 472.17/10 万）与发病率（49.22/10 万 vs 43.00/10 万）高于女性；女性年龄标化死亡率更高（3.69/10 万 vs 2.52/10 万）；年龄标化 DALY 率男性略高（79.24/10 万 vs 77.34/10 万）。死亡率在 1998–2004 年显著下降（APC −5.46，95%CI −6.28～−4.64），但 2020 年后逆转为快速上升（APC 3.91，95%CI 2.23～5.62）。分解分析显示人口老龄化与人口增长是负担上升的主要驱动因素；前沿分析提示实际负担与可达基准间存在持续差距。ARIMA 预测至 2050 年负担仍将沉重，非致死性致残负担持续存在。

结论与意义

中国 AF/AFL 负担在 1990–2023 年间翻倍以上，主要由老龄化与人口增长驱动；2020 年后死亡率反弹及持续高企的非致死性负担，提示需按年龄与性别制定预防、早期筛查（如机会性筛查与可穿戴设备监测）与慢性病全程管理策略，并强化抗凝与节律/心率控制等规范化治疗。局限性在于本研究为 GBD 建模估计而非真实世界登记数据，结果受原始数据质量与模型假设影响；摘要未报告 2020 年后死亡率上升的具体归因分析。

Correlation of QT Dispersion in Patients with Acute Myocardial Infarction and Reperfusion Arrhythmias Following Primary Percutaneous Coronary Intervention: A Prospective Study.

Ann Afr Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42784429
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Kabilash Manivalli Peterpalaniswami、Aravind C S Chandrasekaran、Krishnaswamy Madhavan 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Reperfusion arrhythmias are commonly encountered following primary percutaneous coronary intervention (PPCI) in patients with acute myocardial infarction (AMI). QT dispersion (QTd) and corrected QTd (cQTd) are noninvasive electrocardiographic markers reflecting heterogeneity of ventricular repolarization and may serve as predictors of electrical instability and arrhythmic risk following reperfusion. OBJECTIVES: The objective of this study was to evaluate the association between QTd and reperfusion arrhythmias following PPCI in patients with AMI, and to assess the temporal changes in QTd and cQTd after reperfusion. MATERIALS AND METHODS: This prospective observational study included 125 patients with AMI undergoing PPCI. Standard 12-lead electrocardiograms were recorded at admission, immediately after PPCI, and at discharge for measurement of QTd and cQTd. Continuous Holter monitoring was used to identify reperfusion arrhythmias, which were categorized as major or minor arrhythmias. Clinical, electrocardiographic, and angiographic parameters were analyzed for association with QTd and cQTd values. RESULTS: Frequent premature ventricular complexes were the most common reperfusion arrhythmia (44.8%), followed by accelerated idioventricular rhythm (15.2%) and nonsustained ventricular tachycardia (12.8%). Receiver-operating characteristic curve analysis revealed that corrected QTd at discharge had moderate predictive ability for major reperfusion arrhythmias with an area under the curve of 0.648. A cutoff value of 69.49 ms yielded a sensitivity of 43.5% and specificity of 87.3% for predicting major arrhythmias. Both QTd and cQTd demonstrated a progressive decline from admission to discharge following PPCI. Mean cQTd decreased from 114.09 ms at admission to 80.52 ms post-PPCI and 58.18 ms at discharge. Patients with major reperfusion arrhythmias had significantly higher cQTd values at admission (130.76 ± 41.64 ms vs 110.33 ± 24.18 ms; P = 0.032), post-PPCI (93.75 ± 29.59 ms vs 77.50 ± 16.94 ms; P = 0.0175), and discharge (66.62 ± 18.89 ms vs. 56.27 ± 11.30 ms; P = 0.018) compared to patients with minor arrhythmias. Increasing Killip class was associated with progressively higher discharge cQTd values. Smokers also demonstrated significantly higher discharge cQTd values compared to nonsmokers (P = 0.0061). CONCLUSION: QTd and corrected QTd significantly decrease following successful reperfusion with PPCI. Elevated cQTd values were associated with major reperfusion arrhythmias, suggesting that cQTd may serve as a useful noninvasive marker for predicting arrhythmic risk following PPCI in AMI patients. Increased cQTd was also associated with greater clinical severity as reflected by higher Killip class.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在125例急性心肌梗死行直接PCI的患者中，校正QT离散度（cQTd）随再灌注成功而逐步下降，且发生重大再灌注心律失常者各时点cQTd均显著更高。

研究动机

再灌注心律失常是急性心肌梗死直接PCI（PPCI）后常见临床问题，既往缺乏简便可靠的非侵入性预警指标。QT离散度（QTd）与校正QTd（cQTd）反映心室复极异质性，能否预测再灌注后电不稳定性及心律失常风险尚不明确，本研究旨在填补这一证据缺口。

方法

前瞻性观察性研究，纳入125例接受PPCI的急性心肌梗死患者。于入院时、PPCI后即刻及出院时记录标准12导联心电图，测量QTd与cQTd；采用连续Holter监测识别再灌注心律失常，并分为重大与次要心律失常两类；分析临床、心电图及冠状动脉造影参数与QTd、cQTd的关联。预测能力用ROC曲线评价。

结果

频繁室性早搏最常见（44.8%），其次为加速性室性自主心律（15.2%）和非持续性室性心动过速（12.8%）。出院时cQTd预测重大再灌注心律失常的AUC为0.648，界值69.49 ms时敏感度43.5%、特异度87.3%。cQTd均值由入院时114.09 ms降至PPCI后80.52 ms、出院时58.18 ms。重大心律失常组各时点cQTd均显著高于次要心律失常组：入院130.76±41.64 ms vs 110.33±24.18 ms（P=0.032），PPCI后93.75±29.59 ms vs 77.50±16.94 ms（P=0.0175），出院时66.62±18.89 ms vs 56.27±11.30 ms（P=0.018）。Killip分级越高，出院cQTd越高；吸烟者出院cQTd显著高于非吸烟者（P=0.0061）。

结论与意义

成功的PPCI再灌注后QTd与cQTd显著下降，提示复极异质性随再灌注改善。cQTd升高与重大再灌注心律失常及更高Killip分级相关，可作为AMI患者PPCI后心律失常风险的无创预警指标；但其预测效能仅为中等（AUC 0.648），敏感度偏低，且为单中心小样本观察性研究，尚需更大规模研究验证后方可用于临床决策。

A Giant in Plain Sight: Multimodality Imaging of a Left Ventricular Fibroma.

Cureus·2026-09-24·PMID 42781698
室性心律失常 Case Reports Journal Article

Michael Sammaan、Michael Moore、Suhair Shebani

英文摘要

A 14-year-old survivor of out-of-hospital ventricular fibrillation arrest with a large intramural left ventricular fibroma and a secondary-prevention implantable cardioverter-defibrillator underwent multimodality reassessment after device lead fracture. Left ventricular ejection fraction was mildly reduced, whereas speckle-tracking echocardiography demonstrated near-akinetic deformation within tumour-bearing segments. Cardiac computed tomography and three-dimensional reconstruction showed the left circumflex coronary artery and first obtuse marginal branch coursing directly over the mass, with additional proximity to the lateral mitral apparatus. These findings demonstrated substantial regional mechanical impairment while defining the hazards of complete resection. Following multidisciplinary review, device protection and imaging surveillance were favoured over high-risk surgery. This case illustrates how complementary functional and anatomical imaging can resolve a complex management dilemma in ventricular fibroma.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例14岁院外心室颤动复苏幸存者，左室壁内巨大纤维瘤合并继发性预防ICD，在导线断裂后经多模态影像再评估，最终选择保留器械联合影像随访而非高风险手术。

研究动机

左室纤维瘤属罕见良性心脏肿瘤，可致室性心律失常甚至心脏性猝死；此类患者常需ICD二级预防，但器械相关并发症（如导线断裂）会迫使重新评估治疗方案。完整切除肿瘤虽可根治，但术前需明确肿瘤与冠状动脉、二尖瓣装置的关系及局部心肌机械功能受损程度，既往缺乏将功能学与解剖学影像整合用于手术风险决策的系统描述。

方法

单例病例报告（样本量1例）。对象为14岁院外心室颤动心脏骤停幸存者，患左室壁内巨大纤维瘤，已植入二级预防ICD。因ICD导线断裂而行多模态影像再评估：超声心动图评估左室射血分数，斑点追踪超声评估肿瘤累及节段的形变；心脏CT及三维重建评估肿瘤与左回旋支、第一钝缘支及二尖瓣侧方装置的空间毗邻关系。终点为手术可切除性与风险的判定，最终经多学科讨论形成治疗决策。摘要未报告具体统计方法。

结果

左室射血分数轻度降低；斑点追踪超声显示肿瘤累及节段形变近乎无运动（近无运动状态），提示显著的区域机械功能受损。心脏CT三维重建显示左回旋支及第一钝缘支直接走行于瘤体表面，且瘤体与二尖瓣侧方结构邻近。上述解剖与功能学发现界定了完整切除的高风险性。摘要未报告具体量化数值、95%可信区间或P值。

结论与意义

经多学科评估后，选择器械保护（ICD）联合影像学随访，而非高风险外科切除。该病例提示，功能学与解剖学影像互补可在室性心律失常相关心脏肿瘤的复杂决策中明确手术危险、支持保守策略；局限在于仅为单例报告，缺乏长期随访与结果数据，结论不可外推。

Case Report: Brugada syndrome uncovered by delivery in a 32-year-old patient.

Front Cardiovasc Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42780895
室性心律失常 Case Reports Journal Article

Peng Liu、Ruilong Gao、Yaliu Yang 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

We report a 32-year-old primigravida with no prior symptoms, normal baseline ECG, and no family history of sudden death who developed ventricular fibrillation (VF) shortly after uncomplicated vaginal delivery. She received epidural ropivacaine and sufentanil during labor. Post-resuscitation ECG demonstrated coved ST-segment elevation with T-wave inversion in V1-V2, consistent with spontaneous type 1 Brugada syndrome (BrS) pattern. Echocardiography excluded structural heart disease; coronary CT angiography excluded coronary artery disease. Laboratory investigations revealed borderline hypokalemia (3.5 mmol/L) and mild hypomagnesemia (0.75 mmol/L). Clinical whole-exome sequencing revealed no pathogenic or likely pathogenic variants in established arrhythmia-associated genes. The ECG normalized two days later. An implantable cardioverter-defibrillator was implanted. This case illustrates multifactorial unmasking of latent BrS-involving ropivacaine-mediated sodium channel blockade, postpartum autonomic instability, bradycardia, and electrolyte imbalance in a genotype-negative asymptomatic woman, highlighting the importance of recognizing concealed channelopathies in peripartum cardiac arrest.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

32岁初产妇既往无症状、基线心电图正常，顺产后不久发生室颤，复苏后V1-V2出现穹隆型ST段抬高，符合自发1型Brugada综合征，基因检测阴性，最终植入ICD。

研究动机

围产期心脏骤停的病因鉴别困难，隐匿性离子通道病在妊娠及产褥期可被麻醉药物、自主神经波动和电解质紊乱共同诱发而暴露。既往对Brugada综合征（BrS）围产期“揭面”的认识多来自典型基线心电图或基因阳性患者，而对基线心电图正常、基因阴性的无症状女性缺乏警示，易漏诊并延误二级预防。

方法

个案报道，样本量1例。对象为32岁初产妇（无既往症状、无猝死家族史、基线心电图正常），分娩时接受硬膜外罗哌卡因与舒芬太尼镇痛，顺产后短时间内发生室颤并复苏成功。评估包括复苏后12导联心电图、超声心动图、冠状动脉CT血管造影、血钾血镁检测及临床全外显子测序；观察终点为心律失常事件及诊断与器械治疗决策。摘要未报告统计分析（无效应量、95%CI或P值）。

结果

复苏后心电图示V1-V2穹隆型ST段抬高伴T波倒置，符合自发1型BrS图形；超声心动图排除结构性心脏病，冠状动脉CTA排除冠心病；实验室检查示血钾3.5 mmol/L（临界低钾）、血镁0.75 mmol/L（轻度低镁）；全外显子测序未在已知致心律失常相关基因中发现致病或可能致病变异（基因阴性）。心电图于2天后恢复正常，随后植入植入式心律转复除颤器（ICD）。摘要未报告随访时长及器械相关结局。

结论与意义

本病例提示潜伏BrS可在围产期通过多因素叠加而被“揭面”：罗哌卡因介导的钠通道阻滞、产后自主神经不稳定、心动过缓及电解质紊乱；基线心电图正常与基因阴性均不能排除诊断。对围产期心脏骤停存活者应高度警惕隐匿性通道病，产褥期应加强心电监测与复查心电图，并按危险分层考虑ICD作为二级预防。局限性为单例报道、无法确立因果关联，且缺乏随访数据与系统筛查结果。

Post-induction residual time: a novel sensing phenomenon during induced ventricular fibrillation in subcutaneous ICD recipients

Front Cardiovasc Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42851273
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Lopez C、Mondragon I、Benjamin M 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Background Subcutaneous implantable cardioverter defibrillators (S-ICD) are extensively used in the primary and secondary prevention of sudden cardiac death. Ventricular fibrillation (VF) induction during implantation remains standard to confirm device functionality.Objective To compare electrocardiographic (ECG) and technical variables between induced and spontaneous ventricular arrhythmias in S-ICD recipients.Methods A retrospective observational study was performed on 10 consecutive patients with S-ICD. Variables included episode duration, sensing time, charge time, and shock impedance. For each patient, the implant induced VF episode was compared to at least one spontaneous event during follow-up.Results The mean age was 36.4 years (range: 22-61); 2 patients were male. All patients received intermuscular S-ICD implants, with the lead placed in the left parasternal position in 7 patients and right parasternal in 3. Episode duration and sensing time were consistently shorter in spontaneous events, and shock impedance was lower. Charge time, however, was longer in spontaneous events (by 1.4 to 1.8 s).Conclusions In this case series, spontaneous ventricular arrhythmic events in S-ICD patients demonstrated shorter sensing and therapy delivery times compared with induced ventricular fibrillation episodes. A measurable Post-Induction Residual Time (PRT) was identified in all induced episodes and was absent during spontaneous ventricular arrhythmias, suggesting that 50-Hz-induced VF may not fully reproduce spontaneous arrhythmic events from the perspective of device sensing performance. These findings support the hypothesis that part of the delay observed during implant testing may be related to the induction process itself rather than to the intrinsic sensing capabilities of the S-ICD. Further studies are warranted to confirm these observations and to evaluate alternative VF induction strategies in future device platforms. Future integration of S-ICD systems with leadless pacing technologies may provide alternative ventricular fibrillation induction strategies, including T-wave shock delivery during either sensed intrinsic rhythm or leadless-paced rhythm. Such approaches could potentially avoid the occurrence of Post-Induction Residual Time (PRT) and may provide a model that more closely reproduces spontaneous ventricular fibrillation than current 50-Hz induction techniques.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 S-ICD 植入术中经 50 Hz 交流电诱发的室颤，其感知时间与放电时间均长于随访中自发的室性心律失常事件；所有诱发事件均出现“诱发后残留时间（PRT）”，而自发事件中并无 PRT，提示诱发室颤未能完全模拟自发心律失常下的装置感知表现。

研究动机

S-ICD 已广泛用于心脏性猝死的一级与二级预防，植入时经 50 Hz 诱颤验证装置功能仍是常规流程。但诱发室颤能否真实反映自发室性心律失常时的感知与治疗性能，既往证据有限；若诱发方式本身造成感知延迟，可能导致对装置性能的低估，并影响植入测试的判读标准。

方法

单中心回顾性观察性研究，连续纳入 10 例接受肌间植入 S-ICD 的患者（平均年龄 36.4 岁，范围 22–61 岁；男性 2 例）；导线经左胸骨旁置入 7 例、右胸骨旁 3 例。比较指标包括事件持续时间、感知时间（sensing time）、充电时间（charge time）与放电阻抗（shock impedance）。分析方法为将每位患者植入术中诱发的室颤事件，与随访期间至少一次自发事件进行自身对照比较。终点定义及统计检验方法摘要未报告。

结果

与诱发室颤相比，自发事件的持续时间与感知时间均一致更短，放电阻抗更低；但自发事件的充电时间更长，延长 1.4–1.8 s。所有诱发事件中均可测得“诱发后残留时间（PRT）”，而自发室性心律失常中未出现 PRT。摘要未报告具体时长的数值、95% 置信区间及 P 值。

结论与意义

本研究提示：植入测试中观察到的部分延迟可能源于 50 Hz 诱颤过程本身，而非 S-ICD 固有感知能力不足；PRT 可作为识别诱颤与自发事件差异的感知学指标。该发现支持重新审视以 50 Hz 诱颤作为装置感知性能验证金标准的合理性，并提示未来可将 S-ICD 与无导线起搏技术整合，采用感知自身心律或起搏心律下发放 T 波电击等替代诱颤策略，以避免 PRT。局限为样本量仅 10 例、回顾性设计、单中心、缺乏统计检验与疗效终点，结论需更大样本研究验证。

Pharmacological management and outcomes of sustained ventricular tachycardia in children and adolescents: a single-center retrospective study.

Front Pediatr·2026-09-24·PMID 42780630
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Jinghao Li、Hong Wang、Yanlin Xing 等 6 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Evidence related to the efficacy and prognosis of pharmacologic therapy for sustained ventricular tachycardia (VT) in the pediatric population remains scarce. This single-center retrospective study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic outcomes and drug sensitivity of sustained VT in children and adolescents. METHODS: We analyzed patients hospitalized due to sustained VT at the Department of Pediatric Cardiology, Shengjing Hospital of China Medical University, between February 2018 and September 2025. Patients were prescribed antiarrhythmic drugs according to electrocardiogram (ECG) morphology. Electrocardiographic and echocardiographic data, cardioversion success rate, and adverse events were recorded. Following VT termination, oral antiarrhythmic drugs were initiated for VT prevention. RESULTS: In total, 29 patients (18 boys, 11 girls) were included, with a mean age of 8.3 ± 4.6 years. The patients were diagnosed with idiopathic VT (n = 25), VT associated with myocarditis (n = 3), or VT associated with structural heart disease (n = 1). According to ECG morphology, patients were classified as presumed left fascicular VT (n = 21, 72.4%), outflow tract VT (n = 2, 6.9%), polymorphic VT (n = 3, 10.3%), and other-origin VT (n = 3, 10.3%). A total of 38 sustained VT episodes occurred among these patients. Four episodes of VT terminated without antiarrhythmic intervention, while the remaining 34 episodes were successfully cardioverted under medical intervention, with monotherapy effective in 25 episodes (73.5%). Intravenous verapamil was effective for presumed left fascicular VT (success rate of 92.3%). During a mean follow-up period of 2.1 ± 1.7 years, 41.4% of the patients had complete resolution of VT with drug discontinued, while the remainders still required antiarrhythmic drugs. Drug therapy alone resulted in a higher complete resolution rate in patients with infantile onset (<1 year) compared to those with older onset (66.6% vs. 13.0%, P = 0.006). Transient, reversible cardiac dysfunction occurred in only three patients. All patients maintained cardiac function well during follow-up, and no sudden cardiac death was recorded. CONCLUSION: Idiopathic VT is the predominant type of sustained VT in children and adolescents. In this study, the vast majority of the cases were left fascicular VT. Patients with presumed left fascicular VT exhibit sensitivity to intravenous verapamil, although clinicians must remain vigilant of transient hypotension. The long-term follow-up results revealed an overall favorable prognosis in pediatric VT.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

单中心29例儿童青少年持续性室速回顾性研究显示，特发性室速占绝大多数（25/29），其中左分支型室速占72.4%；静脉维拉帕米对推测左分支型室速有效率达92.3%，长期随访41.4%患者停药后室速完全消失，预后良好。

研究动机

儿童及青少年持续性室速（VT）的药物治疗证据及相关预后数据极为匮乏，现有指南推荐多沿用成人经验；本研究旨在评估该人群持续性VT的药物治疗转复效果、药物敏感性及长期结局，为儿科电生理用药策略提供依据。

方法

单中心回顾性研究，纳入中国医科大学附属盛京医院小儿心内科2018年2月至2025年9月因持续性VT住院的患者，共29例（男18例、女11例），平均年龄8.3±4.6岁。依据心电图形态选择抗心律失常药物，记录心电图、超声心动图数据、转复成功率与不良事件；VT终止后口服抗心律失常药物预防复发。统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

病因构成：特发性VT 25例、心肌炎相关VT 3例、结构性心脏病相关VT 1例。按心电图形态分为推测左分支型VT 21例（72.4%）、流出道VT 2例（6.9%）、多形性VT 3例（10.3%）、其他起源3例（10.3%）。共38次持续性VT发作，其中4次未用抗心律失常药物自行终止，其余34次在药物干预下转复成功，单药有效25次（73.5%）；静脉维拉帕米对推测左分支型VT成功率为92.3%。平均随访2.1±1.7年，41.4%的患者停药后VT完全消失，其余患者仍需服药。婴儿期起病（<1岁）者单纯药物治疗的完全缓解率显著高于年长起病者（66.6% vs. 13.0%，P=0.006）。仅3例出现短暂可逆的心功能障碍；随访期间所有患者心功能维持良好，无心脏性猝死发生。

结论与意义

儿童青少年持续性VT以特发性为主，本组以左分支型VT最常见；推测左分支型VT对静脉维拉帕米敏感，但须警惕一过性低血压。整体长期预后良好，婴儿期起病者药物缓解率更高。本研究为单中心小样本回顾性设计，缺乏对照组与系统统计方法描述，维拉帕米有效率及年龄相关差异需更大样本前瞻性研究验证。

Acute effects of physical exercise in patients with arrhythmogenic cardiomyopathy harboring plakophilin-2 variants.

Heart Rhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42785606
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Fernando G Beltrami、Kyle G P J M Boyle、Elsie L C Nix 等 9 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Despite the connection between physical exercise and arrhythmogenic right ventricular cardiomyopathy (ARVC), there are no data on the safety and suitability of exercise for patients. OBJECTIVE: To quantify the incidence and complexity of premature ventricular contractions (PVCs) and cardiometabolic load during different exercises in patients with ARVC with pathogenic plakophilin-2 variants. METHODS: 25 participants with definite ARVC (9 female and 16 male) performed different exercises while cardiorespiratory function was monitored. Resistance exercises included 2-legged squats and unilateral biceps curls, whereas endurance exercises included walking and cycling at a heart rate of 80, 100, and 120 bpm. Each activity was followed by 10 minutes of recovery. RESULTS: PVC burden more than doubled (P < .001) during the early recovery phase than in the active exercise phases, as did the occurrence of ≥ 3 PVC morphologies (52% vs 84%, P = .03). Only unilateral biceps curls did not increase PVC burden compared with rest (P = .361). Resistance exercises combined lower cardiac load and arrhythmic burden for comparable effort levels. Exercise intensity classification showed large fluctuations, from "light" to "high intensity" for the same task, depending on the metric used. CONCLUSIONS: Early recovery monitoring is essential to properly assess the arrhythmic burden of exercise in plakophilin-2 ARVC. Small muscle mass exercises show the lowest PVC burden and cardiac load-surrogate markers of arrhythmic risk and disease progression-and therefore offer a promising path for prescribing exercise in this population. Exercise intensity guidelines for ARVC are insufficiently clear and should be refined.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在25例携带致病性PKP2变异的确诊ARVC患者中，运动后早期恢复期室性早搏负荷较运动期翻倍以上，且≥3种形态的PVC比例升高；仅小肌群单侧肱二头肌弯举未增加PVC负荷。

研究动机

运动与致心律失常性右室心肌病（ARVC）的关系已被认识，但对于已确诊ARVC患者，何种运动方式与强度是安全的、如何个体化运动处方，既往缺乏直接证据。本研究针对致病性plakophilin-2（PKP2）变异这一最常见基因型人群，试图量化不同运动形式下的室性早搏（PVC）发生情况与心脏代谢负荷，填补运动安全性数据的空白。

方法

单中心前瞻性观察研究，纳入25例确诊ARVC且携带致病性PKP2变异的患者（女9例、男16例），在全程心肺功能监测下完成不同运动方案：抗阻运动包括双腿深蹲与单侧肱二头肌弯举；耐力运动包括步行与骑行，靶心率分别为80、100、120 bpm；每项活动后均记录10分钟恢复期。主要观察指标为各阶段PVC负荷（PVC数量/复杂度）及心脏代谢负荷替代指标，并比较不同运动强度分级标准下同一任务的强度归类差异。摘要未报告具体统计方法细节。

结果

早期恢复期PVC负荷较运动进行期升高逾一倍（P<0.001，具体倍数与95%CI摘要未报告）；出现≥3种PVC形态的患者比例由运动期的52%升至恢复期的84%（P=0.03）。与其他运动形式不同，单侧肱二头肌弯举未较静息状态增加PVC负荷（P=0.361）。在主观用力程度相当的情况下，抗阻运动的心脏负荷与心律失常负荷均较低。此外，同一运动任务按不同强度评价指标可被归为“低强度”至“高强度”，分级波动很大。

结论与意义

对PKP2相关ARVC患者，运动后早期恢复期是心律失常负荷最集中的窗口，仅监测运动期会低估PVC负担，应将恢复期心电监测纳入运动评估与随访。小肌群抗阻运动（如单侧肱二头肌弯举）PVC负荷与心脏负荷最低，可能是该人群运动处方的优先选项。现有ARVC运动强度指南标准不一致、界定不清，需进一步细化。局限性：样本量小（25例）、缺乏对照组与长期结局，PVC负荷为替代终点，结论需更大样本与预后研究验证。

Coronary Arterial Anomalies in Adults with Congenital Heart Disease: From Detection to Management Strategies.

英文摘要

Congenital coronary artery anomalies, whether native, associated with structural heart defects, or acquired following surgical repair, are an increasingly recognized cause of morbidity and sudden cardiac death in the expanding adult congenital heart disease (ACHD) population. Their clinical significance is heightened as the first generation of arterial switch operation (ASO) recipients reaches adulthood. This review presents a practical, evidence-based framework for the classification, multimodality imaging evaluation, risk stratification, and surgical and interventional management of coronary anomalies across the ACHD spectrum. 一句话结论

该综述提出成人先天性心脏病（ACHD）合并冠状动脉异常的实用循证管理框架，整合2025 ACC/AHA与2020 ESC指南，指出对无症状患者的处理策略存在分歧，并强调动脉换位术（ASO）世代长期冠脉结局尚不明确。

研究动机

先天性冠状动脉异常（包括原发、合并结构畸形或术后获得性）在持续扩大的ACHD人群中日益被视为致病与心源性猝死的重要原因；随着首批ASO受者进入成年期，其临床意义进一步凸显。既往证据多为单中心经验，分类与风险评估标准不统一，且2025 ACC/AHA与2020 ESC指南对无症状患者的处理存在明显分歧，缺乏系统性整合框架。

方法

结论与意义

该综述强调，对ACHD患者应系统筛查并分层管理冠状动脉异常，以降低缺血与猝死风险，尤其需关注ASO术后成年人群的长期随访。对电生理实践而言，其意义在于，在室性心律失常评估、ICD一级预防决策及导管消融术前规划中，需整合冠状动脉解剖信息。局限性：本文为叙述性综述，无原始数据与定量效应量，结论主要依赖指南推荐与专家共识；无症状患者的干预阈值及ASO世代的长期冠脉结局仍待前瞻性研究确定。

Arrhythmic Mitral Valve Prolapse as a Regional Stretch-Induced Cardiomyopathy with Fibro-Inflammatory Signature: A Conceptual Review.

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783044
室性心律失常 Journal Article Review

Mili Dave、Shaaf Ahmad、Kirkwood Adams 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

Mitral valve prolapse (MVP) affects 1-3% of the general population and is largely considered benign. However, a small subset of MVP patients develops complex ventricular arrhythmias (VAs) and sudden cardiac death, including patients with nonsignificant mitral regurgitation (MR). This subgroup of arrhythmic MVP (aMVP) is variably associated with features such as mitral annular disjunction, bileaflet myxomatous prolapse, and regional myocardial fibrosis, with arrhythmic risk disproportionate to hemodynamic burden. The EHRA defines aMVP as MVP with complex VAs (frequent premature ventricular contractions, non-sustained or sustained ventricular tachycardia, ventricular fibrillation, or aborted sudden cardiac arrest) in the absence of another defined arrhythmic substrate. This narrative review reframes aMVP as a regional, stretch-induced cardiomyopathy with a distinct fibro-inflammatory signature. We further characterize a two-hit pathogenesis in which abnormal valvular mechanics create regional myocardial stress, while patient-specific vulnerability shapes variable fibro-inflammatory, fibrotic, and electromechanical responses. This narrative review synthesizes contemporary evidence across multimodal cardiac imaging, electrophysiology, surgical outcomes, mechanotransduction biology, and pharmacology, emphasizing a modifiable fibro-inflammatory trajectory underlying the intermediate risk aMVP phenotype and supporting a clinical shift from device-based rescue to substrate-directed prevention. Expanding on this model, we evaluate substrate-modifying pharmacotherapies for mechanistic fit, human cardiac evidence, and trial feasibility. Among these, mineralocorticoid receptor antagonists (MRAs) and sodium-glucose cotransporter 2 (SGLT2) inhibitors demonstrate the strongest convergence of antifibrotic potential and trial readiness.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本综述将致心律失常性二尖瓣脱垂重新定义为区域牵张诱导的心肌病，具纤维炎症特征；双打击机制可解释心律失常风险与血流动力学负担不平行，MRA 与 SGLT2 抑制剂为最具前景的基质修饰药物。

研究动机

二尖瓣脱垂（MVP）累及一般人群 1%–3%，多被视为良性；但少部分患者出现复杂室性心律失常（VAs）乃至心脏性猝死，其中不乏二尖瓣反流（MR）并不显著者。EHRA 将 aMVP 定义为 MVP 合并复杂 VAs（频发室性早搏、非持续性或持续性室速、室颤或复苏成功的心脏骤停），且无其他明确致心律失常基质。该亚组与二尖瓣环分离、双叶黏液样脱垂、区域心肌纤维化等相关，心律失常风险与血流动力学负担明显不匹配。既往证据的缺口在于：发病机制尚未阐明，风险分层缺乏可靠基质指标，临床处理仍以器械（ICD）被动补救为主，缺少针对心肌基质本身的预防策略。

方法

本文为叙述性（概念性）综述，非原始研究，无研究对象招募、无样本量、无终点定义与统计检验（上述要素摘要未报告）。作者综合多模态心脏影像、电生理、外科手术结局、机械转导生物学与药理学五个方面的现有证据，提出 aMVP 的新模型：即区域牵张诱导的心肌病伴独特纤维炎症特征，并构建“双打击”发病假说——异常瓣膜机械力学造成区域心肌应力（第一打击），患者个体易感性决定纤维炎症、纤维化与机电反应的程度（第二打击）。在此基础上，按机制契合度、人体心脏证据与临床试验可行性三项标准，评估候选基质修饰药物。

结果

摘要未报告定量结果，无效应量、95% CI 或 P 值。定性层面的要点为：（1）aMVP 的心律失常风险与 MR 严重程度不平行，而与二尖瓣环分离、双叶黏液样脱垂及区域心肌纤维化等基质特征相关；（2）作者将纤维炎症通路描述为可干预的中间风险表型基础，从而支持从“器械补救”转向“基质导向预防”的临床理念转变；（3）在候选药物中，盐皮质激素受体拮抗剂（MRA）与钠-葡萄糖协同转运蛋白 2 抑制剂（SGLT2i）在抗纤维化潜力与试验可行性上的汇聚度最高，被视为最值得优先开展临床试验的基质修饰药物。

结论与意义

综述结论是：aMVP 不应仅被视为瓣膜结构异常，而应作为区域牵张诱导、具纤维炎症特征的获得性心肌病来认识，这为风险分层（影像纤维化/二尖瓣环分离标志物）与早期药物干预提供了统一框架。对电生理实践的影响在于：对 MVP 患者应主动评估复杂 VAs 与心肌基质，把管理重心前移至基质修饰与预防，而非仅依赖 ICD 补救。局限性明确：本文属叙述性综述，无原始数据，缺乏定量效应证据，“双打击”模型与 MRA/SGLT2i 的获益目前仍属机制推演，需前瞻性队列与随机对照试验验证。
