Monocyte-to-High-Density-Lipoprotein Ratio Is Associated with Ventricular Arrhythmia in Patients with Implantable Cardioverter-Defibrillators: A Cross-Sectional Study.

J Cardiovasc Dev Dis·2026-09-24·PMID 42783027
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Murat Küçükukur、Cenk Ekmekçi

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Residual arrhythmic risk persists in recipients of implantable cardioverter-defibrillators (ICDs) despite guideline-based selection, and inexpensive markers that refine this risk are needed. The monocyte-to-high-density-lipoprotein ratio (MHR) integrates a pro-inflammatory (monocyte) and an anti-inflammatory/antioxidant (HDL) signal in a single index derived from routine blood work. We examined whether MHR is associated with ventricular arrhythmia in ICD patients. METHODS: In this cross-sectional study we assessed 382 ICD recipients attending a device outpatient clinic and classified them by a composite ventricular arrhythmia outcome (non-sustained ventricular tachycardia, sustained ventricular tachycardia, or ventricular fibrillation) documented on device interrogation. MHR and comparator inflammatory markers were measured at the same visit. Discrimination was assessed by receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis, the optimal threshold by the Youden index, and independence by multivariable logistic regression; internal validity was examined with repeated cross-validation, bootstrap optimism correction, and calibration testing. RESULTS: The ventricular arrhythmia outcome was present in 58 patients (15.2%). MHR was higher in patients with the outcome (median 16.3 vs. 12.3, p < 0.001) and discriminated it with an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.756 (95% CI 0.673-0.828). At the optimal threshold of 15.71, sensitivity was 63.8% and specificity was 83.3%, with a negative predictive value of 92.8%; the outcome was present in 40.7% of patients above versus 7.2% below this cut-off. MHR remained independently associated with the outcome after adjustment for age, left ventricular ejection fraction, etiology, and renal function (odds ratio 1.23 per unit, 95% CI 1.16-1.32; p < 0.001) and outperformed high-sensitivity C-reactive protein (DeLong p < 0.001). Discrimination was preserved in both ischaemic and non-ischaemic cardiomyopathy without interaction by etiology (p = 0.67). The model was stable on internal validation (cross-validated AUC 0.761; optimism 0.021; Hosmer-Lemeshow p = 0.66). CONCLUSIONS: MHR is independently associated with documented ventricular arrhythmia in ICD patients and, being derived from routine tests, may warrant prospective evaluation as a low-cost adjunct for risk stratification.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 382 例 ICD 植入患者中，单核细胞/高密度脂蛋白比值（MHR）与装置记录到的室性心律失常独立相关，AUC 0.756，最佳阈值 15.71 时阴性预测值达 92.8%。

研究动机

ICD 植入人群即便按指南筛选，仍存在残余心律失常风险，而现有风险分层手段成本较高或可及性有限。MHR 由单核细胞（促炎）与 HDL（抗炎/抗氧化）构成，来源于血常规与血脂等常规检查，廉价易得，但其与 ICD 患者室性心律失常的关系尚缺乏系统评估，本研究即针对该证据缺口。

方法

单中心横断面研究，纳入某装置门诊随访的 382 例 ICD 植入患者。暴露为同次随访测定的 MHR 及对照炎症标志物（含高敏 C 反应蛋白）。主要终点为装置程控记录到的复合室性心律失常结局（非持续性室速、持续性室速或室颤）。采用 ROC 曲线评估判别能力，Youden 指数确定最佳阈值，多因素 logistic 回归检验独立性（校正年龄、LVEF、病因、肾功能），并以重复交叉验证、bootstrap 乐观度校正及校准检验评估内部效度。

结果

58 例（15.2%）存在室性心律失常结局。该组 MHR 中位数高于无事件组（16.3 vs 12.3，p<0.001），AUC 为 0.756（95%CI 0.673–0.828）。最佳阈值 15.71 时敏感度 63.8%、特异度 83.3%、阴性预测值 92.8%；阈值以上者结局发生率 40.7%，阈值以下者 7.2%。校正年龄、LVEF、病因及肾功能后，MHR 仍独立相关（每单位 OR 1.23，95%CI 1.16–1.32；p<0.001），判别能力优于高敏 C 反应蛋白（DeLong p<0.001）。缺血性与非缺血性心肌病亚组判别能力均保留，病因交互作用无统计学意义（p=0.67）。内部验证稳定：交叉验证 AUC 0.761，乐观度 0.021，Hosmer-Lemeshow p=0.66。

结论与意义

MHR 与 ICD 患者装置记录的室性心律失常独立相关，且来源于常规检验，有望作为低成本辅助指标用于风险分层，并提示炎症—脂代谢轴在该人群心律失常基质中的潜在作用。局限性：横断面设计无法确立因果与时间顺序；单中心样本量有限、事件数仅 58 例；阈值 15.71 未经外部人群验证，尚不能直接用于临床决策，需前瞻性队列进一步评估其增量预测价值与可干预性。

Phenotypic Heterogeneity in Mitral Valve Prolapse: Insights from a Case Series.

J Cardiovasc Echogr·2026-09-24·PMID 42781164
室性心律失常 Case Reports Journal Article

Irene Landi、Luca Favarato、Paolo Toritto 等 9 位作者

英文摘要

Mitral valve prolapse (MVP) is the most common valvular heart disorder and shows marked phenotypic heterogeneity, ranging from benign forms to malignant variants associated with ventricular arrhythmias (VA), mitral annular disjunction (MAD), myocardial fibrosis, and sudden cardiac death (SCD). We present a case series of three patients with bileaflet MVP and mild mitral regurgitation (MR), followed longitudinally for more than a decade. Clinical assessment, electrocardiography, advanced echocardiography, 24-h Holter monitoring, exercise testing, and cardiac magnetic resonance imaging were used to characterize structural, mechanical, and electrical features. Phenotypic variability was evident across the three patients. Case 1 showed progressive MAD (5.4 to 11.3 mm), significant left ventricular (LV) dilatation, marked systolic curling, and a high burden of premature ventricular contractions with non-sustained ventricular tachycardia (NSVT), despite the absence of late gadolinium enhancement (LGE). Case 2 developed mild LV dilatation, curling, and basal inferolateral LGE, with exercise-induced NSVT, whereas Case 3, despite a family history of SCD, showed progression to moderate MR but no MAD, curling, or LGE, and only limited arrhythmic activity. These cases illustrate the broad phenotypic spectrum of MVP and support the concept that this condition may involve valvular, annular, myocardial, and electrophysiological abnormalities. MAD, curling, and LGE appear to represent overlapping but potentially independent markers of arrhythmic risk. Multimodality imaging may therefore be valuable for comprehensive risk stratification, even in asymptomatic patients, as structural and electrical features can evolve over time.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

三例双叶二尖瓣脱垂伴轻度反流患者纵向随访逾十年，显示表型差异显著：瓣环分离、收缩期卷曲与延迟钆增强可分别独立出现，提示需用多模态影像对心律失常风险作分层评估。

研究动机

二尖瓣脱垂（MVP）是最常见的瓣膜性心脏病，多数为良性病程，但部分“恶性表型”与室性心律失常（VA）、二尖瓣环分离（MAD）、心肌纤维化及心脏性猝死（SCD）相关。既往证据多为横断面观察，缺乏长期纵向随访，对于无症状患者中哪些结构和电生理特征提示进展为高危表型，仍缺乏可靠的识别指标。

方法

病例系列研究，连续纳入 3 例双叶 MVP 伴轻度二尖瓣反流（MR）患者，纵向随访超过 10 年。评估手段包括临床评估、心电图、高级超声心动图（测量 MAD、收缩期卷曲）、24 小时动态心电图、运动负荷试验及心脏磁共振（延迟钆增强，LGE），用于刻画结构、机械与电学特征。本研究为描述性分析，摘要未报告定量统计方法与效应量。

结果

3 例患者表型差异明显：例 1 的 MAD 由 5.4 mm 进展至 11.3 mm，左心室（LV）显著扩大、收缩期卷曲明显，室性早搏（PVC）负荷高并伴非持续性室性心动过速（NSVT），但无 LGE；例 2 出现轻度 LV 扩大、卷曲及基底下侧壁 LGE，运动诱发 NSVT；例 3 虽有 SCD 家族史，进展为中度 MR，但无 MAD、无卷曲、无 LGE，心律失常活动有限。摘要未报告组间比较的统计结果及 P 值。

结论与意义

本组病例提示，MVP 的表型谱广泛，可同时累及瓣膜、瓣环、心肌与电生理多个层面；MAD、收缩期卷曲与 LGE 可能是相互重叠但各自独立的心律失常风险标志，多模态影像（超声心动图、心脏磁共振、动态心电图、运动试验）对无症状患者的综合风险分层具有潜在价值，因为结构与电学异常可随时间演变。局限为仅 3 例、无对照、随访方案未标准化，无法推断各标志的发生率、预测效度或因果关系，结论有待更大样本的前瞻性队列验证。

Growth of Cardiac Fibromas During Infancy.

J Cardiovasc Magn Reson·2026-09-24·PMID 42785590
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Kelsey Jurow、Francesca Sperotto、Tal Geva 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Cardiac fibromas commonly present in childhood as large intramyocardial tumors that are frequently associated with arrhythmias, hemodynamic compromise, sudden cardiac death, and/or associated cardiac or lung compression. Although unoperated cardiac fibromas were historically thought to remain stable in size throughout life, their natural history has not been studied. METHODS: In this retrospective single-center study, we identified all patients with cardiac fibromas and serial cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) and/or cardiac computed tomography (CT) before or after surgical intervention to characterize fibroma growth. RESULTS: A total of 36 infants and children with at least 2 serial imaging studies were identified. Younger age at first imaging study was associated with faster cardiac fibroma growth, with infants (<12 months) demonstrating a body surface area (BSA)-indexed growth rate of 11 ± 2mL/m2/month; whereas children older than 2.5 years demonstrated no growth (p=0.005). In one child, the tumor's unadjusted volume increased by 933% and the BSA-adjusted volume increased by 334% between CMR examinations obtained in the first week of life and at age 1.5 years. A higher T2 tumor:myocardium intensity ratio was associated with both younger age at initial examination and faster growth rate (p=0.001). A higher tumor growth rate was associated with the development of hemodynamic impact of the tumor, in particular pulmonary vein compression and/or lung compression. No significant tumor growth was observed after tumor resection. CONCLUSIONS: Cardiac fibromas may exhibit rapid growth in infancy, and tumor growth is associated with the development of hemodynamic compromise. Based on this observation, consideration should be given to frequent monitoring of tumor size and clinical status during the first years of life.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

单中心36例儿童心脏纤维瘤序列影像显示，婴儿期（<12个月）肿瘤按体表面积标化的生长速度达11±2 mL/m²/月，2.5岁以上基本不生长，肿瘤快速生长与血流动力学受损相关。

研究动机

心脏纤维瘤多在儿童期表现为体积较大的心肌内肿瘤，常合并心律失常、血流动力学障碍、心脏性猝死及对邻近心脏或肺组织的压迫。既往认为未手术的心脏纤维瘤终生大小稳定，但这一自然病史从未被系统研究，导致临床对随访频率与干预时机缺乏依据。本研究拟用序列影像学刻画纤维瘤的生长规律及其与临床后果的关系。

方法

回顾性单中心研究。纳入所有诊断为心脏纤维瘤、且在手术前后至少完成2次序列心脏磁共振（CMR）和/或心脏CT检查的患儿，共36例婴幼儿及儿童。以体表面积（BSA）标化的肿瘤体积增长率（mL/m²/月）为主要观察指标，同时分析首诊年龄、T2序列肿瘤/心肌信号强度比与生长速度的关系，以及生长速度与血流动力学影响（尤其肺静脉受压和/或肺受压）的关联；对术后影像随访者评估残余肿瘤生长情况。摘要未报告具体统计方法及95%置信区间。

结果

首诊年龄越小，肿瘤生长越快：<12个月婴儿的BSA标化增长率为11±2 mL/m²/月，而>2.5岁儿童未见生长（p=0.005）。1例患儿自出生第1周至1.5岁的CMR比较显示，未校正体积增加933%，BSA校正体积增加334%。T2肿瘤/心肌信号强度比越高，与首诊年龄越小及生长速度越快均相关（p=0.001）。生长速度较快者更易出现肿瘤的血流动力学影响，尤其是肺静脉受压和/或肺受压。肿瘤切除术后未见明显再生长。

结论与意义

心脏纤维瘤在婴儿期可呈快速生长，且生长速度与血流动力学受损的发生相关，提示对未手术患儿在出生后最初数年内应密切监测肿瘤大小与临床状态。本研究为单中心、小样本（36例）回顾性分析，且以具备序列影像为纳入条件可能引入选择偏倚，生长速度阈值与随访间隔尚无前瞻性数据支持；对合并快速生长或已出现肺静脉/肺受压者，何时行外科切除仍需个体化评估。

Hypertrophic obstructive cardiomyopathy with associated pulmonary arteriopathy: A histopathologic perspective from sudden cardiac death

J Forensic Leg Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42800147
室性心律失常 Journal Article Case Reports

Gangwal P、Pulagura SSK、Meshram V 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

Hypertrophic obstructive cardiomyopathy (HOCM) is one of the common causes of sudden cardiac death which can be associated with pulmonary vascular remodelling changes also known as Pulmonary Arteriopathy (PA). Pulmonary Arteriopathy (PA) is a condition affecting the pulmonary arteries, characterised by a range of proliferative and obstructive vascular lesions that ultimately result in lumen obstruction. It may manifest under diverse settings, encompassing both acquired and congenital disorders. We present a case of a 52-year male with no previous known history of illness or disease, who was brought to the Emergency department and was pronounced dead upon arrival. During the autopsy, the heart exhibited left ventricular thickness (3 cm thick) and the interventricular septum thickness (2.5 cm thick). Histopathological examination revealed localised myocardial disarray, muscle fibre hypertrophy, and nuclear enlargement, indicative of left heart disease characterised by left ventricular hypertrophy. The histopathological examination of the both lungs revealed that the blood vessels exhibited thickened walls, characterised by medial wall hypertrophy, manifesting as muscularization suggestive of Grade 2 Pulmonary Arteriopathy (PA). This case examines the pathophysiological mechanisms connecting the characteristics of cardiomyopathy, pulmonary arterial hypertension, and pulmonary arteriopathy, thereby emphasising the significance of pulmonary vascular alterations as a consequence of HOCM but also as a sign of disease progression. Other causes of pulmonary arteriopathy along with advanced ageing should be ruled out in such cases.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例52岁男性猝死尸检显示肥厚型梗阻性心肌病（左室壁厚3 cm、室间隔厚2.5 cm）合并Grade 2肺动脉病变，提示HOCM可继发肺血管重构，并可作为疾病进展的标志。

研究动机

肥厚型梗阻性心肌病（HOCM）是心源性猝死的常见原因之一，已知可伴发肺血管重构改变，即肺动脉病变（pulmonary arteriopathy，PA）。PA以肺动脉出现一系列增殖性与闭塞性血管病变、最终导致管腔阻塞为特征，可见于获得性与先天性等多种疾病背景。然而，HOCM 与肺动脉高压、肺动脉病变之间的病理生理联系在临床上认识不足，肺血管改变究竟是 HOCM 的继发后果还是疾病进展的标志，尚缺乏组织病理学层面的证据。

方法

个案报告（法医尸检+组织病理学）。对象为一名52岁男性，既往无已知疾病史，送至急诊时已宣告死亡。尸检测量左心室壁厚度与室间隔厚度；对心肌行组织病理学检查，观察心肌排列紊乱、心肌纤维肥大及核增大等改变；对双肺行组织病理学检查，观察肺血管壁增厚、中膜肥厚及“肌化”表现，并按肺动脉病变分级标准判定级别。摘要未报告对照组、统计方法与定量分析。

结果

尸检示左心室壁厚3 cm、室间隔厚2.5 cm。心肌组织病理学可见局限性心肌排列紊乱、心肌纤维肥大及细胞核增大，符合以左心室肥厚为特征的左心疾病。双肺组织病理学示肺血管壁增厚，以中膜肥厚为特征，呈“肌化”表现，符合Grade 2肺动脉病变。摘要未报告血流动力学、影像学或分子生物学数据，亦未报告任何统计学效应量与P值。

结论与意义

该尸检病例从组织病理学角度提示，HOCM 可伴随肺动脉高压与肺动脉病变，肺血管重构既可能是 HOCM 的继发后果，也可能是疾病进展的标志。作者强调，在这类病例中应排除其他可导致肺动脉病变的原因以及高龄相关血管改变。局限性：为单个病例的尸检观察，缺乏生前血流动力学与影像资料，无法确立因果关系，结论仅具提示性，需更大样本的病理与临床研究验证。

Acute Modulation of Arrhythmogenic Ventricular Substrate Following Cardiac Stereotactic Body Radiation Therapy.

JACC Clin Electrophysiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42782228
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Negar Raissi-Dehkordi、Nastaran Raissi-Dehkordi、Jonathan A Salas 等 15 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Cardiac stereotactic body radiation therapy (cSBRT) is a noninvasive, investigational treatment for refractory ventricular tachycardia (VT). The antiarrhythmic effects and time course of action of cSBRT on abnormal ventricular substrate remain poorly understood. OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to investigate the electrophysiological, molecular, and structural changes following cSBRT in a porcine infarct model. METHODS: Anterior myocardial infarction was created in a porcine model of infarct-related re-entrant VT (n = 8). Swine were randomized to cSBRT (n = 4) or control infarct (n = 4) groups. Baseline electrophysiology study and high-resolution electroanatomic maps were performed 8 to 10 weeks postinfarction. The cSBRT group received 25 Gy single-fraction photon radiation guided by initial left ventricular substrate maps. Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging followed by repeat electrophysiology study and mapping studies were performed 6 weeks later. RESULTS: cSBRT resulted in significant prolongation of the effective refractory period in remote, nontargeted myocardium (cSBRT: 216 ± 21-252 ± 11 ms, Δ +17% vs control: 234 ± 7-249 ± 1 ms, Δ +6.5%) and targeted scar tissue (cSBRT: 258 ± 10-291 ± 18 ms, Δ +13% vs control: 274 ± 13-284 ± 8 ms, Δ +3.7%; group × timepoint interaction P = 0.048). Sustained VT was inducible in all 8 subjects at the terminal timepoint. There was a trend toward more prolonged VT cycle length (cSBRT: 195 ± 19-295 ± 55 ms, Δ +53% vs control: 212 ± 15-261 ± 14 ms, Δ +23%; P = 0.22). Connexin-43 immunoreactive area density in the infarct region was lower in the cSBRT group (1.4% vs 2.7%, respectively; P < 0.01). There was no difference detected in late gadolinium enhancement volume (cSBRT: 29.3% ± 7.8% vs control: 27.2% ± 11.3%; P = 0.8) or histologic fibrosis (cSBRT: 54.9% ± 13.4% vs control: 54.6% ± 20.6%; P = 0.95). CONCLUSIONS: Delivery of 25 Gy cSBRT produced early electrical remodeling with increased effective refractory period and decreased connexin-43 area density, without evidence of increased fibrosis.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在猪梗死性室速模型中，25 Gy 单次分级立体定向放疗早期即延长有效不应期（靶区及远隔心肌）并降低梗死区连接蛋白 43 表达密度，但未增加纤维化，室速仍全部可诱发。

研究动机

心脏立体定向体部放疗（cSBRT）是难治性室性心动过速（VT）的无创研究性治疗手段，但其抗心律失常作用及对异常心室基质的起效时间过程尚不清楚，缺乏对电生理、分子与结构改变的机制性证据。

方法

研究为猪梗死相关折返性 VT 模型（n=8），前壁心肌梗死造模后随机分为 cSBRT 组（n=4，25 Gy 单次光子照射，依据基线左心室基质标测定位）与对照梗死组（n=4）。梗死 8–10 周行基线电生理检查与高分辨率三维电解剖标测，6 周后行心脏磁共振及重复电生理检查与标测；比较有效不应期、VT 诱发与周长、LGE 体积、组织学纤维化及连接蛋白 43 表达，统计采用组×时间点交互分析。

结果

cSBRT 组有效不应期在远隔非靶区明显延长（216±21→252±11 ms，Δ+17% vs 对照 234±7→249±1 ms，Δ+6.5%），靶区瘢痕亦延长（258±10→291±18 ms，Δ+13% vs 对照 274±13→284±8 ms，Δ+3.7%；组×时间点交互 P=0.048）。终末时间点 8 只动物均仍可诱发持续性 VT；VT 周长有延长趋势（195±19→295±55 ms，Δ+53% vs 对照 212±15→261±14 ms，Δ+23%；P=0.22）。梗死区连接蛋白 43 免疫反应面积密度 cSBRT 组更低（1.4% vs 2.7%，P<0.01）；LGE 体积（29.3%±7.8% vs 27.2%±11.3%，P=0.8）与组织学纤维化（54.9%±13.4% vs 54.6%±20.6%，P=0.95）无差异。

结论与意义

25 Gy cSBRT 可在 6 周内引起早期电学重塑——有效不应期延长、连接蛋白 43 密度下降，而无纤维化增加，提示其抗心律失常作用可能早于结构重塑；但持续性 VT 仍全部可诱发，样本量小（每组 4 只）、随访仅 6 周，对临床实践仅为机制层面的提示，需更大样本与更长随访验证。

Translating Population-Based Epidemiology into Clinical Recognition of Coronary Artery Spasm: Prevalence, Incidence, and Geographic-Seasonal Variation in Taiwan.

Med Sci (Basel)·2026-09-24·PMID 42783455
室性心律失常 Journal Article

Alexander T H Wu、Ming-Jui Hung、Claire Hung 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Background: Coronary artery spasm (CAS) is a reversible cause of myocardial ischemia, infarction, and sudden cardiac death. Often under-recognized and influenced by ethnicity, geography, and environment, CAS requires population-level data. This study examined nationwide CAS epidemiology in Taiwan by age, sex, region, and season. Methods: This cross-sectional population-based study used the Taiwan National Health Insurance Research Database (2004-2018). Diagnosis-coded CAS was operationalized using ICD-9-CM code 413.1 or ICD-10-CM code I20.1 recorded on at least three outpatient encounters or one inpatient encounter. Age- and sex-standardized prevalence and incidence were calculated, and temporal, geographic, and seasonal variations assessed. Results: Among 27,974 patients with diagnosis-coded CAS, the mean age at diagnosis was 57.9 years, with nearly equal sex distribution (49.7% female). In 2018, the age-standardized prevalence was 35.1 per 100,000 population, with comparable rates in men (35.4) and women (34.8 per 100,000). The incidence was 11.5 per 100,000 in both sexes. Both prevalence and incidence increased linearly from 2004 to 2018 (p < 0.001), peaked at ages 75-79 years, and then declined. Geographic analysis revealed the highest disease burden in central Taiwan, particularly in Taichung City, Changhua County, and Yunlin County. Seasonal analysis showed a clear pattern, with incidence peaking in winter (December-February) and spring (March-May), and notably lower counts in summer and autumn. Conclusions: From 2004 to 2018, CAS prevalence and incidence increased in Taiwan, affected both sexes similarly, peaked at ages 75-79 years, and were highest in central western regions during winter and spring. These findings may improve CAS recognition and resource allocation.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

台湾2004–2018年共27,974例冠状动脉痉挛患者，2018年年龄标化患病率35.1/10万、发病率11.5/10万，男女相近，75–79岁达峰，中西部冬春季高发。

研究动机

冠状动脉痉挛（CAS）是可逆性心肌缺血、心肌梗死乃至心源性猝死的病因之一，临床上常被低估；其发生受种族、地理与环境因素影响，而既往缺乏基于人群的国家级流行病学数据，导致对该病的识别阈值、危险分层与医疗资源配置缺乏依据。本研究旨在利用全台医保数据库填补这一缺口，明确CAS的患病率、发病率及时间、地域与季节分布特征。

方法

横断面、基于人群的研究，数据来源为台湾全民健康保险研究数据库（2004–2018年）。CAS定义为门诊至少3次或住院至少1次记录ICD-9-CM 413.1或ICD-10-CM I20.1诊断编码。计算年龄及性别标化的患病率与发病率（每10万人），并分析年龄、性别、地区（含县市）与季节（12–2月为冬、3–5月为春等）的分布差异及时间趋势；采用趋势检验评估2004–2018年变化。摘要未报告具体统计模型与置信区间方法。

结果

共纳入27,974例诊断编码为CAS的患者，诊断时平均年龄57.9岁，女性占49.7%。2018年年龄标化患病率为35.1/10万，男性35.4/10万、女性34.8/10万，两性相当；发病率为11.5/10万，男女相同。2004–2018年患病率与发病率均呈线性上升（p < 0.001）。年龄分布于75–79岁达峰，此后下降。地理分布以台湾中部疾病负担最高，尤以台中市、彰化县与云林县为著。季节分析显示明确规律：发病在冬季（12–2月）与春季（3–5月）达峰，夏季与秋季计数明显较低。效应量与95%置信区间摘要未报告。

结论与意义

2004–2018年间台湾CAS患病率与发病率持续上升，两性受累相近，75–79岁为高峰，中西部冬春季负担最重。该结果提示应提高对老年人群及冬春季胸痛患者的CAS识别意识，并据此优化导管室、激发试验与心血管急救资源的区域与季节性配置。局限性包括：仅基于诊断编码、缺乏冠状动脉造影或激发试验证实，可能受编码与就诊行为偏倚影响；未报告结局（心肌梗死、室性心律失常、猝死）及治疗数据，无法评估CAS与恶性心律失常风险的关联，需前瞻性研究验证。

Pregnancy-Associated Spontaneous Coronary Artery Dissection in the Third Trimester Requiring Multistent Percutaneous Coronary Intervention: A Case Report

Case Rep Cardiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42787811
器械治疗 Journal Article

Liu S、Layton A、Chastain H

英文摘要

Background Pregnancy-associated spontaneous coronary artery dissection (P-SCAD) is an uncommon cause of acute coronary syndrome (ACS) in young women. Although SCAD accounts for less than 1% of ACS cases, it causes up to 40%-50% of myocardial infarctions during pregnancy and the postpartum period. Third-trimester cases requiring complex percutaneous coronary intervention (PCI) are particularly rare.Case presentation A 33-year-old gravida 5, para 3 woman at 27 weeks' gestation presented with sudden severe substernal chest pain. Electrocardiographic findings and elevated cardiac biomarkers confirmed an anterior ST-segment elevation myocardial infarction (STEMI). Coronary angiography demonstrated spontaneous coronary artery dissection involving the mid-to-distal left anterior descending artery. Owing to ongoing ischemia and extensive dissection, she underwent intravascular ultrasound-guided PCI with four overlapping drug-eluting stents, restoring TIMI III flow. Following revascularization, she remained hospitalized for maternal and fetal monitoring because of concerns for preeclampsia and severe fetal growth restriction. Although expectant management was initially pursued, worsening fetal heart-rate concerns necessitated cesarean delivery at 29 weeks' gestation without maternal cardiac complications. Although left ventricular function initially improved with guideline-directed medical therapy (GDMT), discontinuation of several medications because she felt symptomatically better contributed to progressive left ventricular dysfunction and NYHA class III heart failure, ultimately requiring cardiac rehabilitation and prophylactic implantable cardioverter-defibrillator placement.Discussion This case highlights the complexity of managing extensive third-trimester P-SCAD requiring multistent PCI while balancing maternal cardiovascular stabilization with ongoing obstetric complications. It further emphasizes the importance of longitudinal imaging, sustained GDMT, medication adherence, and coordinated multidisciplinary follow-up after significant myocardial injury.Conclusion Third-trimester P-SCAD requiring complex PCI is rare. Early recognition, individualized revascularization strategies, multidisciplinary obstetric and cardiovascular management, and close long-term follow-up are essential to optimize maternal and fetal outcomes while reducing the risk of adverse ventricular remodeling and heart failure progression.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

33岁孕妇于妊娠27周发生前壁STEMI，冠脉造影证实中远段左前降支自发性冠脉夹层，经IVUS指导植入4枚重叠药物洗脱支架后恢复TIMI III级血流，母婴度过急性期，但产后因自行停用GDMT而进展为NYHA III级心衰并植入预防性ICD。

研究动机

妊娠相关自发性冠脉夹层（P-SCAD）虽仅占ACS不足1%，却占妊娠及产后心肌梗死的40%-50%，是年轻女性ACS的少见但重要病因。妊娠晚期需多支架PCI的广泛夹层病例尤为罕见，且缺乏关于血运重建策略、抗栓与指南导向药物治疗（GDMT）在孕期及产后如何平衡、以及长期左室重构风险的证据，本病例报告旨在补充这一临床空白。

方法

单例病例报告。对象为33岁、孕5产3、妊娠27周的孕妇，因突发严重胸骨下疼痛就诊；心电图与心肌生物标志物升高确诊前壁ST段抬高型心肌梗死（STEMI），冠脉造影示左前降支中远段自发性冠脉夹层。因持续缺血且夹层范围广泛，行血管内超声（IVUS）指导的PCI，植入4枚重叠药物洗脱支架。术后住院监测母婴情况（疑诊子痫前期、严重胎儿生长受限），产后随访左室功能、心衰分级及器械治疗指征。摘要未报告具体统计方法及定量检测数值。

结果

PCI术后TIMI血流恢复至III级。因胎儿心率恶化于妊娠29周行剖宫产，母亲无心脏并发症。初始GDMT后左室功能改善，但患者自觉症状好转后自行停用多种药物，随后出现进行性左室功能不全和NYHA III级心衰，最终需心脏康复并植入预防性ICD。摘要未报告LVEF具体数值、支架型号及随访时间。

结论与意义

妊娠晚期需复杂多支架PCI的P-SCAD虽罕见，但提示早期识别、个体化血运重建、产科与心内科多学科协作管理及密切长期随访对优化母婴结局至关重要。本病例特别警示：即使症状缓解，也不可擅自停用GDMT，否则可能导致左室重构和心衰进展，甚至需要ICD；局限性为单例报告，无法推广因果关系与疗效结论。

4

Electrocardiographic Identification and Correction of Left Ventricular Pacing Latency.

J Cardiovasc Echogr·2026-09-24·PMID 42781222
器械治疗 Case Reports Journal Article

T R Muralidharan、D R Nivetha、S Sanjith 等 9 位作者

英文摘要

Pacing latency, defined as the delay between a pacemaker stimulus and myocardial depolarization, is an uncommon but clinically relevant phenomenon in patients with permanent pacing systems. Its presence may be mistaken for a lead malfunction, exit block, or device failure, leading to unnecessary interventions. A 55-year-old male with a history of nonischemic dilated cardiomyopathy (left ventricular [LV] ejection fraction-30% on standard two-dimensional echocardiography; 17% on speckle-tracking assessment) and severe systolic dysfunction, previously implanted with a cardiac resynchronization therapy with defibrillator (CRT-D), was admitted with progressive abdominal distension, bilateral lower limb edema, and weight gain of 5-6 kg over 2 weeks. Examination revealed hypotension (90/60 mmHg), elevated jugular venous pressure, pedal edema, and basal crepitations. Laboratory evaluation showed elevated N-terminal pro-B-type natriuretic peptide (4750 pg/mL). He was treated with intravenous diuretics and guideline-directed medical therapy, resulting in clinical improvement and weight reduction from 75 to 70 kg. During hospitalization, device interrogation confirmed stable parameters. However, a 12-lead electrocardiography demonstrated consistent pacing latency with an 80 ms delay between the pacing spike and QRS onset. Electrolytes were normal, and no offending medications were identified. The patient was discharged in New York Heart Association Functional Class II after symptomatic improvement. This case highlights the mechanical and electrophysiological consequences of LV pacing latency in a patient with CRT-D and worsening heart failure. Pacing latency required active correction to restore effective biventricular resynchronization. Recognition of this entity is essential to guide targeted device optimization and prevent unnecessary interventions. Advanced echocardiographic techniques play a critical complementary role in assessing the mechanical impact of pacing latency and the efficacy of its correction.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例55岁非缺血性扩张型心肌病合并CRT-D患者，心电图发现80 ms的左室起搏潜伏期并主动纠正，恢复了有效的双室再同步，提示该现象容易被误判为导线故障。

研究动机

起搏潜伏期指起搏刺激到心肌除极之间的延迟，在永久起搏患者中并不常见，但具有临床意义。它容易被误认为导线故障、传出阻滞或装置失效，从而导致不必要的导线更换或修正手术。在CRT患者中，左室起搏潜伏期还可能削弱双室再同步的疗效，加重心衰。既往对此类现象的识别方法、机械与电生理后果，以及纠正策略的认识仍不充分。

方法

这是一篇单例病例报告，没有对照组，也没有进行统计学检验。研究对象为55岁男性，患有非缺血性扩张型心肌病：二维超声测得LVEF为30%，斑点追踪测得LVEF为17%；既往植入CRT-D。患者因进行性腹胀、双下肢水肿、2周内体重增加5-6 kg入院。检查包括装置程控、12导联心电图、电解质检测和超声心动图（含斑点追踪）评估。治疗为静脉利尿剂联合指南导向药物治疗，并针对左室起搏潜伏期进行主动纠正；摘要未报告具体纠正方式，如调整AV/VV间期或重新定位左室导线。观察指标包括症状、体重、NYHA心功能分级以及双室再同步是否恢复。

结果

入院时血压90/60 mmHg，颈静脉压升高，双下肢水肿，肺底湿啰音；NT-proBNP 4750 pg/mL。装置程控参数稳定；12导联心电图显示恒定的起搏潜伏期，起搏钉至QRS起点延迟80 ms。电解质正常，未发现相关药物因素。经利尿和指南导向药物治疗后，症状改善，体重由75 kg降至70 kg，出院时为NYHA II级。摘要未报告纠正后心电图延迟时间、超声再同步指标的量化变化，也未报告随访时长。

结论与意义

该病例说明，在CRT-D合并心衰恶化的患者中，左室起搏潜伏期会带来机械和电生理后果，需要主动识别和纠正，才能恢复有效的双室再同步；认识这一现象有助于进行针对性的装置优化，避免误判为导线故障而实施不必要的干预。超声心动图，尤其是斑点追踪技术，在评估潜伏期的机械影响和纠正效果方面具有重要补充价值。局限性在于，该研究为单例报告，无对照，随访信息有限，纠正措施细节及疗效的量化数据均未报告，结论不能外推。

Left Bundle Branch Area Pacing in Heart Failure: A Physiological Alternative to Conventional Biventricular Cardiac Resynchronization Therapy and a Step Towards Personalized Device Therapy.

J Pers Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42783494
器械治疗 Journal Article Review

Ioanna Koniari、Andreas Gerakaris、Efthimia Kapsali 等 13 位作者

英文摘要

Heart failure remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality despite advances in guideline-directed medical therapy and device-based treatment. Cardiac resynchronization therapy delivered by conventional biventricular pacing constitutes an established therapeutic strategy for selected patients with heart failure with reduced ejection fraction and electrical desynchrony. Left bundle branch area pacing has emerged as a physiological pacing strategy that directly engages the conduction system, providing more effective electrical and mechanical resynchronization. Current evidence from observational studies, randomized pilot trials, and recent multicentre data suggest that left bundle branch pacing may achieve greater QRS narrowing, more favourable pacing thresholds, improved left ventricular ejection fraction, and reduced heart failure hospitalizations compared with conventional biventricular pacing in selected patients. This review summarizes the rationale, implantation criteria, patient selection, and current clinical evidence supporting left bundle branch pacing as an alternative pacing modality in heart failure patients. Despite encouraging available data, larger randomized trials with longer follow-up and broader patient populations are required to clarify long-term safety and to determine whether left bundle branch pacing should be adopted as a routine alternative to conventional cardiac resynchronization therapy. From a personalized medicine perspective, the choice between conduction system pacing and conventional biventricular pacing should be guided by individual patient characteristics, including conduction phenotype, QRS morphology and duration, myocardial scar burden, heart failure aetiology, coronary venous anatomy, and the chance of achieving confirmed conduction-system capture. This review therefore renders left bundle branch pacing within an individualized, phenotype-guided resynchronization strategy rather than as a universal replacement for biventricular pacing.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本综述指出，左束支区域起搏在部分心衰患者中较传统双心室起搏可获得更显著的QRS缩窄、更优的起搏阈值、LVEF改善及心衰住院减少，但长期安全性仍待大型随机试验证实。

研究动机

心衰尽管已有指南导向药物治疗与器械治疗，仍是致病与致死的主要原因。对射血分数降低且存在电同步性不良的患者，传统双心室起搏的心脏再同步治疗（CRT）已是成熟策略，但存在无反应者与起搏效果不理想的问题。左束支区域起搏（LBBAP）作为直接夺获传导系统的生理性起搏方式，可能提供更有效的电与机械再同步；既往证据多来自观察性研究与小样本随机预试验，缺乏长期、大样本的验证，其相对于双心室起搏的定位尚不明确。

方法

本文为叙述性综述，非原始研究，摘要未报告样本量、纳入排除标准的具体数值及终点定义。综述系统梳理LBBAP的原理、植入标准、患者选择及现有临床证据（观察性研究、随机预试验及近期多中心数据），并将其与常规双心室起搏CRT进行对比；关键比较指标包括QRS波宽度变化、起搏阈值、左心室射血分数（LVEF）及心衰住院。统计方法：摘要未报告（无定量合并分析）。

结果

综述归纳现有证据显示，在经过选择的心衰患者中，与常规双心室起搏相比，LBBAP可实现更明显的QRS缩窄、更有利的起搏阈值、更好的LVEF改善以及心衰住院减少。摘要未报告上述各项指标的具体效应量、95%置信区间及P值，亦未提供各研究样本量与随访时长。作者同时强调，现有数据虽令人鼓舞，但仍需更大规模、更长随访、覆盖更广人群的随机试验来明确长期安全性。

结论与意义

LBBAP有望成为心衰再同步治疗中优于双心室起搏的生理性替代方案，但现有证据尚不足以支持其作为常规一线替代。从个体化器械治疗角度，传导系统起搏与双心室起搏的选择应由患者个体特征指导，包括传导表型、QRS形态与时限、心肌瘢痕负荷、心衰病因、冠状静脉解剖以及能否实现确证的传导系统夺获。局限在于本文为综述、缺乏定量合成与长期随机对照数据，长期安全性及对硬终点（死亡、心衰住院）的影响仍待证实。

Selective inhibition of LY294002 across cloned human cardiac ion channels: implications for atrium-selective therapy in atrial fibrillation.

Front Pharmacol·2026-09-24·PMID 42780784
遗传与基础 Journal Article

Rui Niu、Jiawei Li、Huanle Fang 等 15 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND AND PURPOSE: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most prevalent clinical arrhythmia, yet current antiarrhythmic pharmacotherapies have limited efficacy and pose risks of off-target ventricular block. LY294002 was reported to inhibit the ultrarapid delayed-rectifier potassium current (I Kur) carried by Kv1.5 channels, a promising atrium-selective therapeutic target for AF. To determine whether LY294002 exhibits functional selectivity for I Kur, this study comprehensively examined its inhibitory effects on major counter-target cardiac ion currents. METHODS: The cDNAs encoding the principal pore-forming subunits of major cardiac channels-mediating I Kr(Kv11.1), I Ks(Kv7.1), I K1(Kir2.1), I to(Kv4.3), I Na(Nav1.5), and I Ca-L(Cav1.2)-were heterologously expressed in Chinese hamster ovary cells. The inhibitory effects of LY294002 on these channels were assessed using the whole-cell patch-clamp technique. Furthermore, molecular docking analysis was conducted to characterize the structural interactions and binding configurations of LY294002 with Kv1.5. RESULTS: LY294002 inhibited all investigated currents in a concentration-dependent manner, albeit with markedly varying potencies. Based on Hill equation fits, the half-maximal inhibitory concentration (IC50) values for LY294002 on I Ks, I Kr , I to , I K1, I Na, and I Ca-L were 21.5, 26.3, 55.9, 81.5, 131.6, and 46.5 μM, respectively. Notably, the lowest off-target IC50 (I Ks) was approximately 2.7-fold higher than the previously reported IC50 for I Kur (7.9 μM). Molecular docking analysis revealed that LY294002 bound within the internal cavity of Kv1.5, with predicted binding affinity of -8.3 kcalImol-1. CONCLUSION: The inhibitory potency of LY294002 against I Kur is substantially higher than its potency against other major cardiac currents, demonstrating its potential to act as a selective I Kur blocker. Structurally, this preferential inhibition is likely mediated by the compound's favorable occupancy of key gating or pore regions unique to the Kv1.5 channel conformation.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在CHO细胞异源表达体系中，LY294002对Kv1.5通道介导的I Kur（IC50 7.9 μM）抑制效力高于主要心脏反靶电流约2.7–16倍，提示其有望成为房选择性I Kur阻滞剂。

研究动机

AF是最常见的临床心律失常，现有抗心律失常药物疗效有限，且存在脱靶阻滞心室离子通道（如I Kr）导致致心律失常的风险。Kv1.5通道携带的超快速延迟整流钾电流（I Kur）在心室表达很低（摘要未具体报告），是心房选择性治疗AF的理想靶点；LY294002虽被报道可抑制I Kur，但其对主要心脏反靶电流的功能选择性尚未系统评价，本研究即针对这一证据缺口展开。

方法

基础研究（体外电生理加分子模拟）。将主要心脏通道成孔亚基cDNA——I Kr（Kv11.1）、I Ks（Kv7.1）、I K1（Kir2.1）、I to（Kv4.3）、I Na（Nav1.5）、I Ca-L（Cav1.2）——异源表达于中国仓鼠卵巢细胞，采用全细胞膜片钳技术评估LY294002对各电流的浓度依赖性抑制，经Hill方程拟合得到IC50；并利用分子对接分析LY294002与Kv1.5的结构性结合方式与结合构象。I Kur的IC50（7.9 μM）为既往文献报道值而非本研究测定；细胞数、重复次数及统计检验方法摘要未报告。

结果

LY294002对全部受试电流均呈浓度依赖性抑制，但效力差异显著：IC50分别为I Ks 21.5 μM、I Kr 26.3 μM、I Ca-L 46.5 μM、I to 55.9 μM、I K1 81.5 μM、I Na 131.6 μM。最低的反靶电流IC50（I Ks 21.5 μM）约为I Kur（7.9 μM）的2.7倍；据此推算其余反靶电流IC50为I Kur的3.3–16.7倍（摘要未报告95%CI及P值）。分子对接显示LY294002结合于Kv1.5通道内部腔隙，预测结合亲和力为-8.3 kcal/mol，提示其占据Kv1.5构象特有的门控或孔道区域。

结论与意义

LY294002对I Kur的抑制效力明显高于其他主要心脏电流，功能上具备选择性I Kur阻滞剂的特征，其结构基础可能在于对Kv1.5内部腔隙结合位点的有利占据，为房选择性AF抗心律失常药物先导化合物的优化提供了依据。局限性：研究仅在CHO异源表达系统进行，未经人心房肌细胞或在体模型验证；缺乏对心室复极及致心律失常风险的整体评估；I Ks的安全窗仅约2.7倍，选择性尚有限；对接结果为计算预测，需突变-功能实验证实。摘要未报告统计学检验与样本量细节。

Atrial fibrillation and left bundle branch block followed by Brugada phenocopy induced by Entrectinib in a female Caucasian patient with metastatic non-small cell lung cancer: a case report.

Int Cancer Conf J·2026-09-24·PMID 42781027
遗传与基础 Journal Article

Helen Triantafyllidi、Vasiliki Dragona、Charalambos Varlamos

英文摘要

Brugada syndrome (BrS), a genetic disease with complex pathophysiology due to genetic and pharmacological factors, might lead to ventricular arrythmia and sudden cardiac arrest. Entrectinibe, a novel kinase inhibitor, is currently used for the treatment of metastatic of non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). Its adverse effects include rarely cardiotoxicity, malignant arrythmias and left ventricular myocardial dysfunction, which is highlighted in this case report. We present the case of a 52-year-old Caucasian female with a history of NSCLC and a recent initiation of Entrectinib due to cancer relapse. Three days later, the patient was diagnosed with almost asymptomatic atrial fibrillation (AF) and left bundle branch block (LBBB) which were automatically converted to sinus rhythm and a Brugada Type I pattern, which in turn was automatically resolved and replaced by a normal ECG pattern. The above-described ECG phenomena lasted for just a few hours. Entrectinib treatment was discontinued after Oncology consultation. Unexpected cardiotoxicity and arrhythmogenicity should be monitored when administrating new anti-cancer drugs. In the case of pre-existing Brugada pattern, Entrectinib is contra-indicated as it can followed by malignant ventricular arrhythmias. Additionally, treatment with Entrectinib must be interrupted in the presence of a QT prolongation (> 500msec) or/and syncope or when a newly diagnosed Brugada pattern appears. The management of these patients must be individualized according to their status and the existence of comorbidities while the role of a Cardiologist is essential.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

一例52岁转移性NSCLC女性在启用Entrectinib 3天后出现几乎无症状的AF与LBBB，数小时内自行转为窦性心律并出现I型Brugada拟表型，随后心电图恢复正常。

研究动机

Brugada综合征病理生理复杂，受遗传与药物因素影响，可导致室性心律失常和心脏骤停。Entrectinib是用于转移性NSCLC的新型激酶抑制剂，其心脏毒性、恶性心律失常及左心室心肌功能障碍罕见但值得关注；既往证据多来自药物说明书和零散病例，需进一步识别其致心律失常风险。

方法

个案报告。对象为1例52岁白人女性，有NSCLC病史，因肿瘤复发近期开始Entrectinib治疗。观察暴露为Entrectinib，结局为用药后心电图异常及心律失常事件，包括AF、LBBB、Brugada I型图形及QT间期等；摘要未报告系统统计方法。

结果

启用Entrectinib 3天后，患者被诊断为几乎无症状的AF和LBBB；上述心电图异常在数小时内自动转为窦性心律并出现I型Brugada图形，随后又自动消失并恢复为正常心电图。经肿瘤科会诊后停用Entrectinib。摘要未报告具体心率、QT间期数值、随访时间及统计学比较。

结论与意义

该病例提示使用新型抗癌药物时应监测意外心脏毒性和致心律失常性。对既往存在Brugada图形者，Entrectinib属禁忌，因其可能继发恶性室性心律失常；出现QT间期延长（>500 ms）、晕厥或新发Brugada图形时应中断治疗。患者管理需结合个体状况和合并症，心内科医生作用关键。局限性为单一病例，不能确立因果关系，且摘要未报告长期随访。

Prediction of Atrial Fibrillation Risk Through the Integration of Genetic Information and Artificial Intelligence‐Based Electrocardiogram Data

J Arrhythm·2026-09-24·PMID 42787646
遗传与基础 research-article Journal Article 开放获取

Yang P、Park H、Kwon O 等 10 位作者

英文摘要

Background Accurate prediction of atrial fibrillation (AF) is essential for prevention. The CHARGE-AF score, based on routinely available clinical factors, provides a practical tool for estimating AF risk but has limited predictive accuracy. This study aimed to improve AF risk prediction by integrating artificial intelligence (AI)-based electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis of age and sex with genetic information in addition to established clinical risk factors.Methods We analyzed 39 478 UK Biobank participants without prior AF. A polygenic risk score for AF (AF-PRS), the AI-ECG age gap (AI-ECG-predicted age minus chronological age), and AI-ECG-predicted sex mismatch were evaluated on top of the CHARGE-AF model. Model performance was compared across sequential prediction models.Results Over a median follow-up of 2.7 years (interquartile range, 1.7-4.2; maximum, 6.7), 533 participants (1.3%) developed AF. AF-PRS (HR 1.61, 95% CI: 1.48-1.76) and the AI-ECG age gap (HR 1.37, 95% CI: 1.24-1.51) were independently associated with incident AF. Compared with CHARGE-AF alone (C-index 0.708, 95% CI: 0.686-0.730), adding AF-PRS improved discrimination (C-index 0.743; Δ0.035, 95% CI: 0.021-0.049; p < 0.001). Adding traditional ECG parameters did not enhance performance, whereas incorporating AI-ECG features (age gap and sex mismatch) into the combined clinical and genetic model provided additional gain in discrimination (C-index 0.754; Δ0.045, 95% CI: 0.028-0.062), reclassification (net reclassification improvement 0.408), and discrimination improvement (0.007; all p < 0.001).Conclusions Integrating genetic risk and AI-ECG-derived features enhances AF prediction beyond an established clinical model, supporting the use of combined digital and genetic biomarkers for risk stratification.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在无既往 AF 的 UK Biobank 人群中，CHARGE-AF 模型基础上加入 AF 多基因风险评分与 AI-ECG 年龄差、AI-ECG 性别错配特征，可显著提升新发 AF 的判别与风险重分类能力。

研究动机

准确识别 AF 高危人群是预防 AF 及其并发症的前提。基于常规临床因素的 CHARGE-AF 评分虽便于使用，但判别能力有限（C-index 约 0.7），既往证据显示单纯临床模型难以进一步提升；本研究拟验证在临床风险因素之外，联合 AF 遗传风险与 AI-ECG 衍生表型（预测年龄差、性别错配）能否改善 AF 风险预测。

方法

基于 UK Biobank 的前瞻性队列分析，纳入 39 478 名无既往 AF 的参与者。暴露/预测因子包括 AF 多基因风险评分（AF-PRS）、AI-ECG 年龄差（AI 预测年龄减实际年龄）及 AI-ECG 预测性别与实际性别错配。以 CHARGE-AF 模型为基线，逐层加入上述变量构建序贯预测模型，比较判别能力（C-index）、重分类（NRI）与判别改善（IDI）；并分析各预测因子与新发 AF 的独立关联（Cox 模型风险比）。

结果

中位随访 2.7 年（IQR 1.7-4.2，最长 6.7 年），533 例（1.3%）发生 AF。AF-PRS（HR 1.61，95%CI 1.48-1.76）与 AI-ECG 年龄差（HR 1.37，95%CI 1.24-1.51）均与新发 AF 独立相关。CHARGE-AF 单独 C-index 0.708（95%CI 0.686-0.730）；加入 AF-PRS 后升至 0.743（Δ0.035，95%CI 0.021-0.049，p<0.001）；加入传统 ECG 参数未见提升；在临床+遗传模型上再加 AI-ECG 特征（年龄差与性别错配）后 C-index 达 0.754（Δ0.045，95%CI 0.028-0.062），NRI 0.408、IDI 0.007（均 p<0.001）。

结论与意义

在 CHARGE-AF 临床模型之上联合 AF 遗传风险与 AI-ECG 衍生特征可进一步改善 AF 风险分层，支持将数字化 ECG 标志物与遗传标志物联合用于 AF 一级预防的风险评估。局限性包括：事件数较少（1.3%）、随访时间偏短、仅限 UK Biobank 人群（以欧洲裔为主）的外部推广性受限，且摘要未报告校准度、绝对风险阈值决策分析及性别错配单独效应，尚需多队列外部验证后才能用于临床路径。

Macrophage Immunometabolism in Epicardial Adipose Tissue and Obesity-Related Atrial Fibrillation

J Inflamm Res·2026-09-24·PMID 42807583
遗传与基础 review-article Review Journal Article 开放获取

Xia L、Sun X、Xie L 等 8 位作者

英文摘要

Obesity is a crucial risk factor for atrial fibrillation (AF). The underlying mechanism is partly related to increased epicardial adipose tissue (EAT). EAT directly contacts the adjacent cardiac myocardium where immune and metabolic activities interact. Obesity induces inflammatory and metabolic signals within EAT, which directly influence atrial structure and electrical remodeling. However, the specific immunometabolic mechanisms linking EAT macrophages to atrial arrhythmogenesis remain unclear. We highlight evidence that nutrient excess, hypoxia, oxidative stress, and adipocyte-derived inflammatory signals reprogram glucose, lipid, and amino acid metabolism in EAT macrophages, promoting persistent pro-inflammatory and profibrotic states. Metabolic pathways involving mechanistic target of rapamycin (mTOR)-hypoxia-inducible factor-1α (HIF-1α) signaling, fatty acid and ceramide metabolism, the NOD-, LRR- and pyrin domain-containing protein 3 (NLRP3) inflammasome, and metabolic-epigenetic interactions may sustain macrophage activation and amplify local inflammation. These metabolically reprogrammed macrophages subsequently promote atrial fibrosis, electrical heterogeneity, and autonomic dysfunction, while systemic inflammation, insulin resistance, and neurohumoral activation further reinforce the EAT-atrial pathological circuit. Emerging evidence also suggests that EAT characteristics may improve AF risk stratification and that weight loss and metabolic interventions can attenuate EAT inflammation. More importantly, targeting macrophage immunometabolism may offer a mechanistically distinct approach to interrupt the link between obesity and atrial remodeling rather than treating AF solely after its substrate has developed. Collectively, targeting EAT immunometabolism may shift obesity-related AF management from treating an established arrhythmia toward identifying and modifying the metabolic-inflammatory substrate that promotes its development and recurrence, potentially complementing conventional rhythm-control and ablation strategies. Future studies should establish causal relationships and identify clinically actionable metabolic targets for EAT macrophage-directed therapy.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本综述指出，肥胖时心外膜脂肪组织（EAT）巨噬细胞发生糖、脂及氨基酸代谢重编程，经mTOR-HIF-1α、神经酰胺与NLRP3等通路维持促炎促纤维化状态，推动心房重构与AF发生；靶向该免疫代谢环节可能成为肥胖相关AF的新干预方向。

研究动机

肥胖是房颤（AF）的重要危险因素，其致心律失常机制部分由心外膜脂肪组织（EAT）增多所介导。EAT与相邻心房心肌直接接触，是免疫与代谢信号交互的界面；肥胖可在EAT内诱导炎症与代谢信号，进而影响心房结构与电重构。然而，将EAT巨噬细胞与房性心律失常发生联系起来的具体免疫代谢机制仍不清楚，本综述即针对这一证据缺口进行梳理。

方法

本文为叙述性综述，非原始研究：摘要未报告样本量、干预或暴露分组、预设终点及统计方法，也未说明检索策略与纳入文献数量。综述整合的证据围绕营养过剩、缺氧、氧化应激及脂肪细胞来源炎症信号如何重编程EAT巨噬细胞的葡萄糖、脂质与氨基酸代谢，并重点讨论雷帕霉素机制靶点（mTOR）-缺氧诱导因子-1α（HIF-1α）信号、脂肪酸与神经酰胺代谢、NLRP3炎症小体及代谢-表观遗传交互等通路。

结果

综述层面证据显示，上述代谢重编程使EAT巨噬细胞维持持续促炎与促纤维化表型，并通过mTOR-HIF-1α、脂肪酸与神经酰胺代谢、NLRP3炎症小体及代谢-表观遗传交互等通路放大局部炎症；这些被代谢重塑的巨噬细胞继而促进心房纤维化、电异质性与自主神经功能障碍，而系统性炎症、胰岛素抵抗与神经体液激活进一步强化“EAT-心房”病理环路。此外，新近证据提示EAT特征可能有助于改善AF风险分层，减重与代谢干预可减轻EAT炎症。摘要未报告任何效应量、95%置信区间或P值。

结论与意义

靶向EAT巨噬细胞免疫代谢可能提供一种机制上不同于传统节律控制与导管消融的策略：在致心律失常基质形成之前即识别并干预促发AF的代谢-炎症基础，从而把肥胖相关AF的管理从“治疗已发生的心律失常”前移为“修饰其发生与复发的底物”，并可能与现有节律控制及消融策略互补。局限性在于本文为综述，所依据证据以机制性与观察性研究为主，因果关系尚未确立，临床可操作的代谢靶点仍需未来研究验证。

Impact of Cytochrome P450 and P-glycoprotein Gene Polymorphisms on the Risk of Hemorrhagic Complications in Older Patients with Non-Valvular Atrial Fibrillation Treated with Rivaroxaban.

J Pers Med·2026-09-24·PMID 42783501
遗传与基础 Journal Article

Ivan V Sychev、Andrey P Kondrakhin、Sherzod P Abdullaev 等 10 位作者

英文摘要

Background: The administration of direct oral anticoagulants to older adults requires careful risk stratification against a backdrop of polypharmacy and age-related renal function decline. The role of single nucleotide polymorphisms in cytochrome P450 genes and efflux transporters in this context remains a clinically understudied issue. This study aimed to investigate the association of ABCB1, CYP3A4, and CYP3A5 allelic variants with the safety of rivaroxaban therapy in geriatric patients with non-valvular atrial fibrillation (AF). Materials and Methods: This prospective cohort study consecutively enrolled 94 patients (mean age 83.2 ± 9.2 years). Rivaroxaban trough plasma concentrations (Cmin,ss ) were quantified using a validated high-performance liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (HPLC-MS/MS) assay. Real-time polymerase chain reaction was utilized for the molecular genetic profiling of the ABCB1 (rs1045642, rs2032582), CYP3A4*22 (rs35599367), and CYP3A5*3 (rs776746) loci. Comorbidity severity and the spectrum of drug-drug interactions were evaluated as additional predictors. Results: Clinically relevant hemorrhagic complications were documented in 36.2% (n = 34) of the monitored patients. A significant association was established between the homozygous CC genotype at the ABCB1 rs1045642 locus and increased bleeding propensity (odds ratio [OR] 2.42; 95% CI: 1.02-5.74; p = 0.042), whereas the alternative TT variant was associated with a pronounced protective effect. No statistically significant correlations were identified for biotransformation isoenzyme markers (CYP3A4*22, CYP3A5*3). Anticoagulant metrics were comparably distributed across groups and showed no dependence on genetic status. The leading non-genetic factors associated with hemorrhage were advanced age (p < 0.0001), progressive glomerular filtration rate decline (CKD stages 3b-4; p = 0.0009), and pharmacokinetic/pharmacodynamic interference from the co-administration of amiodarone (OR 3.61), non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs, and antiplatelet agents. The validated HAS-BLED score demonstrated no predictive power within the comorbid conditions of this cohort (p = 0.052). Conclusions: The ABCB1 rs1045642 (C3435T) polymorphism is associated with the occurrence of hemorrhagic events. Implementing pharmacogenetic testing of the P-glycoprotein transporter combined with a rigorous audit of renal excretory function and concomitant therapy management may optimize the safety profile of rivaroxaban in geriatric practice. These findings warrant confirmation in a larger cohort.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 94 例接受利伐沙班抗凝的高龄非瓣膜性 AF 患者中，ABCB1 rs1045642 的 CC 基因型与出血风险升高相关（OR 2.42），而 CYP3A4/CYP3A5 变异无显著关联。

研究动机

高龄 AF 患者常合并多重用药及年龄相关肾功能减退，使用直接口服抗凝药时出血风险分层困难。CYP450 代谢酶与 P-糖蛋白外排转运体基因多态性是否影响利伐沙班安全性，既往证据有限，临床上仍未充分研究。

方法

前瞻性队列研究，连续纳入 94 例非瓣膜性 AF 老年患者（平均年龄 83.2±9.2 岁），均服用利伐沙班。用经验证的 HPLC-MS/MS 法测定利伐沙班稳态谷浓度（Cmin,ss）；用实时 PCR 对 ABCB1（rs1045642、rs2032582）、CYP3A4*22（rs35599367）、CYP3A5*3（rs776746）位点进行基因分型；同时评估合并症严重程度与药物相互作用谱作为附加预测因素；终点为临床相关出血并发症。

结果

中位随访期内 36.2%（n=34）患者发生临床相关出血。ABCB1 rs1045642 纯合 CC 基因型与出血倾向增加显著相关（OR 2.42；95% CI 1.02–5.74；p=0.042），TT 变异则显示明显保护效应；CYP3A4*22 与 CYP3A5*3 均无统计学显著关联。各组抗凝药浓度分布相当，未见对基因状态的依赖。主要非遗传危险因素为高龄（p<0.0001）、肾小球滤过率进行性下降（CKD 3b–4 期；p=0.0009），以及合用胺碘酮（OR 3.61）、非甾体抗炎药和抗血小板药的药代/药效学干扰。HAS-BLED 评分在该队列合并症背景下无预测价值（p=0.052）。

结论与意义

ABCB1 rs1045642（C3435T）多态性与出血事件发生相关，提示对 P-糖蛋白转运体开展药物基因组检测，并结合肾功能与合并用药的严格核查，可能改善高龄患者利伐沙班治疗的安全性。本研究样本量小、为单中心前瞻性队列，结果需在更大规模队列中验证。

Transthyretin Cardiac Amyloidosis in a Safety-Net Hospital: A Case Series of 10 Afro-Caribbean Patients.

JACC Case Rep·2026-09-24·PMID 42782039
遗传与基础 Case Reports Journal Article

Keston Rattan、Elizabeth L Allison、Wayne-Andrew Palmer 等 4 位作者

英文摘要

Transthyretin cardiac amyloidosis (ATTR-CA) is profoundly underdiagnosed in Afro-Caribbean populations despite the high prevalence of the Val122Ile (V142Ile) variant (approximately 3.4% carrier frequency among self-identified Black individuals in the United States). This case series describes 10 Afro-Caribbean patients diagnosed with ATTR-CA at a safety-net hospital. Seven patients presented with heart failure symptoms, while 3 presented with alternative manifestations including syncope with high-degree atrioventricular block and pericardial tamponade. The mean age was 75 ± 8 years, with 80% male predominance. All patients demonstrated a positive 99mTc-PYP scan, and cardiac magnetic resonance imaging was performed in 20%. Among patients with completed genetic testing, 50% had the Val122Ile variant, and 50% had the wild-type ATTR. Atrial fibrillation was present in 50%, and 30% required permanent pacemaker implantation for symptomatic bradycardia. This series demonstrates that ATTR-CA can be effectively diagnosed in resource-limited safety-net hospitals, emphasizing these institutions' critical role in reducing health disparities.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在资源有限的安全网医院中，10 例非裔加勒比 ATTR-CA 患者经 99mTc-PYP 扫描确诊，一半为 Val122Ile 变异型，30% 需植入永久起搏器。

研究动机

ATTR-CA 在非裔加勒比人群中诊断严重不足，尽管 Val122Ile（V142Ile）变异在自我认同为黑人者中的携带率约 3.4%；既往证据多来自大型学术中心，安全网医院（服务低收入与弱势人群的机构）中的诊断可行性与临床表型尚缺乏数据。

方法

回顾性病例系列，单中心安全网医院，纳入 10 例确诊 ATTR-CA 的非裔加勒比患者；报告年龄、性别分布、临床表现类型（心衰症状与其他表现）、99mTc-PYP 扫描与心脏磁共振的使用情况、基因检测结果（Val122Ile 变异型与野生型）、合并房颤及永久起搏器植入比例；摘要未报告具体统计方法与终点定义。

结果

平均年龄 75 ± 8 岁，男性占 80%；7 例以心衰症状起病，3 例为其他表现（晕厥伴高度房室传导阻滞、心包填塞）；10 例 99mTc-PYP 扫描均为阳性，20% 完成心脏磁共振；完成基因检测者中 50% 为 Val122Ile 变异型、50% 为野生型；房颤占 50%，30% 因症状性心动过缓植入永久起搏器。摘要未报告置信区间与 P 值。

结论与意义

ATTR-CA 可在资源有限的安全网医院中通过 99mTc-PYP 扫描有效诊断，此类机构在缩小健康差异方面具有关键作用；传导系统受累与起搏器需求在本组比例较高，提示需重视非裔加勒比人群的筛查与心律监测。局限为单中心小样本病例系列、缺乏对照与随访结局，结论需更大规模研究验证。

Sustained Ventricular Tachycardia as the First Presentation of Hereditary Transthyretin Amyloidosis With the Glu109Gln Mutation.

JACC Case Rep·2026-09-24·PMID 42782034
遗传与基础 Case Reports Journal Article

Thomas Weiss、Sie K Fensman、Astrid J Terkelsen 等 5 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Transthyretin amyloid cardiomyopathy (ATTR-CM) most commonly presents with heart failure symptoms. Sustained ventricular tachycardia (VT) is an uncommon initial manifestation. CASE SUMMARY: A 57-year-old man presented with hemodynamically unstable monomorphic VT requiring cardioversion. Imaging suggested cardiac amyloidosis and bone scintigraphy demonstrated Perugini grade 3 uptake. Genetic testing revealed a pathogenic p.Glu109Gln variant in the transthyretin gene. An implantable cardioverter-defibrillator (ICD) was implanted, and ATTR-stabilizer therapy was initiated. The patient remains stable without recurrent VT after 2.5 years. DISCUSSION: This case highlights VT as a rare initial presentation of ATTR-CM and emphasizes the limited evidence guiding the management of ventricular arrhythmias and the role of ICDs in these patients. Notably, the patient remained free of recurrent ventricular arrhythmias and ICD therapies during 2.5 years of follow-up without antiarrhythmic therapy. TAKE-HOME MESSAGES: VT may represent an atypical presentation of ATTR-CM. Management of ventricular arrhythmias remains individualized due to limited evidence.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

1例携带转甲状腺素蛋白基因p.Glu109Gln致病变异的遗传性ATTR心肌病患者，以血流动力学不稳定的持续性单形性室速为首发表现，植入ICD并启动ATTR稳定剂治疗后2.5年未再发室速。

研究动机

转甲状腺素蛋白淀粉样心肌病（ATTR-CM）通常以心力衰竭症状起病，持续性室速作为首发表现罕见。此类患者室性心律失常的治疗策略及ICD的作用目前证据有限，缺乏指南层面的明确推荐，本病例为这一临床缺口提供参考。

方法

JACC Case Rep发表的单例病例报告。患者为57岁男性，因血流动力学不稳定的单形性室速就诊，需电复律。影像学提示心脏淀粉样变，骨闪烁显像示Perugini 3级摄取，基因检测发现转甲状腺素蛋白基因p.Glu109Gln致病变异。干预为植入ICD并启动ATTR稳定剂治疗。观察终点为随访期间室速复发及ICD治疗情况。摘要未报告具体统计学方法。

结果

患者植入ICD并接受ATTR稳定剂治疗后，随访2.5年期间未再发室速，也未接受ICD治疗，且未使用抗心律失常药物，病情保持稳定。摘要未报告LVEF、淀粉样变负荷、ICD程控参数等具体数值。

结论与意义

持续性室速可作为ATTR-CM的非典型首发表现，临床遇不明原因室速时应考虑心脏淀粉样变筛查。由于证据有限，此类患者室性心律失常的处理及ICD适应证仍需个体化决策；本例在未用抗心律失常药物的情况下2.5年无复发，提示ICD联合ATTR稳定剂治疗或可作为一种管理路径，但单例经验不足以推广，需更大规模研究验证。

Complete Atrioventricular Block in an HLA-B27-Positive Woman Without Overt Spondyloarthritis

JACC Case Rep·2026-09-24·PMID 42788914
遗传与基础 Journal Article Case Reports

Batista Lage JG、Tavares Pinheiro MV、Ferreira de Souza O 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

Background Complete atrioventricular block (CAVB) in adults younger than 50 years warrants investigation for inflammatory, infiltrative, infectious, autoimmune, and inherited disorders. Human leukocyte antigen B27 (HLA-B27)-associated conduction disease without overt spondyloarthritis is exceptionally rare.Case summary A 43-year-old physically active woman presented with symptomatic CAVB requiring dual-chamber pacemaker implantation. She had recurrent HLA-B27-positive anterior uveitis and Hashimoto thyroiditis but no clinical evidence of spondyloarthritis. Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging, fluorodeoxyglucose positron emission tomography, comprehensive genetic testing, and an extensive autoimmune evaluation were unrevealing, supporting a diagnosis of exclusion.Discussion Although atrioventricular conduction abnormalities are recognized in ankylosing spondylitis, isolated CAVB associated with HLA-B27 in the absence of overt spondyloarthritis has rarely been reported. This case broadens the recognized clinical spectrum of HLA-B27-associated disease and highlights recurrent HLA-B27-positive uveitis as a potential diagnostic clue in otherwise unexplained CAVB.Take-home messages HLA-B27 testing should be considered in selected younger patients with unexplained CAVB and suggestive immune-mediated features. A multidisciplinary evaluation is essential after exclusion of structural, inflammatory, infectious, autoimmune, and inherited causes.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

43岁运动活跃女性因症状性完全性房室传导阻滞植入双腔起搏器，HLA-B27阳性并反复前葡萄膜炎、桥本甲状腺炎，无脊柱关节炎表现，全面排查后为排除性诊断，提示HLA-B27相关孤立性传导病变。

研究动机

50岁以下成人出现完全性房室传导阻滞（CAVB）时，需系统排查炎症性、浸润性、感染性、自身免疫性及遗传性病因。房室传导异常在强直性脊柱炎中已被认识，但在缺乏显性脊柱关节炎的情况下，HLA-B27相关的孤立性CAVB极为罕见，既往仅有零星报道；此类患者的识别线索与评估路径尚不明确，本例旨在补充这一少见临床表型。

方法

单例病例报告（JACC Case Rep）。对象为43岁、平时运动活跃的女性，因症状性CAVB入院并植入双腔起搏器。既往有反复HLA-B27阳性前葡萄膜炎及桥本甲状腺炎，但无脊柱关节炎的临床证据。所行检查包括心脏磁共振（CMR）、氟脱氧葡萄糖正电子发射断层扫描（FDG-PET）、全面基因检测及广泛的自身免疫学评估。研究终点为病因学诊断，最终以排除性诊断成立。摘要未报告样本量以外的统计学方法及随访方案。

结果

CMR、FDG-PET、基因检测及自身免疫学评估均无阳性发现（摘要未报告具体影像、基因位点、抗体滴度及随访时间），在排除结构性、炎症性、感染性、自身免疫性及遗传性病因后，诊断为HLA-B27相关的孤立性CAVB。摘要未报告起搏器植入参数、传导阻滞恢复情况等定量数据。

结论与意义

本例将HLA-B27相关疾病的已知临床谱扩展至无脊柱关节炎的孤立性CAVB，并提示反复HLA-B27阳性葡萄膜炎可作为不明原因CAVB的诊断线索。对较年轻、原因不明且伴免疫介导特征的CAVB患者，可考虑检测HLA-B27，并在排除各类继发病因后开展多学科评估。局限在于仅为单一病例、缺乏随访数据，HLA-B27与传导病变之间的因果关系无法确立，结论需更大样本验证。

9

Shared genetic architecture of HCM and AF: Elevated NPPA/ANP is linked to atrial electrical remodeling.

PLoS One·2026-09-24·PMID 42784636
遗传与基础 Journal Article research-article 开放获取

A Z M Fahim Siddique、Xinru Wang、Ruyi Du 等 7 位作者

英文摘要

BACKGROUND: Hypertrophic cardiomyopathy (HCM) patients exhibit heightened susceptibility to atrial fibrillation (AF), yet the underlying genetic nexus remains poorly defined. This study aimed to identify genes common to HCM and AF, thereby revealing the underlying pathogenesis of susceptibility to AF in HCM patients. METHODS: Genes associated with HCM and AF were sourced from the Gene Expression Omnibus (GEO), Online Mendelian Inheritance in Man (OMIM), Comparative Toxicogenomics Database (CTD), GeneCards, and DisGeNET databases. We conducted a comparative analysis to identify overlapping genes between HCM and AF for further investigation, which included functional enrichment analysis, protein-protein interaction networks, and immune infiltration assessment. Key hub genes were determined using cytoHubba, a Cytoscape plugin, and their validation was performed using independent datasets GSE36961 and GSE115574. The genes corroborated by these datasets were additionally validated in clinical samples. RESULTS: A total of 11 overlapping genes were identified between HCM and AF. Functional enrichment and immune infiltration analyses revealed their involvement in immune regulation and inflammatory processes. Three hub genes NPPA, ACE, and POMC were recognized as key molecular links between HCM and AF. Validation using the GSE36961 dataset confirmed the upregulation of NPPA in HCM, supporting its potential as a central gene connecting the two conditions. Consistent with the bioinformatics findings, exploratory clinical verification findings of serum levels of N-terminal fragment of A-type natriuretic peptide (NT-proANP), encoded by NPPA, were markedly elevated in both the HCM and HCM with AF groups compared with healthy controls (P < 0.01). Moreover, NT-proANP concentrations were significantly higher in the HCM + AF group than in the HCM-only group (P < 0.01). CONCLUSION: This study identifies a shared genetic link between HCM and AF. The findings strongly associate elevated NT-proANP levels, encoded by the NPPA gene, with AF in HCM patients. We propose NT-proANP as a promising circulating biomarker for stratifying AF risk in this patient population.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

生物信息学分析锁定 NPPA、ACE、POMC 为 HCM 与 AF 的共享枢纽基因，临床验证显示 HCM 合并 AF 患者血清 NT-proANP 显著高于单纯 HCM 与健康对照。

研究动机

肥厚型心肌病（HCM）患者对心房颤动（AF）的易感性明显增高，但两者共有的遗传与分子基础尚不清楚，既往研究多局限于单病种，缺乏对共享基因网络及可解释 AF 易感性的循环标志物的系统识别，临床上亦缺少可用于 HCM 患者 AF 风险分层的生物学指标。

方法

研究为生物信息学分析联合探索性临床验证，非前瞻性队列。从 GEO、OMIM、CTD、GeneCards、DisGeNET 数据库提取 HCM 与 AF 相关基因，取交集后行功能富集分析、蛋白-蛋白相互作用（PPI）网络构建与免疫浸润评估，并用 Cytoscape 插件 cytoHubba 筛选枢纽基因；以独立数据集 GSE36961 与 GSE115574 进行表达验证，再在临床血清样本中检测 NPPA 编码的 N 端 A 型利钠肽前体（NT-proANP），比较 HCM 组、HCM 合并 AF 组与健康对照组的水平。临床样本量、终点定义细节及具体统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

共识别 11 个 HCM 与 AF 交集基因，富集分析与免疫浸润分析提示其参与免疫调节与炎症过程；确定 NPPA、ACE、POMC 三个枢纽基因作为两种疾病的關鍵分子连接。GSE36961 数据集验证显示 NPPA 在 HCM 中上调，支持其作为核心连接基因；GSE115574 的具体验证结果摘要未报告。临床验证中，NT-proANP 血清水平在 HCM 组与 HCM 合并 AF 组均显著高于健康对照（P < 0.01），且 HCM 合并 AF 组显著高于单纯 HCM 组（P < 0.01）；效应量与 95% 置信区间摘要未报告。

结论与意义

研究提示 HCM 与 AF 存在共享遗传结构，NPPA 编码的 NT-proANP 升高与 HCM 患者合并 AF 密切相关，可作为该类人群 AF 风险分层的候选循环标志物，为理解 HCM 心房电重构与基质形成提供了线索。局限性包括：临床验证为探索性、样本量未报告且规模有限，属横断面设计、缺乏随访与心电/影像学表型关联，ACE 与 POMC 未获临床样本验证，NT-proANP 与心房电重构的因果关系尚需前瞻性队列与机制研究确认。

10

Calmodulin mutation N54I causes autosomal dominant CPVT in mice.

bioRxiv·2026-09-24·PMID 42779895
遗传与基础 Journal Article Preprint

Daniel J Blackwell、Nieves Gomez-Hurtado、Kyungsoo Kim 等 9 位作者

英文摘要

Catecholaminergic polymorphic ventricular tachycardia (CPVT) is an inherited arrhythmia syndrome characterized by stress- or catecholamine-induced ventricular arrhythmias in the absence of overt structural heart disease. Mutations in RYR2 and CASQ2 account for most genetically defined cases, although pathogenic variants in the three genes encoding calmodulin (CALM1-3) have also been linked to CPVT. Because all three CALM genes encode an identical calmodulin protein, pathogenic calmodulin variants are expected to be expressed in only a small fraction of total cellular calmodulin, raising the question of whether this limited abundance is sufficient to produce an arrhythmogenic phenotype in vivo. We generated a heterozygous mouse model carrying the human disease-associated N54I-equivalent mutation, N54I, in Calm1. Mutant calmodulin accounted for 13.7% of total cardiac calmodulin, consistent with expression from one of six Calm alleles. Under basal conditions, N54I/+ mice exhibited normal growth, survival, cardiac morphology, and surface electrocardiogram parameters. However, cardiomyocytes isolated from N54I/+ mice had increased rates of RyR2-mediated spontaneous calcium release. Following catecholaminergic challenge with isoproterenol and caffeine, N54I/+ mice exhibited significantly more premature ventricular contractions and arrhythmias than wild-type littermates. Exercise challenge in conscious mice similarly provoked ventricular ectopy and ventricular tachycardia. In addition, N54I/+ mice exhibited abnormalities of atrial and sinoatrial electrical activity, including premature atrial contractions, ectopic P waves, atrioventricular conduction slowing, and beat-to-beat variability. These findings demonstrate that expression of the N54I calmodulin variant from a single Calm1 allele is sufficient to produce a CPVT phenotype in vivo. This model provides experimental evidence linking a human disease-associated calmodulin variant to arrhythmogenesis and demonstrates the functional dominance of mutant calmodulin in the heart.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

杂合敲入人源 Calm1 N54I 的小鼠中，突变钙调蛋白仅占心脏总量的 13.7%，却足以在儿茶酚胺与运动负荷下诱发室性心律失常，即复制出 CPVT 表型。

研究动机

儿茶酚胺敏感性多形性室性心动过速（CPVT）是应激或儿茶酚胺诱发的遗传性心律失常综合征，患者无明显的结构性心脏病；已明确的致病基因主要是 RYR2 与 CASQ2。CALM1-3 编码的钙调蛋白变异也被发现与 CPVT 相关，但这三个基因编码同一种钙调蛋白蛋白，因此致病变异理论上只占总细胞钙调蛋白的一小部分。既往证据的缺口在于：这一极低丰度是否足以在体内产生致心律失常表型，尚缺乏直接的体内证据。

方法

研究类型：遗传学与基础机制研究，采用人源化杂合敲入小鼠模型。研究对象：携带与人疾病相关 N54I 等效突变的 Calm1 N54I/+ 小鼠，对照为野生型同窝小鼠；样本量摘要未报告。干预/暴露：Calm1 单等位基因敲入突变。检测与终点：测定突变钙调蛋白占总心脏钙调蛋白的比例；在基础状态下评估生长、生存、心脏形态与体表心电图；在分离心肌细胞中评估 RyR2 介导的自发钙释放；在异丙肾上腺素联合咖啡因的儿茶酚胺负荷及清醒小鼠运动负荷下，记录室性早搏、心律失常与室性心动过速；并评估心房及窦房电活动（房性早搏、异位 P 波、房室传导减慢、逐搏变异）。统计方法摘要未报告。

结果

突变钙调蛋白占总心脏钙调蛋白的 13.7%，与 6 个 Calm 等位基因中 1 个表达的水平一致。基础状态下，N54I/+ 小鼠生长、生存、心脏形态及体表心电图参数均正常；但分离心肌细胞的 RyR2 介导自发钙释放速率增加。在异丙肾上腺素联合咖啡因负荷后，N54I/+ 小鼠的室性早搏及心律失常显著多于野生型同窝小鼠；清醒状态下运动负荷同样诱发室性异位搏动与室性心动过速。此外还出现房性早搏、异位 P 波、房室传导减慢及逐搏变异等心房与窦房电活动异常。各终点具体效应量、95% 置信区间及 P 值摘要未报告。

结论与意义

单一 Calm1 等位基因表达 N54I 突变钙调蛋白即可在体内产生 CPVT 表型，提示突变钙调蛋白在心脏中呈功能显性；该模型首次将人类疾病相关钙调蛋白变异与在体致心律失常性直接联系起来，支持在 CPVT 家系中筛查 CALM1-3，并提示钙调蛋白相关 CPVT 可能呈常染色体显性遗传。局限性：证据来自小鼠模型，样本量与统计细节未报告，RyR2 自发钙释放增加与临床表型之间的定量关系及能否外推至人类仍需验证。

2

Adaptive feature fusion based semi-supervised federated learning system for ECG arrhythmia detection.

Front Artif Intell·2026-09-24·PMID 42780366
其他 Journal Article

Silla Ann Regi、Jagadeesan Srinivasan、Thanapal Pandi

英文摘要

Automatic cardiac arrhythmia detection using electrocardiogram (ECG) signals is essential for early diagnosis of cardiovascular conditions. Most of the previously existing systems for arrhythmia detection depend on fully supervised learning approaches. In addition to that, since the ECG patient data is centrally stored, it can raise concerns like privacy and security in healthcare environments. To address these issues, this work explores a semi-supervised federated learning framework that detects arrhythmia using ECG signals. The MIT-BIH Arrhythmia dataset is used from which the heartbeat segments are extracted and relevant features are obtained. The framework incorporates the use of multi-level feature extraction, adaptive feature fusion and pseudo-labeling to utilize both labeled and unlabeled data efficiently. In order to improve the performance of the model, a weighted federated aggregation approach is used where the contribution of each of the clients is considered during the global model updates. Implementation and evaluation of multiple machine learning and deep learning models including Random Forest, Support Vector Machine, K-Nearest Neighbor, AdaBoost, Gradient Boosting and Multi-Layer Perceptron are performed within the proposed framework. This proposed approach achieves an accuracy of 96.81% in semi-supervised federated settings and has an AUC value of 0.9957 that shows high classification reliability. The system helps improve the model learning with limited number of labeled data at the same time ensuring privacy preserving distributed training. The model maintains a competitive performance under federated settings as compared to the centralized training. Among the evaluated models, ensemble and neural network based approaches show superior performance. The framework demonstrates a stable performance across federated training rounds. Overall, the results obtained show the effectiveness of the proposed framework for arrhythmia detection in a simulated federated setting.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

在 MIT-BIH 心律失常心拍数据上，半监督联邦学习框架结合自适应特征融合与伪标签，实现 96.81% 准确率和 0.9957 AUC，隐私保护下性能接近集中式训练。

研究动机

自动心电图的房性/室性心律失常检测对心血管疾病早期诊断至关重要。既往系统多依赖全监督学习，且心电数据集中存储带来隐私与安全问题；同时标注数据有限，需探索能利用未标注数据的隐私保护分布式训练方法。

方法

方法学研究与仿真实验。使用 MIT-BIH Arrhythmia 数据集，提取心拍片段及特征；框架包含多级特征提取、自适应特征融合、伪标签半监督学习与加权联邦聚合，并比较随机森林、支持向量机、K 近邻、AdaBoost、梯度提升和多层感知机等模型。主要观察分类准确率与 AUC，并评估联邦轮次稳定性；摘要未报告样本量、置信区间及 P 值。

结果

在半监督联邦设置下，所提框架准确率为 96.81%，AUC 为 0.9957；集成学习与神经网络类模型表现较优，框架在联邦训练轮次间保持稳定，且性能与集中式训练具有竞争力。摘要未报告 95%CI 和 P 值。

结论与意义

结果提示该框架在仿真联邦环境下可有效检测心律失常，并在有限标注数据下兼顾隐私保护与分布式训练。局限性包括仅使用单一公开数据集、为仿真联邦设置、缺乏外部验证与临床部署及前瞻性评估，尚需进一步验证其泛化性和实际临床价值。

6

Double transition in ventricular preexcitation.

J Electrocardiol·2026-09-24·PMID 42784991
其他 Journal Article

Laszlo Littmann、J Warren Holshouser

英文摘要

In the Wolff-Parkinson-White syndrome, numerous electrocardiographic (ECG) algorithms have been developed for the prediction of accessory pathway (AP) location. In addition to such algorithms, recognizing simple, unique and eye-catching ECG patterns that are specific for particular AP positions can provide further visual aid in quickly predicting the likely effective ablation site. Recently, such a distinctive ECG configuration has been introduced that showed high specificity for right posteroseptal (right paraseptal) APs. The "double transition" sign was characterized by showing more positive QRS complexes in lead V2 than in either of its neighboring leads V1 and V3. In some cases, the entire QRS complex was downgoing in V1 and V3, but upgoing in V2. In the double transition study, no explanation was provided as to the cause of this curious phenomenon, and why it had high specificity but low sensitivity for right posteroseptal APs. In this report we present our experience with the double transition sign, expand on its electrocardiographic spectrum, and offer a simple and rational explanation for the probable cause of this seemingly strange and perplexing phenomenon.

AI 中文解读

一句话结论

本文基于临床经验扩展WPW综合征“双移行”征的心电图谱，并为该征对右后间隔旁路高特异、低敏感的机制提供合理解释。

研究动机

WPW综合征已发展出多种预测旁路定位的心电图算法，但临床仍需简单、醒目、可直接提示可能有效消融部位的心电图模式。近期提出的“双移行”征对右后间隔（右间隔旁）旁路特异性高但敏感性低，原始研究未解释其成因。

方法

本文为经验性报告，摘要未报告研究对象、样本量、纳入标准及统计方法；作者展示双移行征的病例经验，扩展其心电图表现谱，并对其可能的电生理机制作解释。

结果

摘要未报告定量结果、效应量、95%CI或P值。作者描述双移行征表现为V2导联QRS较V1、V3更正向，部分病例V1、V3导联QRS主波向下而V2向上；并称该征对右后间隔旁路特异性高、敏感性低，本文补充其心电图谱并提出机制解释。

结论与意义

双移行征可作为快速预判右后间隔旁路的视觉辅助，有助于规划消融靶点；但其敏感性低、依赖病例经验，且摘要未报告样本量与统计验证，临床推广价值仍需进一步研究。
